# Simulator — web lane implementation plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the simulator's whole browser surface — `/sim`, `/sim/<sim_id>`, `/sim/specs`, the WebAssembly worker pool that runs the engine for free on the player's own machine, the one character model shared with the planner, and the ambient surfaces (live DPS in the planner, the execution column on every ranking) — so that a player pastes an addon string and sees, inside a second, what their character does and why.

**Architecture:** One Svelte island (`sim-island`, a standalone Vite entry like `planner-island`) mounted on three static Astro shells. The engine is `sim.wasm`, loaded after first paint into a pool of `min(8, hardwareConcurrency)` workers, each holding one instance; iterations are split with the engine's `raidSimRequestSplit`, run with `raidSimAsync`, and the final result combined with `raidSimResultCombination`, while a running DPS estimate is pooled numerically on the main thread from each shard's progress so the number moves without a wasm round-trip. The engine's result becomes a `logs/engine/summary.Summary`, so results are rendered by the report components the logs lane already shipped — no second renderer, and compare mode is a two-column layout over the same components.

**Tech Stack:** Astro 7 (static, `build.format: 'file'`), Svelte 5 runes, Tailwind 4 with `web/src/styles/tokens.css`, TypeScript 6, Vitest 5, Playwright 1.63, MSW 2, Cloudflare Workers (`web/src/worker.ts`), Node 22.12.0.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-simulator-design.md` (sections 4.1–4.7 and 5.1 are this lane). Binding contract: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-simulator-interfaces.md` (the "Web" section is this lane; the rest constrains it). Supporting: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-phase-2-addon-design.md` (the FS1 export string), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-phase-3-interfaces.md` (report data, rankings rows, the Worker's shell routes), `design/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`.

---

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements implicitly include this section.

**Toolchain**

- Node 22.12.0. Every shell that runs npm starts with `export PATH=/Users/jh/.nvm/versions/node/v22.12.0/bin:$PATH`. All npm commands run from `web/`.
- Playwright needs `ASTRO_PREVIEW_BACKGROUND=1`; it is already in `web/playwright.config.ts`'s `webServer.env` and must stay.
- `FOREVER_DATA=fixture` for every test run. `npm run pretest` already sets it; a bare `npx vitest`/`npx playwright` does not, so prefix it.
- Lighthouse (`npm run lhci`) runs **once**, in Task 22. No other task runs it.
- Tests are run for the files the task touches, not the whole suite: `npx vitest run src/lib/sim/run` rather than `npm test`. The full suite runs in Task 22 and in CI.
- `astro check` type-checks `.ts` **and** `.svelte`, and CI runs it. Three things it catches that a passing test does not: MSW 2's `HttpResponse` is generic and must not be used as a bare annotation (return `Response`); `Ruleset` in `web/src/lib/characters.ts` is the closed union `normal | pvp | rp | hardcore`, so a fixture character key never says `forever`; and `RosterRow.class` is optional, so anything passing it to a `string` parameter needs `?? ''`.
- Vitest's default environment in this repository is Node. Any test file that touches `document`, `window`, `Worker` or a module that reads `document.cookie` (which `requestEnvelope` does on every non-GET) **must** start with the line `// @vitest-environment jsdom`, the way `src/lib/account/api.test.ts` and `src/report-island.test.ts` already do. Without it a POST fails as a network error with status 0 and the test's assertion about the status is silently wrong.
- The API is not running. Every route in the contract is served in tests by MSW handlers (Task 2) for unit tests and by `page.route` for Playwright.

**Branch and commits**

- Work only in the `sim-web` worktree at `/Users/jh/code/forever/.worktrees/sim-web`, on branch `sim-web`.
- Every commit ends with exactly one trailer line in the final `-m`:
  `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`
- Conventional-commit types: `feat`, `fix`, `refactor`, `docs`, `test`, `chore`, `perf`, `ci`.

**Contract values, verbatim**

- `ENGINE_VERSION` is the short commit sha of `wowsims-forever`, e.g. `7779ebb`. It appears in `web/public/_sim/<ENGINE_VERSION>/sim.wasm` and `sim.js`, and in every `SimEnvelope.engine_version`. A result produced by a different `ENGINE_VERSION` than the current one is still readable and is **labelled stale in the UI; it is never silently re-run.**
- `sim_id`: 12 lowercase base32 characters (`[a-z2-7]{12}`), the same alphabet as `report_id`. Route `/sim/<sim_id>`.
- `spec_slug`: `<class-slug>-<spec-slug>` in lower kebab, e.g. `warrior-fury`, `mage-frost`. The canonical list lives in `data/curated/specs.json` and is generated into `web/src/lib/sim/specs.ts`. **Nothing hardcodes a spec list elsewhere.**
- `character_key`: `<region>/<ruleset>/<name-slug>`.
- Iterations are `500`, `3000` or `10000` — no other value.
- `EncounterSpec` defaults: `duration_sec` 180, `variation` 0.2, `targets` 1, `execute_ratio` 0.25, `profile` `""`.
- JSON field names in `web/src/lib/sim/types.ts` are identical to the Go `json:` tags in `sim/api/envelope.go`. Renaming one here means renaming it there.
- `POST /v1/sims/run` answers 402 with code `premium_required` when the user is not premium.
- Compare mode reads the fight summary from the report's existing `data_base_url`. **No new API.**
- `web/src/lib/sim/worker.ts` owns the pool; `web/src/lib/sim/run.ts` owns request building and partial-result combination. **Neither imports Astro.**

**Design system** (`design/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`)

- No new hex literal in any component. Colours come from Tailwind utilities bound to `web/src/styles/tokens.css`: `bg-bg`, `bg-raised`, `bg-card-top`, `border-line`, `border-line-soft`, `border-line-warm`, `border-line-warm-strong`, `text`, `text-strong`, `text-muted`, `text-nav`, `text-gold`, `rounded-panel`, `rounded-control`, `rounded-pill`, `font-display`, `font-mono`. Class and rarity colours go through `classColorVar()` / `rarityClassFor()`, never a literal.
- No emoji anywhere in the UI. Icons are inline SVG on a 24px grid, 2px stroke, round caps and joins, `currentColor` or gold.
- Errors are plain text with `role="alert"` in `text-strong`, never a colour. `--color-ember` is the sky horizon only; the faction and class colours are never repurposed.
- Hit targets 44px minimum on phone (`min-h-11` on every button, select, tab and row control).
- Page gutter 18px phone / 48px desktop (`px-[18px] md:px-12`); content `max-w-[1344px]`; section gap 22px phone / 32px desktop (`gap-[22px] md:gap-8`). Every `<main>` on this lane uses the site's one class string:
  `class="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[1344px] flex-col gap-[22px] pt-4 pb-10 md:gap-8 md:px-12 md:pt-7"`.
- Numbers are `font-mono tabular`.
- Every user-visible string on this lane lives in `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts` and is imported by both the component and its test. No string literals in components, the way `web/src/lib/planner/rules.ts` holds `messages`.

**Budgets**

- Sim page first paint inside the site's 1.6 s mobile LCP budget. **The wasm loads after first paint** and never blocks it: no module in the shell's critical path may import `web/src/lib/sim/engine.ts`, and the pool is created on the first run, not on mount.
- `sim-island.js` ≤ 90 KB gzipped, enforced by `web/scripts/check-island-size.mjs`. The wasm is not counted; it is a separate immutable asset.
- Quick Sim, 3,000 iterations, 4 workers: first estimate within 1 s, final within 8 s.
- Planner live DPS: 500 iterations, under a second, with its error shown.

**Phone from the start.** Every component in this plan is written with its phone layout in the same step as its desktop layout, not retrofitted. Task 19 is the audit, not the implementation.

## Parallel groups

Task 1 and Task 2 are the base: both must land before anything else, in that order.

| Group | Tasks | Depends on | Independent of |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A — the engine in the browser** | 3, 4, 5 (ordered within the group) | 1, 2 | B, C, E |
| **B — the character model** | 6, 7 (ordered within the group) | 1, 2 | A, C, E |
| **C — the routes, the island and the Worker** | 8, 9 (ordered within the group) | 1, 2 | A, B, E |
| **E — the execution column** | 10 | 1, 2 | A, B, C, D, F |
| **D — the sim page** | 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18, 19 (strictly ordered) | A, B, C | E, F |
| **F — the planner surfaces** | 20, 21 (ordered within the group) | A, B | C, D, E |
| **Final** | 22 | everything | — |

Groups A, B, C and E may run at the same time in four worktrees or four subagents. D is one ordered group because every task in it composes the report components on top of the task before it; F is one ordered group for the same reason. Task 22 runs last, alone.

Everything downstream renders results with the report components and reads the spec list. Both have to exist before any group starts. `phase-3-web` is not merged to `main`; this lane is written against it and merges it first, so there is exactly one base.

**Files:**
- Modify: `/Users/jh/code/forever/.worktrees/sim-web` (merge `phase-3-web`)
- Create: `data/curated/specs.json`
- Create: `web/scripts/sync-specs.mjs`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/specs.ts` (generated, committed)

- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/version.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/sim/specs.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/package.json`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

  - `web/src/lib/sim/specs.ts`: `interface SpecRow { spec: string; class_slug: string; spec_slug: string; name: string; role: string; tree_index: number }`, `const SPECS: readonly SpecRow[]`, `function specRow(spec: string): SpecRow | null`, `function specLabel(spec: string): string`, `function classOfSpec(spec: string): string`.
  - Every later task imports `ENGINE_VERSION` and `SPECS` from here and never writes a sha or a spec name inline.

```
web/astro.config.mjs
web/src/layouts/Base.astro
```

`phase-3-web`'s last merge from `main` predates the simulator design commits, which is what puts the two branches in the same hunks. Both conflicts are unions, not choices: each side added something the other needs. If a third file conflicts, stop and report it — the branches have moved since this plan was written.

- [ ] **Step 2: Resolve `web/astro.config.mjs` — keep both the filter and the serializer**

`main` gave the sitemap a `serialize` that carries each page's `updated` date as `lastmod`; `phase-3-web` added `/reports` to the filter because the shell is plumbing, not a destination. Replace the whole conflicted `sitemap(...)` call with:

```js
    // /b/:id request, and /reports is the shell the Worker clones for every report id.
    // Both are plumbing rather than destinations, so they stay out of the sitemap; the
    // report pages people actually link to are /reports/<id>, which cannot be enumerated
    // at build time.
    sitemap({
      filter: (page) => !page.endsWith('/b-unavailable') && !page.endsWith('/reports'),
      // lastmod comes from each content file's `updated` field, so a page's sitemap entry
      // moves only when its facts do; pages without a content file carry no lastmod.
      serialize: (item) => {
        const lastmod = contentLastmod(item.url);
        return lastmod ? { ...item, lastmod } : item;
      },
    }),
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Resolve `web/src/layouts/Base.astro` — keep both props and both meta sets**

`main` added `jsonLd` and moved the site name and url into `lib/seo`; `phase-3-web` added `session` and the `data-og` selectors the Worker rewrites. Both are load-bearing. Replace the conflicted frontmatter block with:

```astro
  /** Extra JSON-LD nodes for this page; the homepage always carries the WebSite node. */
  jsonLd?: object[];
  /**
   * Renders the signed-in state in the header. Only the logs-product pages set it: a
   * content page that did would ship client JavaScript for a link, and the Lighthouse
   * budgets on those pages have no room for it.
   */
  session?: boolean;
}
const { title, description, path, jsonLd = [], session = false } = Astro.props;
const site = SITE_URL;
const fullTitle = title === SITE_NAME ? title : `${title} · ${SITE_NAME}`;
const ldNodes = path === '/' ? [websiteLd(), ...jsonLd] : jsonLd;
```

and replace the conflicted Open Graph block with the `data-og` versions plus the three tags `main` added:

```astro
    <meta property="og:title" content={fullTitle} data-og="og-title" />
    <meta property="og:description" content={description} data-og="og-description" />
    <meta property="og:url" content={`${site}${path}`} data-og="og-url" />
    <meta
      property="og:image"
      content={`${site}/og${path === '/' ? '/index' : path}.png`}
      data-og="og-image"
    />
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta property="og:site_name" content={SITE_NAME} />
    <meta property="og:locale" content="en_US" />
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the generator**

Create `web/scripts/sync-specs.mjs`:

```js
// web/scripts/sync-specs.mjs
// data/curated/specs.json -> web/src/lib/sim/specs.ts. The contract makes the JSON file the
// canonical spec list and forbids a second one anywhere else, so this emits a module rather
// than letting components import the JSON: the emitted file carries the types, the lookups
// and the failure message, and `git diff` shows when the data lane moves the list.
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import process from 'node:process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const webRoot = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const source = path.resolve(webRoot, '../data/curated/specs.json');
const target = path.join(webRoot, 'src/lib/sim/specs.ts');

let rows;
try {
  rows = JSON.parse(await readFile(source, 'utf8'));
} catch (cause) {
  console.error(`sync-specs: cannot read ${source}. The data lane owns this file.`);
  console.error(cause.message);
  process.exit(1);
}

const required = ['spec', 'class_slug', 'spec_slug', 'name', 'role', 'tree_index'];
for (const row of rows) {
  const missing = required.filter((key) => row[key] === undefined);
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    console.error(`sync-specs: ${JSON.stringify(row)} is missing ${missing.join(', ')}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

const body = `// web/src/lib/sim/specs.ts
// GENERATED by scripts/sync-specs.mjs from data/curated/specs.json. Do not edit by hand.
// The contract makes that file the canonical spec list; nothing else may hold one.

export interface SpecRow {
  /** spec_slug: "<class-slug>-<spec-slug>", e.g. "warrior-fury". */
  spec: string;
  class_slug: string;
  spec_slug: string;
  name: string;
  role: string;
  /** 0-based index of the spec's talent tree, left to right. */
  tree_index: number;
}

export const SPECS: readonly SpecRow[] = ${JSON.stringify(rows, null, 2)} as const;

const BY_SPEC = new Map(SPECS.map((row) => [row.spec, row]));

export function specRow(spec: string): SpecRow | null {
  return BY_SPEC.get(spec) ?? null;
}

/** The spec's display name, or the slug itself when the list does not know it. */
export function specLabel(spec: string): string {
  return BY_SPEC.get(spec)?.name ?? spec;
}

/** The class slug, for classColorVar and the planner. Empty for an unknown spec. */
export function classOfSpec(spec: string): string {
  return BY_SPEC.get(spec)?.class_slug ?? '';
}
`;

await writeFile(target, body, 'utf8');
console.log(`sync-specs: wrote ${rows.length} specs to ${path.relative(webRoot, target)}`);
```

- [ ] **Step 13: Write the module**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/version.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/version.ts
// One string identifies the engine build everywhere (simulator interface contract, "Engine
// version"): the short sha of wowsims-forever the artifacts were built from. It names the
// immutable asset directory, it goes into every SimRequest and SimResult, and a result that
// carries a different one is labelled stale and never silently re-run.
//
// It is a literal rather than an import from sim/enginever/version.go because the web build
// has no Go toolchain; `make engine-pin` writes both, and web.yml fails when they disagree.

export const ENGINE_VERSION = '7779ebb';

/** Where a version's browser artifacts live. Cached immutably, so the path carries the sha. */
export function engineAssetUrl(file: 'sim.wasm' | 'sim.js', version: string = ENGINE_VERSION): string {
  return `/_sim/${version}/${file}`;
}

/**
 * True when a stored result was produced by some other engine build. An empty version counts
 * as stale: a result with no provenance is exactly the one a player should be told about.
 */
export function isStale(resultVersion: string): boolean {
  return resultVersion !== ENGINE_VERSION;
}

/** How a version reads in the UI. */
export function engineLabel(version: string): string {
  return `Engine ${version}`;
}
```

## Task 2: The envelope types, the sims API client, and the MSW handlers

The contract's JSON shapes, one client over them, and a Mock Service Worker server that answers every route the contract defines. The API is not running, so this is what every other task tests against.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/types.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`

- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/api.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/package.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ENGINE_VERSION` from `web/src/lib/sim/version.ts` (Task 1); `requestEnvelope`, `AccountError` from `web/src/lib/account/api.ts`; `API_BASE_URL` from `web/src/lib/planner/config.ts`; `Summary` from `web/src/lib/report/types.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/types.ts`: `SimRequest`, `CharacterSource`, `SourceKind`, `EncounterSpec`, `SimResult`, `Estimate`, `SimListRow`, `SimListPage`, `SimProgress`, `SpecFidelity`, `SpecState`, `SimInput`, `DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER`, `ITERATIONS`.
  - `web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts`: `durationLabel(seconds: number): string`, `encounterLabel(encounter: EncounterSpec, buffed: boolean): string`. These live here, in the base task, rather than in `settings.ts`: the Worker's unfurl copy needs them (group C) and the settings bar needs them (group B), and the two groups run in parallel. They are pure formatting over `EncounterSpec`, which this task defines, so this is where they belong anyway.

  - `web/src/test-support/msw.ts`: `simHandlers`, `startMsw`, `FIXTURE_SIM_ID`, `fixtureResult`, `fixtureSpecs`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the contract's types**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/types.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/types.ts
// TypeScript mirrors of sim/api/envelope.go. Every key here is that file's `json:` tag;
// the contract (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-simulator-interfaces.md) makes the JSON
// names authoritative and identical in both, so anything renamed here is renamed there.
//
// `raw` is the engine's own RaidSimRequest protobuf, which neither lane interprets: it is
// built by the web from the character model and handed to the engine untouched. It travels
// as a base64 string in JSON, because that is what Go's encoding/json does with []byte.
import type { Summary } from '../report/types';

/** Where the character came from. */
export type SourceKind = 'armory' | 'addon' | 'build' | 'fight' | 'manual';

/** The only iteration counts the contract allows. */
export const ITERATIONS = { live: 500, normal: 3000, precise: 10_000 } as const;
export type IterationCount = (typeof ITERATIONS)[keyof typeof ITERATIONS];

export interface CharacterSource {
  kind: SourceKind;
  /** character_key | "" | build id | "<report_id>:<fight_index>". */
  ref: string;
  /** RFC3339. */
  captured_at: string;
}

export interface EncounterSpec {
  duration_sec: number;
  variation: number;
  targets: number;
  execute_ratio: number;
  /** "" | "patchwerk" | "encounter:<encounter_id>". */
  profile: string;
}

export const DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER: EncounterSpec = {
  duration_sec: 180,
  variation: 0.2,
  targets: 1,
  execute_ratio: 0.25,
  profile: '',
};

export interface SimRequest {
  engine_version: string;
  /** spec_slug. */
  spec: string;
  source: CharacterSource;
  encounter: EncounterSpec;
  iterations: number;
  /** 0 means random; paired runs set it. */
  random_seed: number;
  /** base64 of the proto-encoded wowsims RaidSimRequest; omitted on a stored result. */
  raw?: string;
}

export interface Estimate {
  mean: number;
  stddev: number;
  /** Standard error of the mean. */
  error: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
}

export interface SimResult {
  sim_id?: string;
  engine_version: string;
  request: SimRequest;
  lane: 'browser' | 'server';
  dps: Estimate;
  iterations_run: number;
  /** Wall clock of the run. */
  duration_ms: number;
  summary: Summary;
  error?: string;
}

/** One row of GET /v1/sims?mine=1. */
export interface SimListRow {
  sim_id: string;
  spec: string;
  dps: number;
  engine_version: string;
  created_at: string;
  title: string;
}

export interface SimListPage {
  rows: SimListRow[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  per_page: number;
}

export interface SimProgress {
  state: 'queued' | 'running' | 'done' | 'error';
  iterations_done: number;
  dps?: number;
}

export type SpecState = 'validated' | 'in_progress' | 'unsupported';

export interface SpecFidelity {
  spec: string;
  state: SpecState;
  /** Median DPS gap as a fraction; null until the validation job has run. */
  median_gap: number | null;
  parses: number;
  worst_actions: { name: string; sim_casts: number; actual_casts: number }[];
  engine_version: string | null;
  updated_at: string;
}

/** GET /v1/characters/{character_key}/sim-input. */
export interface SimInput {
  spec: string;
  /** Slot name to item id, the planner's own Gear shape. */
  gear: Record<string, number>;
  /** Talent ids in the order the points were spent. */
  talents: number[];
  /** Spell ids of the buffs and consumables recorded for the character. */
  buffs: number[];
  captured_at: string;
  /**
   * Which source the API actually had. Today that is `"addon"` or `"fight"`, newest wins:
   * nothing in the repo stores an Armory refresh yet, so `"armory"` appears here only when
   * Blizzard's profile API for Forever exists. The shape does not change when it does.
   */
  source: SourceKind;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the encounter vocabulary**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts
// How an EncounterSpec reads. Two functions, in the base task rather than beside the
// settings bar, because two groups of this plan need them at the same time: the Worker's
// unfurl copy (the routes group) and the settings bar (the character-model group). Pure
// formatting over the shape types.ts defines, so this is where they belong regardless.
import type { EncounterSpec } from './types';

/** Seconds as a clock: 180 is "3:00", not "180s" and not "3 minutes". */
export function durationLabel(seconds: number): string {
  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  return `${minutes}:${String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * The settings clause a saved sim's title and a shared sim's unfurl both carry:
 * "raid-buffed, 3:00, single target".
 */
export function encounterLabel(encounter: EncounterSpec, buffed: boolean): string {
  const targets = encounter.targets === 1 ? 'single target' : `${encounter.targets} targets`;
  return `${buffed ? 'raid-buffed' : 'solo'}, ${durationLabel(encounter.duration_sec)}, ${targets}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Start the copy module**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts
// Every user-visible string on the simulator lane. Components import from here and tests
// assert against the same constants, the way web/src/lib/planner/rules.ts holds `messages`:
// a copy change is then one diff in one file, and no test asserts on a literal that a
// component could quietly stop rendering.
//
// Voice, per design/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md: reference, not pitch. State the number and stop.

export const simCopy = {
  /** Network and API failures. */
  saveFailed: 'The sim could not be saved; try again.',
  loadFailed: 'That sim did not load.',
  notFound: 'No sim with that id.',
  premiumRequired: 'Running on our servers is a premium feature. The browser lane is free and unlimited.',
  specsFailed: 'Spec support could not be read.',
  characterFailed: 'That character could not be read.',
  // Used by the sim page (group D) and the planner's live estimate (group F), which run in
  // parallel. Both need the same sentence, so it starts here rather than in either.
  specUnsupportedLead: 'The simulator does not model this spec yet.',
} as const;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing test for the client**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/api.test.ts`. The jsdom pragma is load-bearing: `requestEnvelope` reads `document.cookie` for the CSRF header on every non-GET, and under the default Node environment that throws before the fetch, which surfaces as a status-0 network failure rather than the 201 the handler sends.

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { CharacterPath } from '../characters';
import { FIXTURE_SIM_ID, fixtureResult, server } from '../../test-support/msw';
import {
  PREMIUM_REQUIRED_STATUS,
  SimApiError,
  dispatchServerSim,
  fetchSim,
  fetchSimInput,
  fetchSimProgress,
  fetchSpecs,
  listMySims,
  saveSim,
} from './api';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from './version';

const API = 'https://api.test';

beforeAll(() => server.listen({ onUnhandledRequest: 'error' }));
afterEach(() => server.resetHandlers());
afterAll(() => server.close());

describe('fetchSim', () => {
  it('reads a stored result', async () => {
    const result = await fetchSim(FIXTURE_SIM_ID, API);
    expect(result.sim_id).toBe(FIXTURE_SIM_ID);
    expect(result.dps.mean).toBeCloseTo(fixtureResult.dps.mean, 5);
    expect(result.summary.damage_done[0].abilities[0].name).toBe('Bloodthirst');
  });

  it('says so when there is no such sim', async () => {
    await expect(fetchSim('zzzzzzzzzzzz', API)).rejects.toThrow(simCopy.notFound);
  });
});

describe('saveSim', () => {
  it('posts a browser result without its raw request and returns the new id', async () => {
    const saved = await saveSim({ ...fixtureResult, sim_id: undefined }, API);
    expect(saved).toMatch(/^[a-z2-7]{12}$/);
  });

  it('refuses to send `raw`, which the contract omits on a stored result', async () => {
    const sent = await saveSim(
      { ...fixtureResult, sim_id: undefined, request: { ...fixtureResult.request, raw: 'AAAA' } },
      API,
    );
    expect(sent).toMatch(/^[a-z2-7]{12}$/);
    expect(server.lastBody()?.request.raw).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('listMySims', () => {
  it('reads the signed-in player’s history', async () => {
    const page = await listMySims(1, API);
    expect(page.per_page).toBe(100);
    expect(page.rows[0].spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
  });
});

describe('dispatchServerSim', () => {
  it('returns the id the premium lane will write to', async () => {
    const id = await dispatchServerSim(fixtureResult.request, API);
    expect(id).toMatch(/^[a-z2-7]{12}$/);
  });

  it('turns a 402 into the premium message, not a generic failure', async () => {
    server.setPremium(false);
    const error = await dispatchServerSim(fixtureResult.request, API).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(SimApiError);
    expect((error as SimApiError).status).toBe(PREMIUM_REQUIRED_STATUS);
    expect((error as SimApiError).message).toBe(simCopy.premiumRequired);
  });
});

describe('fetchSimProgress', () => {
  it('reads the running job’s state', async () => {
    const progress = await fetchSimProgress(FIXTURE_SIM_ID, API);
    expect(progress.state).toBe('running');
    expect(progress.iterations_done).toBe(4200);
  });
});

describe('fetchSpecs', () => {
  it('reads one row per spec with its fidelity', async () => {
    const specs = await fetchSpecs(API);
    expect(specs.find((row) => row.spec === 'warrior-fury')?.state).toBe('validated');
    expect(specs.find((row) => row.spec === 'rogue-combat')?.median_gap).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('fetchSimInput', () => {

  const path: CharacterPath = { region: 'us', ruleset: 'normal', slug: 'thrallgar' };

  it('reads the newest character model the API holds', async () => {

    expect(input.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');

    expect(server.lastUrl()).not.toContain('%2F');

});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the MSW server**

Create `web/src/test-support/msw.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/test-support/msw.ts
// The API does not run in this repository's tests, so every route the simulator contract
// defines is answered here instead. One server, imported by every sim test, so a route that
// changes shape changes in one place -- and `onUnhandledRequest: 'error'` means a test that
// calls a route nobody wrote a handler for fails loudly rather than hanging on a real fetch.
//
// `lastBody()` and `lastUrl()` exist because the assertions that matter are often about what
// the client *sent*: that `raw` was stripped, that a character key was path-escaped, that the
// engine version travelled. Recording them here keeps every test from re-wrapping fetch.
import { HttpResponse, http } from 'msw';
import { setupServer } from 'msw/node';
import fixtureSpecsJson from '../fixtures/sim/specs.json';
import fixtureResultJson from '../fixtures/sim/result.json';
import type { SimResult, SpecFidelity } from '../lib/sim/types';

export const FIXTURE_SIM_ID = 'simfixture01';
export const NEW_SIM_ID = 'simnew234567';

export const fixtureResult = fixtureResultJson as unknown as SimResult;
export const fixtureSpecs = fixtureSpecsJson as unknown as SpecFidelity[];

const API = 'https://api.test';

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
let lastBody: any = null;
let lastUrl = '';
let premium = true;

// Response, not HttpResponse: MSW 2's HttpResponse is generic in its body type, and an
// unparameterised annotation is a ts(2314). It extends Response, so this is the honest type.
function envelope(data: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return HttpResponse.json({ ok: status < 400, data, error: null, request_id: 'req-test' }, { status });
}

function failure(message: string, status: number): Response {
  return HttpResponse.json({ ok: false, data: null, error: { message }, request_id: 'req-test' }, { status });
}

async function record(request: Request): Promise<void> {
  lastUrl = request.url;
  lastBody = null;
  if (request.method === 'GET') return;
  try {
    lastBody = await request.clone().json();
  } catch {
    lastBody = null;
  }
}

export const simHandlers = [
  http.post(`${API}/v1/sims`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    return envelope({ sim_id: NEW_SIM_ID }, 201);
  }),

  http.get(`${API}/v1/sims/:id/progress`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    return envelope({ state: 'running', iterations_done: 4200, dps: 1428.9 });
  }),

  http.get(`${API}/v1/sims/:id`, async ({ request, params }) => {
    await record(request);
    if (params.id !== FIXTURE_SIM_ID) return failure('no such sim', 404);
    return envelope(fixtureResult);
  }),

  http.get(`${API}/v1/sims`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    return envelope({
      rows: [
        {
          sim_id: FIXTURE_SIM_ID,
          spec: 'warrior-fury',
          dps: 1432.4,
          engine_version: '7779ebb',
          created_at: '2026-09-14T10:02:00Z',
          title: 'Raid-buffed, 3 minutes',
        },
      ],
      total: 1,
      page: 1,
      per_page: 100,
    });
  }),

  http.post(`${API}/v1/sims/run`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    if (!premium) return failure('premium required', 402);
    return envelope({ sim_id: NEW_SIM_ID }, 202);
  }),

  http.get(`${API}/v1/specs`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    return envelope({ specs: fixtureSpecs });
  }),

  // Three path segments, not one key: the contract spells this route the way the existing
  // character route is spelled. `source` is "addon" or "fight" -- Armory is not a source yet.
  http.get(`${API}/v1/characters/:region/:ruleset/:name/sim-input`, async ({ request }) => {
    await record(request);
    return envelope({
      spec: 'warrior-fury',
      gear: { head: 12640, chest: 11726, main_hand: 12784 },
      talents: [2001, 2001, 2001, 2001, 2001, 2002, 2002],
      buffs: [16609, 11405],
      captured_at: '2026-09-14T09:40:00Z',
      source: 'addon',
    });
  }),
];

const base = setupServer(...simHandlers);

/**
 * The bare MSW server plus the three things sim tests need from it. `setPremium(false)` is
 * reset by `resetHandlers()` so a test that flips it cannot leak into the next one.
 */
export const server = {
  listen: (options?: Parameters<typeof base.listen>[0]) => base.listen(options),
  close: () => base.close(),
  resetHandlers: (...handlers: Parameters<typeof base.resetHandlers>) => {
    premium = true;
    lastBody = null;
    lastUrl = '';
    base.resetHandlers(...handlers);
  },
  use: (...handlers: Parameters<typeof base.use>) => base.use(...handlers),
  setPremium: (value: boolean) => {
    premium = value;
  },
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
  lastBody: (): any => lastBody,
  lastUrl: (): string => lastUrl,
};
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write the client**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/api.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/api.ts
// Every call this lane makes to our API. It composes requestEnvelope from the account module
// rather than re-implementing the envelope, the session cookie and the CSRF header: that
// function is the one place a browser request to our API is built, and a second copy here
// would be a second place for the CSRF header to go wrong.
//
// Two rules the contract sets and this file enforces:
//   * a stored SimResult never carries `raw` -- the engine's request can be megabytes and
//     the API has no use for it, so saveSim strips it rather than trusting the caller;
//   * POST /v1/sims/run answers 402 when the account is not premium. The envelope's error
//     has no machine-readable code in the phase-3 TypeScript mirror, so premium is keyed off
//     the status, and the copy is ours rather than the API's.
import { AccountError, requestEnvelope } from '../account/api';
import type { CharacterPath } from '../characters';
import { API_BASE_URL } from '../planner/config';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import type { SimInput, SimListPage, SimProgress, SimRequest, SimResult, SpecFidelity } from './types';

export const PREMIUM_REQUIRED_STATUS = 402;

export class SimApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly status: number,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'SimApiError';
  }
}

/** Every failure this module raises, with the copy the page shows, keyed off the status. */
function asSimError(error: unknown, fallback: string): SimApiError {
  const status = error instanceof AccountError ? error.status : 0;
  if (status === PREMIUM_REQUIRED_STATUS) return new SimApiError(simCopy.premiumRequired, status);
  if (status === 404) return new SimApiError(simCopy.notFound, status);
  return new SimApiError(fallback, status);
}

async function call<T>(
  path: string,
  apiBase: string,
  fallback: string,
  init: { method?: string; body?: unknown; credentials?: RequestCredentials } = {},
): Promise<T> {
  let data: T | null;
  try {
    ({ data } = await requestEnvelope<T>(path, apiBase, { ...init, failureMessage: fallback }));
  } catch (error) {
    throw asSimError(error, fallback);
  }
  if (data === null) throw new SimApiError(fallback, 0);
  return data;
}

/** Saves a browser-run result. `raw` is dropped here, not by the caller. */
export async function saveSim(result: SimResult, apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<string> {
  const { raw: _raw, ...request } = result.request;
  const body: SimResult = { ...result, lane: 'browser', request };
  delete body.sim_id;
  const data = await call<{ sim_id: string }>('/v1/sims', apiBase, simCopy.saveFailed, {
    method: 'POST',
    body,
  });
  return data.sim_id;
}

export function fetchSim(simId: string, apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<SimResult> {
  return call<SimResult>(`/v1/sims/${simId}`, apiBase, simCopy.loadFailed, { credentials: 'omit' });
}

export function listMySims(page: number = 1, apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<SimListPage> {
  return call<SimListPage>(`/v1/sims?mine=1&page=${page}`, apiBase, simCopy.loadFailed);
}

/** The premium lane. Throws SimApiError with status 402 when the account is not premium. */
export async function dispatchServerSim(
  request: SimRequest,
  apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL,
): Promise<string> {
  const data = await call<{ sim_id: string }>('/v1/sims/run', apiBase, simCopy.saveFailed, {
    method: 'POST',
    body: request,
  });
  return data.sim_id;
}

export function fetchSimProgress(simId: string, apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<SimProgress> {
  return call<SimProgress>(`/v1/sims/${simId}/progress`, apiBase, simCopy.loadFailed, {
    credentials: 'omit',
  });
}

export async function fetchSpecs(apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<SpecFidelity[]> {
  const data = await call<{ specs: SpecFidelity[] }>('/v1/specs', apiBase, simCopy.specsFailed, {
    credentials: 'omit',
  });
  return data.specs;
}

/**
 * The character model the API holds, from the newest source it has. The route is three path
 * segments -- `/v1/characters/{region}/{ruleset}/{name}/sim-input` -- exactly as the existing
 * character route is spelled, never one percent-encoded `{character_key}` segment. Each
 * segment is encoded on its own so a name with a character needing escaping still resolves.
 */
export function fetchSimInput(path: CharacterPath, apiBase: string = API_BASE_URL): Promise<SimInput> {
  const segments = [path.region, path.ruleset, path.slug].map(encodeURIComponent).join('/');
  return call<SimInput>(`/v1/characters/${segments}/sim-input`, apiBase, simCopy.characterFailed);
}
```

# Group A — the engine in the browser

Tasks 3, 4 and 5, in order. Independent of groups B, C and E: nothing here imports a component, a page or the character model. It depends only on Task 1 and Task 2.

## Task 3: The fake engine, the loader, and the request bytes

`sim.wasm` does not exist yet. The contract's stub is `web/src/fixtures/sim/engine-fake.ts`: a module with the same five entrypoints that returns a checked-in result after a delay. This task builds the `EngineModule` interface both implementations satisfy, the fake, the real loader beside it as a one-line swap, and the function that turns a character into the bytes the engine is handed.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/engine.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/raw.ts`
- Create: `web/src/fixtures/sim/wire.ts`
- Create: `web/src/fixtures/sim/engine-fake.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/raw.test.ts`, `web/src/fixtures/sim/engine-fake.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ENGINE_VERSION`, `engineAssetUrl` (Task 1); `EncounterSpec`, `Estimate`, `SimResult` (Task 2); `fixtureResult` via `web/src/fixtures/sim/result.json`.
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/engine.ts`: `interface EngineModule { raidSimRequestSplit(request: Uint8Array, splits: number): Uint8Array[]; raidSimAsync(request: Uint8Array, onProgress: (bytes: Uint8Array) => void, runId: string): Promise<Uint8Array>; raidSimResultCombination(results: Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array; computeStats(request: Uint8Array): Uint8Array; abortById(runId: string): void }`, `type EngineMode = 'fake' | 'wasm'`, `const ENGINE_MODE: EngineMode`, `function loadEngine(version?: string): Promise<EngineModule>`.
  - `web/src/lib/sim/raw.ts`: `interface RawInput`, `function buildRaidSimRequest(input: RawInput): Uint8Array`, `function bytesToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string`, `function base64ToBytes(text: string): Uint8Array`.
  - `web/src/fixtures/sim/wire.ts`: `interface WireRequest`, `interface WireProgress`, `interface WireResult`, `encodeWireRequest`, `decodeWireRequest`, `encodeWireProgress`, `decodeWireProgress`, `encodeWireResult`, `decodeWireResult`.
  - Task 4 constructs an `EngineModule` per worker; Task 5 calls `buildRaidSimRequest` and reads `decodeWireProgress`/`decodeWireResult` through the pool.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { decodeWireRequest } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import { DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER } from './types';
import { base64ToBytes, buildRaidSimRequest, bytesToBase64, type RawInput } from './raw';

const input: RawInput = {
  spec: 'warrior-fury',
  race: 'orc',
  level: 60,
  gear: { head: 12640, main_hand: 12784 },
  talents: [2001, 2001, 2002],
  buffs: [16609],
  consumables: [11405],
  encounter: DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER,
  iterations: 3000,
  randomSeed: 0,
};

describe('bytesToBase64 and base64ToBytes', () => {
  it('round-trip arbitrary bytes, including the ones btoa mangles', () => {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array([0, 1, 127, 128, 200, 255, 254, 13, 10]);
    expect(base64ToBytes(bytesToBase64(bytes))).toEqual(bytes);
  });

  it('round-trip an empty array', () => {
    expect(base64ToBytes(bytesToBase64(new Uint8Array()))).toEqual(new Uint8Array());
  });

  it('round-trip a payload longer than one chunk, so the loop is exercised', () => {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(70_000).map((_, i) => i % 256);
    expect(bytesToBase64(bytes).length).toBeGreaterThan(90_000);
    expect(base64ToBytes(bytesToBase64(bytes))).toEqual(bytes);
  });
});

describe('buildRaidSimRequest', () => {
  it('carries every field the engine needs to run this character', () => {
    const decoded = decodeWireRequest(buildRaidSimRequest(input));
    expect(decoded).toEqual({
      spec: 'warrior-fury',
      race: 'orc',
      level: 60,
      gear: { head: 12640, main_hand: 12784 },
      talents: [2001, 2001, 2002],
      buffs: [16609],
      consumables: [11405],
      encounter: DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER,
      iterations: 3000,
      randomSeed: 0,
    });
  });

  it('is byte-identical for the same input, so a paired run really is paired', () => {
    const a = buildRaidSimRequest({ ...input, randomSeed: 99 });
    const b = buildRaidSimRequest({ ...input, randomSeed: 99 });
    expect(a).toEqual(b);
  });

  it('differs when one slot differs, so a single-swap sim is a different request', () => {
    const swapped = buildRaidSimRequest({ ...input, gear: { ...input.gear, head: 12641 } });
    expect(swapped).not.toEqual(buildRaidSimRequest(input));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the wire format the fake speaks**

Create `web/src/fixtures/sim/wire.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/fixtures/sim/wire.ts
// The byte format the fake engine speaks. It exists because the real engine's format is a
// protobuf whose generated bindings ship with sim.wasm, which does not exist yet: every
// byte array this lane passes around is opaque to it, so a JSON encoding under the same
// Uint8Array signatures is a faithful stand-in and lets the pool, the combination and the
// live estimate be written and tested for real.
//
// Nothing outside src/fixtures/sim/ and src/lib/sim/raw.ts's fake branch may import this.
// When the engine lands, raw.ts's encode switches to the generated bindings and the fake
// stays exactly as it is, still driving the tests.
import type { EncounterSpec } from '../../lib/sim/types';
import type { Summary } from '../../lib/report/types';

export interface WireRequest {
  spec: string;
  race: string;
  level: number;
  gear: Record<string, number>;
  talents: number[];
  buffs: number[];
  consumables: number[];
  encounter: EncounterSpec;
  iterations: number;
  randomSeed: number;
}

export interface WireProgress {
  iterationsDone: number;
  mean: number;
  stddev: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
}

export interface WireResult {
  iterationsRun: number;
  mean: number;
  stddev: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  summary: Summary;
}

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const decoder = new TextDecoder();

function encode(value: unknown): Uint8Array {
  return encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(value));
}

function decode<T>(bytes: Uint8Array): T {
  return JSON.parse(decoder.decode(bytes)) as T;
}

export const encodeWireRequest = (value: WireRequest): Uint8Array => encode(value);
export const decodeWireRequest = (bytes: Uint8Array): WireRequest => decode<WireRequest>(bytes);
export const encodeWireProgress = (value: WireProgress): Uint8Array => encode(value);
export const decodeWireProgress = (bytes: Uint8Array): WireProgress => decode<WireProgress>(bytes);
export const encodeWireResult = (value: WireResult): Uint8Array => encode(value);
export const decodeWireResult = (bytes: Uint8Array): WireResult => decode<WireResult>(bytes);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the request builder**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/raw.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/raw.ts
// SimRequest.raw is "the engine's own RaidSimRequest protobuf, built by the web from the
// character model... Both lanes pass it to the engine untouched: the engine never sees our
// envelope" (simulator interface contract). So this is the one place the character model
// becomes engine bytes, and the one function that changes when sim.wasm lands: encode()
// below switches from the fake's wire format to the engine's generated bindings and nothing
// else in this lane moves.
//
// Keys are sorted on the way out. A paired run -- a single-gear-swap delta, a talent
// variant -- is only paired if the same character produces byte-identical requests, and
// JSON.stringify follows insertion order, which a Svelte $state object does not promise.
import { encodeWireRequest, type WireRequest } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import { ENGINE_MODE } from './engine';
import type { EncounterSpec } from './types';

export interface RawInput {
  /** spec_slug. */
  spec: string;
  race: string;
  level: number;
  /** Planner slot name to item id. */
  gear: Record<string, number>;
  /** Talent ids, one entry per point, in the order they were spent. */
  talents: number[];
  /** Spell ids of the raid buffs in effect. */
  buffs: number[];
  /** Spell ids of the consumables in effect. */
  consumables: number[];
  encounter: EncounterSpec;
  iterations: number;
  /** 0 means the engine picks; a paired run sets it. */
  randomSeed: number;
}

function sortedGear(gear: Record<string, number>): Record<string, number> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const slot of Object.keys(gear).sort()) out[slot] = gear[slot];
  return out;
}

function normalise(input: RawInput): WireRequest {
  return {
    spec: input.spec,
    race: input.race,
    level: input.level,
    gear: sortedGear(input.gear),
    talents: [...input.talents],
    buffs: [...input.buffs].sort((a, b) => a - b),
    consumables: [...input.consumables].sort((a, b) => a - b),
    encounter: { ...input.encounter },
    iterations: input.iterations,
    randomSeed: input.randomSeed,
  };
}

export function buildRaidSimRequest(input: RawInput): Uint8Array {
  const wire = normalise(input);
  if (ENGINE_MODE === 'fake') return encodeWireRequest(wire);
  // When sim.wasm lands this becomes the generated RaidSimRequest encoder. It is the only
  // line in this lane that has to change, which is why the normalisation above sits outside
  // the branch: both encodings get the same, already-canonical object.
  throw new Error('buildRaidSimRequest: the wasm encoder is not wired up yet');
}

// btoa works on a binary string, one character per byte, so bytes go through String
// .fromCharCode. It is applied in chunks because spreading a 4 MB array into apply() blows
// the argument limit; 0x8000 is the usual safe stride.
const CHUNK = 0x8000;

export function bytesToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK));
  }
  return btoa(binary);
}

export function base64ToBytes(text: string): Uint8Array {
  const binary = atob(text);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i += 1) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
  return bytes;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the engine interface and the loader**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/engine.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/engine.ts
// The five WebAssembly entrypoints the contract allows this lane to use -- raidSimAsync,
// raidSimRequestSplit, raidSimResultCombination, computeStats, abortById -- and the two
// implementations of them: the checked-in fake, and the real one loaded from
// /_sim/<ENGINE_VERSION>/. Everything else in the lane talks to this interface, so swapping
// the fake for the real engine is one environment variable.
//
// PUBLIC_SIM_ENGINE is read through import.meta.env so both the Astro build and the
// standalone island build inline it (vite.island.config.ts allow-lists the PUBLIC_ prefix).
// It defaults to 'fake' because sim.wasm does not exist yet; web.yml sets it to 'wasm' once
// the engine lane publishes an artifact for the pinned sha.
import { ENGINE_VERSION, engineAssetUrl } from './version';

export type EngineMode = 'fake' | 'wasm';

export const ENGINE_MODE: EngineMode =
  (import.meta.env.PUBLIC_SIM_ENGINE as EngineMode | undefined) ?? 'fake';

export interface EngineModule {
  /** Splits one request's iterations into `splits` requests that sum to the original. */
  raidSimRequestSplit(request: Uint8Array, splits: number): Uint8Array[];
  /**
   * Runs one request, calling `onProgress` with an encoded progress message as iterations
   * land, and resolving with the encoded result. `runId` is what abortById cancels.
   */
  raidSimAsync(
    request: Uint8Array,
    onProgress: (bytes: Uint8Array) => void,
    runId: string,
  ): Promise<Uint8Array>;
  /** Folds shard results into one result with the combined distribution and metrics. */
  raidSimResultCombination(results: Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array;
  /** The character's stats after gear, talents and buffs, without running a fight. */
  computeStats(request: Uint8Array): Uint8Array;
  /** Cancels a run started with the same `runId`. A run that already finished is a no-op. */
  abortById(runId: string): void;
}

/** The glue sim.js defines. Declared, not imported: it attaches itself to the global. */
interface GoGlue {
  new (): { importObject: WebAssembly.Imports; run(instance: WebAssembly.Instance): Promise<void> };
}

/**
 * The real loader. It is deliberately the smallest thing that can work: fetch the glue,
 * instantiate the wasm against it, and hand back the globals the engine exported. Every
 * decision above it -- how many instances, how iterations are split, when to abort -- lives
 * in worker.ts and run.ts, so this file has nothing to unit-test and nothing to get wrong
 * beyond the URL, which version.test.ts already pins.
 */
async function loadWasmEngine(version: string): Promise<EngineModule> {
  const glueUrl = engineAssetUrl('sim.js', version);
  await import(/* @vite-ignore */ glueUrl);
  const Go = (globalThis as unknown as { Go?: GoGlue }).Go;
  if (Go === undefined) throw new Error(`sim.js at ${glueUrl} did not define Go`);
  const go = new Go();
  const { instance } = await WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming(
    fetch(engineAssetUrl('sim.wasm', version)),
    go.importObject,
  );
  void go.run(instance);
  const engine = globalThis as unknown as Partial<EngineModule>;
  if (typeof engine.raidSimAsync !== 'function') {
    throw new Error('sim.wasm did not export raidSimAsync');
  }
  return engine as EngineModule;
}

export async function loadEngine(version: string = ENGINE_VERSION): Promise<EngineModule> {
  if (ENGINE_MODE === 'fake') {
    const { createFakeEngine } = await import('../../fixtures/sim/engine-fake');
    return createFakeEngine();
  }
  return loadWasmEngine(version);
}
```

```ts
    const b = decodeWireResult(await engine.raidSimAsync(buildRaidSimRequest(input), noop, 'b'));
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the fake engine**

Create `web/src/fixtures/sim/engine-fake.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/fixtures/sim/engine-fake.ts
// The contract's stub for sim.wasm: "a worker that returns a checked-in RaidSimResult
// fixture after a delay". It is not a mock in the test sense -- the pool, the live estimate,
// the abort path and the results rendering all run against it in development and in every
// test, so it has to behave like the real thing in the ways the UI can observe:
//
//   * iterations split and sum exactly, including the remainder;
//   * progress arrives in ticks, so the DPS figure on screen really does move;
//   * the same seed gives the same numbers, so a paired run is paired;
//   * an abort rejects the pending run rather than resolving it;
//   * the combined result carries the fixture summary, so every report component mounts.
//
// The numbers are drawn from the fixture's own mean and stddev with a seeded generator, not
// randomly, so a screenshot test or a Playwright assertion on the DPS figure is stable.
import type { EngineModule } from '../../lib/sim/engine';
import type { Summary } from '../../lib/report/types';
import fixtureResultJson from './result.json';
import {
  decodeWireRequest,
  encodeWireProgress,
  encodeWireResult,
  decodeWireResult,
  encodeWireRequest,
  type WireResult,
} from './wire';

interface FixtureShape {
  dps: { mean: number; stddev: number; min: number; max: number };
  summary: Summary;
}
const fixture = fixtureResultJson as unknown as FixtureShape;

export interface FakeEngineOptions {
  /** Milliseconds between progress ticks. 0 runs the whole shard on the microtask queue. */
  tickMs?: number;
  /** How many progress ticks a shard reports. */
  ticks?: number;
}

/** mulberry32: 32 bits of state, uniform enough for a stand-in and identical everywhere. */
function seeded(seed: number): () => number {
  let state = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    state = (state + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = state;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

/** Box-Muller, so the shard distributions pool the way real ones do. */
function normal(random: () => number, mean: number, stddev: number): number {
  const u = Math.max(random(), Number.EPSILON);
  const v = random();
  return mean + stddev * Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v);
}

function statsOf(samples: number[]): Omit<WireResult, 'summary'> {
  const n = samples.length;
  const mean = samples.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  const variance = samples.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / n;
  return {
    iterationsRun: n,
    mean,
    stddev: Math.sqrt(variance),
    min: Math.min(...samples),
    max: Math.max(...samples),
  };
}

const delay = (ms: number): Promise<void> =>
  ms <= 0 ? Promise.resolve() : new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

export function createFakeEngine(options: FakeEngineOptions = {}): EngineModule {
  const tickMs = options.tickMs ?? 120;
  const ticks = options.ticks ?? 10;
  const aborted = new Set<string>();

  return {
    raidSimRequestSplit(request, splits) {
      const wire = decodeWireRequest(request);
      const used = Math.max(1, Math.min(splits, wire.iterations));
      const base = Math.floor(wire.iterations / used);
      const remainder = wire.iterations % used;
      return Array.from({ length: used }, (_, i) =>
        encodeWireRequest({
          ...wire,
          iterations: base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0),
          // Each shard gets its own stream, derived from the request's seed so the whole run
          // stays reproducible. Seed 0 means "the engine picks", so the shard index alone.
          randomSeed: wire.randomSeed === 0 ? i + 1 : wire.randomSeed * 1000 + i,
        }),
      );
    },

    async raidSimAsync(request, onProgress, runId) {
      aborted.delete(runId);
      const wire = decodeWireRequest(request);
      const random = seeded(wire.randomSeed * 7919 + wire.iterations);
      const samples: number[] = [];
      const perTick = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(wire.iterations / ticks));

      while (samples.length < wire.iterations) {
        await delay(tickMs);
        if (aborted.has(runId)) {
          aborted.delete(runId);
          throw new Error(`sim run ${runId} aborted`);
        }
        const upTo = Math.min(wire.iterations, samples.length + perTick);
        while (samples.length < upTo) {
          samples.push(normal(random, fixture.dps.mean, fixture.dps.stddev));
        }
        onProgress(encodeWireProgress({ ...statsOf(samples) , iterationsDone: samples.length }));
      }

      return encodeWireResult({ ...statsOf(samples), summary: fixture.summary });
    },

    raidSimResultCombination(results) {
      const parts = results.map(decodeWireResult);
      let n = 0;
      let sum = 0;
      let sumSquares = 0;
      let min = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      let max = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
      for (const part of parts) {
        n += part.iterationsRun;
        sum += part.mean * part.iterationsRun;
        sumSquares += part.iterationsRun * (part.stddev ** 2 + part.mean ** 2);
        min = Math.min(min, part.min);
        max = Math.max(max, part.max);
      }
      const mean = n === 0 ? 0 : sum / n;
      const variance = n === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, sumSquares / n - mean * mean);
      return encodeWireResult({
        iterationsRun: n,
        mean,
        stddev: Math.sqrt(variance),
        min: n === 0 ? 0 : min,
        max: n === 0 ? 0 : max,
        summary: fixture.summary,
      });
    },

    computeStats(request) {
      const wire = decodeWireRequest(request);
      const gearScore = Object.values(wire.gear).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
      const talentScore = wire.talents.length;
      return new TextEncoder().encode(
        JSON.stringify({
          attack_power: 900 + Math.round(gearScore / 100) + talentScore * 5,
          crit: 0.21,
          hit: 0.08,
          spell_power: 0,
        }),
      );
    },

    abortById(runId) {
      aborted.add(runId);
    },
  };
}
```

---
## Task 4: The worker pool

`min(8, hardwareConcurrency)` workers, each holding one engine instance; iterations split with the engine's own `raidSimRequestSplit`; progress per shard; abort on demand and on navigation. This is the contract's `web/src/lib/sim/worker.ts`, and the module it drives.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/estimate.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/worker.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/sim.worker.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/worker.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EngineModule`, `loadEngine` (Task 3); `decodeWireProgress` (Task 3); `ENGINE_VERSION` (Task 1); `Estimate` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/estimate.ts`: `interface ShardProgress { shard: number; iterationsDone: number; mean: number; stddev: number; min: number; max: number }`, `interface PooledEstimate { estimate: Estimate; iterationsDone: number }`, `const EMPTY_ESTIMATE: Estimate`, `function combineEstimate(shards: readonly ShardProgress[]): PooledEstimate`, `function confidenceBand(estimate: Estimate): number`.
  - `web/src/lib/sim/worker.ts`: `const MAX_WORKERS = 8`, `function poolSize(hardwareConcurrency: number | undefined): number`, `type ToWorker`, `type FromWorker`, `interface PoolWorker`, `interface PoolOptions`, `interface SimPool`, `function createPool(options?: PoolOptions): SimPool`.
  - Task 5 calls `pool.split`, `pool.run`, `pool.combine`, `pool.abort`; Task 11 calls `pool.stats`; Task 20 creates a pool of its own for the planner.

### Why the live estimate is pooled in TypeScript and not in the engine

The contract allows five wasm entrypoints and `raidSimResultCombination` is one of them, so the **final** result is always the engine's own combination — nothing here reimplements the per-ability metrics. But calling it on every progress tick would mean a wasm round-trip per tick, and the contract also says the partial results are "combined on the main thread as they arrive". So `combineEstimate` pools the shard distributions numerically — mean, pooled variance, standard error, min, max — for the figure on screen, and the engine combines once at the end for the result that is stored. Step 8's test asserts the two agree to six decimal places, which is what makes the fast path honest rather than an approximation nobody checked.

`raidSimResultCombination`, `raidSimRequestSplit` and `computeStats` are wasm exports, so the main thread has no instance to call them on and loading a second 4 MB module there would defeat the LCP budget. The pool routes all three to worker 0, which already holds one. `run.ts` still owns the decision of what to combine and when, which is what the contract's division of ownership actually asks for; record this reading in the finish report.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { EMPTY_ESTIMATE, combineEstimate, confidenceBand, type ShardProgress } from './estimate';

/** Turns raw samples into the shape a shard reports, so the test can check against truth. */
function shardsFromSamples(groups: number[][]): ShardProgress[] {
  return groups.map((values, shard) => {
    const n = values.length;
    const mean = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
    const variance = values.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / n;
    return {
      shard,
      iterationsDone: n,
      mean,
      stddev: Math.sqrt(variance),
      min: Math.min(...values),
      max: Math.max(...values),
    };
  });
}

describe('combineEstimate', () => {
  it('matches the statistics of the pooled sample, not an average of averages', () => {
    const all = [900, 1100, 1000, 1200, 800, 1050, 950, 1300, 700, 1000];
    const combined = combineEstimate(shardsFromSamples([all.slice(0, 4), all.slice(4, 7), all.slice(7)]));
    const mean = all.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / all.length;
    const variance = all.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / all.length;
    expect(combined.iterationsDone).toBe(10);
    expect(combined.estimate.mean).toBeCloseTo(mean, 9);
    expect(combined.estimate.stddev).toBeCloseTo(Math.sqrt(variance), 9);
    expect(combined.estimate.error).toBeCloseTo(Math.sqrt(variance / 10), 9);
    expect(combined.estimate.min).toBe(700);
    expect(combined.estimate.max).toBe(1300);
  });

  it('ignores shards that have not reported an iteration yet', () => {
    const [only] = shardsFromSamples([[1000, 1000, 1000]]);
    const idle: ShardProgress = { shard: 1, iterationsDone: 0, mean: 0, stddev: 0, min: 0, max: 0 };
    const combined = combineEstimate([only, idle]);
    expect(combined.iterationsDone).toBe(3);
    expect(combined.estimate.mean).toBe(1000);
    expect(combined.estimate.min).toBe(1000);
  });

  it('is all zeros before any shard reports, so the page can render it', () => {
    expect(combineEstimate([])).toEqual({ estimate: EMPTY_ESTIMATE, iterationsDone: 0 });
  });
});

describe('confidenceBand', () => {
  it('is the 95% band, which is what the page shows beside the figure', () => {
    expect(confidenceBand({ mean: 1400, stddev: 140, error: 2.5, min: 0, max: 0 })).toBeCloseTo(4.9, 9);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `web/src/lib/sim/estimate.ts`**

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/estimate.ts
// Pooling shard distributions into one, for the DPS figure that updates while the sim runs.
//
// An average of the shards' averages is only right when the shards are equal-sized, and a
// mean of their standard deviations is never right. Both are pooled properly here from the
// sum and the sum of squares each shard implies (n * (s^2 + m^2)), which is exactly what
// the engine's own raidSimResultCombination does at the end -- worker.test.ts asserts the
// two agree, which is what lets the page show this number a wasm round-trip earlier.
import type { Estimate } from './types';

export const EMPTY_ESTIMATE: Estimate = { mean: 0, stddev: 0, error: 0, min: 0, max: 0 };

export interface ShardProgress {
  shard: number;
  iterationsDone: number;
  mean: number;
  stddev: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
}

export interface PooledEstimate {
  estimate: Estimate;
  iterationsDone: number;
}

export function combineEstimate(shards: readonly ShardProgress[]): PooledEstimate {
  let n = 0;
  let sum = 0;
  let sumSquares = 0;
  let min = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  let max = Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;

  for (const shard of shards) {
    if (shard.iterationsDone <= 0) continue;
    n += shard.iterationsDone;
    sum += shard.mean * shard.iterationsDone;
    sumSquares += shard.iterationsDone * (shard.stddev * shard.stddev + shard.mean * shard.mean);
    if (shard.min < min) min = shard.min;
    if (shard.max > max) max = shard.max;
  }

  if (n === 0) return { estimate: EMPTY_ESTIMATE, iterationsDone: 0 };

  const mean = sum / n;
  // Floating-point subtraction of two near-equal large numbers can land a hair below zero.
  const variance = Math.max(0, sumSquares / n - mean * mean);
  const stddev = Math.sqrt(variance);
  return { estimate: { mean, stddev, error: stddev / Math.sqrt(n), min, max }, iterationsDone: n };
}

/** The 95% band the run control prints after the figure: "1,432 ± 5". */
export function confidenceBand(estimate: Estimate): number {
  return 1.96 * estimate.error;
}
```

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createFakeEngine } from '../../fixtures/sim/engine-fake';
import { decodeWireProgress, decodeWireResult } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import { combineEstimate, type ShardProgress } from './estimate';
import { buildRaidSimRequest, type RawInput } from './raw';
import { DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER } from './types';
import { createPool, poolSize, type FromWorker, type PoolWorker, type ToWorker } from './worker';

const input: RawInput = {
  spec: 'warrior-fury',
  race: 'orc',
  level: 60,
  gear: { head: 12640 },
  talents: [2001],
  buffs: [],
  consumables: [],
  encounter: DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER,
  iterations: 800,
  randomSeed: 3,
};

/** One in-process worker: the same protocol sim.worker.ts speaks, over the fake engine. */
function fakeWorker(): PoolWorker & { terminated: boolean } {
  const engine = createFakeEngine({ tickMs: 0, ticks: 2 });
  const listeners: ((event: { data: FromWorker }) => void)[] = [];
  const emit = (data: FromWorker): void => listeners.forEach((listen) => listen({ data }));

  const worker = {
    terminated: false,
    addEventListener(_type: 'message', listener: (event: { data: FromWorker }) => void) {
      listeners.push(listener);
    },
    terminate() {
      worker.terminated = true;
    },
    postMessage(message: ToWorker) {
      void (async () => {
        if (message.kind === 'abort') {
          engine.abortById(message.runId);
          return;
        }
        const token = message.token;
        try {
          if (message.kind === 'split') {
            emit({
              kind: 'many',
              token,
              results: engine.raidSimRequestSplit(message.request, message.shards),
            });
          } else if (message.kind === 'combine') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.raidSimResultCombination(message.results) });
          } else if (message.kind === 'stats') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.computeStats(message.request) });
          } else {
            const result = await engine.raidSimAsync(
              message.request,
              (bytes) => emit({ kind: 'progress', token, ...decodeWireProgress(bytes) }),
              message.runId,
            );
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          emit({ kind: 'failed', token, message: (error as Error).message });
        }
      })();
    },
  };
  return worker;
}

const spawned: (PoolWorker & { terminated: boolean })[] = [];
const spawn = (): PoolWorker => {
  const worker = fakeWorker();
  spawned.push(worker);
  return worker;
};

afterEach(() => {
  spawned.length = 0;
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe('poolSize', () => {
  it('caps at eight cores, the contract ceiling', () => {
    expect(poolSize(16)).toBe(8);
    expect(poolSize(8)).toBe(8);
    expect(poolSize(4)).toBe(4);
    expect(poolSize(1)).toBe(1);
  });

  it('falls back to four when the browser does not say', () => {
    expect(poolSize(undefined)).toBe(4);
    expect(poolSize(0)).toBe(4);
    expect(poolSize(Number.NaN)).toBe(4);
  });
});

describe('createPool', () => {
  it('sizes itself from hardwareConcurrency', () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 12, spawn });
    expect(pool.size).toBe(8);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('splits, runs every shard, reports progress per shard and combines', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn });
    const shards = await pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), pool.size);
    expect(shards).toHaveLength(4);

    const seen: ShardProgress[] = [];
    const results = await pool.run(shards, 'run-1', (progress) => seen.push(progress));
    expect(results).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(new Set(seen.map((p) => p.shard))).toEqual(new Set([0, 1, 2, 3]));

    const combined = decodeWireResult(await pool.combine(results));
    expect(combined.iterationsRun).toBe(800);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('spawns one worker per shard and no more', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn });
    const shards = await pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), pool.size);
    await pool.run(shards, 'run-2', () => {});
    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(4);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('pools the shard progress into the same numbers the engine combines to', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn });
    const shards = await pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), pool.size);
    const latest = new Map<number, ShardProgress>();
    const results = await pool.run(shards, 'run-3', (progress) => latest.set(progress.shard, progress));
    const pooled = combineEstimate([...latest.values()]);
    const combined = decodeWireResult(await pool.combine(results));
    expect(pooled.iterationsDone).toBe(combined.iterationsRun);
    expect(pooled.estimate.mean).toBeCloseTo(combined.mean, 6);
    expect(pooled.estimate.stddev).toBeCloseTo(combined.stddev, 6);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('rejects every pending call when the pool is terminated, and terminates its workers', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 2, spawn });
    const shards = await pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), 2);
    const running = pool.run(shards, 'run-4', () => {});
    pool.terminate();
    await expect(running).rejects.toThrow('terminated');
    expect(spawned.every((worker) => worker.terminated)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('refuses new work after termination rather than spawning again', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 2, spawn });
    pool.terminate();
    await expect(pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), 2)).rejects.toThrow('terminated');
    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('tears the pool down when the page goes away', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 2, spawn });
    const shards = await pool.split(buildRaidSimRequest(input), 2);
    const running = pool.run(shards, 'run-5', () => {});
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('pagehide'));
    await expect(running).rejects.toThrow('terminated');
  });
});
```

Create `web/src/lib/sim/worker.ts`:

```ts
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from './version';
import type { ShardProgress } from './estimate';

export const MAX_WORKERS = 8;
const DEFAULT_CORES = 4;

export function poolSize(hardwareConcurrency: number | undefined): number {
  const cores =
    typeof hardwareConcurrency === 'number' && Number.isFinite(hardwareConcurrency) && hardwareConcurrency > 0
      ? Math.floor(hardwareConcurrency)
      : DEFAULT_CORES;
  return Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_WORKERS, cores));
}

export type ToWorker =
  | { kind: 'split'; token: number; request: Uint8Array; shards: number }
  | { kind: 'run'; token: number; runId: string; request: Uint8Array }
  | { kind: 'combine'; token: number; results: Uint8Array[] }
  | { kind: 'stats'; token: number; request: Uint8Array }
  | { kind: 'abort'; runId: string };

export type FromWorker =
  | {
      kind: 'progress';
      token: number;
      iterationsDone: number;
      mean: number;
      stddev: number;
      min: number;
      max: number;
    }
  | { kind: 'one'; token: number; result: Uint8Array }
  | { kind: 'many'; token: number; results: Uint8Array[] }
  | { kind: 'failed'; token: number; message: string };

export interface PoolWorker {
  postMessage(message: ToWorker): void;
  addEventListener(type: 'message', listener: (event: { data: FromWorker }) => void): void;
  terminate(): void;
}

export interface PoolOptions {
  engineVersion?: string;
  hardwareConcurrency?: number;
  spawn?: (index: number) => PoolWorker;
}

export interface SimPool {
  readonly size: number;
  split(request: Uint8Array, shards: number): Promise<Uint8Array[]>;
  run(
    shards: readonly Uint8Array[],
    runId: string,
    onProgress: (progress: ShardProgress) => void,
  ): Promise<Uint8Array[]>;
  combine(results: readonly Uint8Array[]): Promise<Uint8Array>;
  stats(request: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array>;
  abort(runId: string): void;
  terminate(): void;
}

interface Pending {
  resolve: (value: never) => void;
  reject: (error: Error) => void;
  onProgress?: (progress: ShardProgress) => void;
  shard: number;
}

function defaultSpawn(): PoolWorker {
  return new Worker(new URL('./sim.worker.ts', import.meta.url), {
    type: 'module',
    name: 'forever-sim',
  }) as unknown as PoolWorker;
}

export function createPool(options: PoolOptions = {}): SimPool {
  const size = poolSize(options.hardwareConcurrency ?? globalThis.navigator?.hardwareConcurrency);
  const spawn = options.spawn ?? defaultSpawn;
  const engineVersion = options.engineVersion ?? ENGINE_VERSION;
  void engineVersion;

  const workers: PoolWorker[] = [];
  const pending = new Map<number, Pending>();
  let nextToken = 1;
  let stopped = false;

  function workerAt(index: number): PoolWorker {
    let worker = workers[index];
    if (worker === undefined) {
      worker = spawn(index);
      worker.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
        const message = event.data;
        const entry = pending.get(message.token);
        if (entry === undefined) return;
        if (message.kind === 'progress') {
          entry.onProgress?.({
            shard: entry.shard,
            iterationsDone: message.iterationsDone,
            mean: message.mean,
            stddev: message.stddev,
            min: message.min,
            max: message.max,
          });
          return;
        }
        pending.delete(message.token);
        if (message.kind === 'failed') entry.reject(new Error(message.message));
        else if (message.kind === 'one') (entry.resolve as (v: Uint8Array) => void)(message.result);
        else (entry.resolve as (v: Uint8Array[]) => void)(message.results);
      });
      workers[index] = worker;
    }
    return worker;
  }

  function send<T>(
    index: number,
    build: (token: number) => ToWorker,
    onProgress?: (progress: ShardProgress) => void,
  ): Promise<T> {
    if (stopped) return Promise.reject(new Error('sim pool: terminated'));
    const token = nextToken;
    nextToken += 1;
    return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
      pending.set(token, {
        resolve: resolve as unknown as (value: never) => void,
        reject,
        onProgress,
        shard: index,
      });
      workerAt(index).postMessage(build(token));
    });
  }

  function abort(runId: string): void {
    for (let index = 0; index < workers.length; index += 1) {
      workers[index]?.postMessage({ kind: 'abort', runId });
    }
  }

  function terminate(): void {
    stopped = true;
    for (const entry of pending.values()) entry.reject(new Error('sim pool: terminated'));
    pending.clear();
    for (const worker of workers) worker.terminate();
    workers.length = 0;
    globalThis.removeEventListener?.('pagehide', onPageHide);
  }

  function onPageHide(): void {
    terminate();
  }
  globalThis.addEventListener?.('pagehide', onPageHide);

  return {
    get size() {
      return size;
    },
    split(request, shards) {
      return send<Uint8Array[]>(0, (token) => ({ kind: 'split', token, request, shards }));
    },
    run(shards, runId, onProgress) {
      return Promise.all(
        shards.map((request, index) =>
          send<Uint8Array>(
            index % size,
            (token) => ({ kind: 'run', token, runId, request }),
            (progress) => onProgress({ ...progress, shard: index }),
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    combine(results) {
      return send<Uint8Array>(0, (token) => ({ kind: 'combine', token, results: [...results] }));
    },
    stats(request) {
      return send<Uint8Array>(0, (token) => ({ kind: 'stats', token, request }));
    },
    abort,
    terminate,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the worker script**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/sim.worker.ts`. This is the file `new Worker(new URL('./sim.worker.ts', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })` loads; Vite compiles it as a separate chunk, which is why it may not be imported by anything on the page's critical path.

```ts
/// <reference lib="webworker" />
import { loadEngine, type EngineModule } from './engine';
import { decodeWireProgress } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import type { FromWorker, ToWorker } from './worker';

const scope = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

let engine: EngineModule | null = null;
let loading: Promise<EngineModule> | null = null;

function engineOnce(): Promise<EngineModule> {
  if (engine !== null) return Promise.resolve(engine);
  loading ??= loadEngine().then((loaded) => {
    engine = loaded;
    return loaded;
  });
  return loading;
}

function reply(message: FromWorker): void {
  scope.postMessage(message);
}

async function handle(message: ToWorker): Promise<void> {
  if (message.kind === 'abort') {
    engine?.abortById(message.runId);
    return;
  }
  const token = message.token;
  try {
    const loaded = await engineOnce();
    switch (message.kind) {
      case 'split':
        reply({ kind: 'many', token, results: loaded.raidSimRequestSplit(message.request, message.shards) });
        return;
      case 'combine':
        reply({ kind: 'one', token, result: loaded.raidSimResultCombination(message.results) });
        return;
      case 'stats':
        reply({ kind: 'one', token, result: loaded.computeStats(message.request) });
        return;
      case 'run': {
        const result = await loaded.raidSimAsync(
          message.request,
          (bytes) => {
            const progress = decodeWireProgress(bytes);
            reply({ kind: 'progress', token, ...progress });
          },
          message.runId,
        );
        reply({ kind: 'one', token, result });
        return;
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    reply({ kind: 'failed', token, message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
  }
}

scope.addEventListener('message', (event: MessageEvent<ToWorker>) => {
  void handle(event.data);
});
```
