---
## Task 5: Request building, partial-result combination, and the live estimate

The contract's `web/src/lib/sim/run.ts`: one function that turns a character and its settings into a `SimResult`, driving the pool, publishing a running estimate, and reducing every failure to two a player can read.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/summarize.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/run.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/run.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimPool`, `ShardProgress`, `combineEstimate`, `EMPTY_ESTIMATE` (Task 4); `buildRaidSimRequest`, `RawInput` (Task 3); `ENGINE_VERSION` (Task 1); `SimRequest`, `SimResult`, `Estimate`, `CharacterSource` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/summarize.ts`: `interface DecodedResult { iterationsRun: number; mean: number; stddev: number; min: number; max: number; summary: Summary }`, `function decodeResult(bytes: Uint8Array): DecodedResult`.
  - `web/src/lib/sim/run.ts`: `const RUN_CANCELLED`, `const RUN_FAILED`, `class SimRunError`, `interface RunInput { spec: string; source: CharacterSource; raw: RawInput }`, `interface RunUpdate { estimate: Estimate; iterationsDone: number; iterationsTotal: number }`, `interface RunHandle { runId: string; result: Promise<SimResult>; cancel(): void }`, `function buildSimRequest(input: RunInput): SimRequest`, `function runSim(pool, input, onUpdate, now?): RunHandle`.
  - Task 13 drives the run control from `RunHandle` and `RunUpdate`; Task 20 calls `runSim` with 500 iterations.

### The contract gap this task fills, and how

The contract names one adapter — `sim/adapter.Summarize(res *proto.RaidSimResult, req SimRequest)` — and it is Go, running in the API and the validation job. The browser lane also needs a `summary.Summary`, because the whole design rests on the report components rendering a sim. It cannot call Go, it cannot post the result to the API (the browser lane's promise is that nothing leaves the device unless the player saves), and it cannot ask the engine for one: the contract allows exactly five wasm entrypoints and no others.

So `web/src/lib/sim/summarize.ts` is the TypeScript sibling of `sim/adapter`, and the mapping it implements is the contract's own table, field by field. Under the fake engine it is the identity over the fixture's summary; when the engine lands, its body decodes the protobuf and applies that table, and a golden test pins its output against `sim/adapter`'s checked-in golden JSON so the two cannot drift. **Record this in the finish report:** the contract does not say how the browser lane produces a summary, and this is the ruling.

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createFakeEngine } from '../../fixtures/sim/engine-fake';
import { decodeWireProgress } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import {
  RUN_CANCELLED,
  RUN_FAILED,
  SimRunError,
  buildSimRequest,
  runSim,
  type RunInput,
  type RunUpdate,
} from './run';
import { DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER, ITERATIONS } from './types';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from './version';
import { createPool, type FromWorker, type PoolWorker, type ToWorker } from './worker';

const input: RunInput = {
  spec: 'warrior-fury',
  source: { kind: 'addon', ref: '', captured_at: '2026-09-14T10:00:00Z' },
  raw: {
    spec: 'warrior-fury',
    race: 'orc',
    level: 60,
    gear: { head: 12640 },
    talents: [2001],
    buffs: [],
    consumables: [],
    encounter: DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER,
    iterations: ITERATIONS.normal,
    randomSeed: 5,
  },
};

function fakeWorker(tickMs = 0): PoolWorker {
  const engine = createFakeEngine({ tickMs, ticks: 3 });
  const listeners: ((event: { data: FromWorker }) => void)[] = [];
  const emit = (data: FromWorker): void => listeners.forEach((listen) => listen({ data }));
  return {
    addEventListener(_type, listener) {
      listeners.push(listener);
    },
    terminate() {},
    postMessage(message: ToWorker) {
      void (async () => {
        if (message.kind === 'abort') return engine.abortById(message.runId);
        const token = message.token;
        try {
          if (message.kind === 'split') {
            emit({
              kind: 'many',
              token,
              results: engine.raidSimRequestSplit(message.request, message.shards),
            });
          } else if (message.kind === 'combine') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.raidSimResultCombination(message.results) });
          } else if (message.kind === 'stats') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.computeStats(message.request) });
          } else {
            const result = await engine.raidSimAsync(
              message.request,
              (bytes) => emit({ kind: 'progress', token, ...decodeWireProgress(bytes) }),
              message.runId,
            );
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          emit({ kind: 'failed', token, message: (error as Error).message });
        }
      })();
    },
  };
}

describe('buildSimRequest', () => {
  it('is the contract envelope, with raw left out', () => {
    expect(buildSimRequest(input)).toEqual({
      engine_version: ENGINE_VERSION,
      spec: 'warrior-fury',
      source: { kind: 'addon', ref: '', captured_at: '2026-09-14T10:00:00Z' },
      encounter: DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER,
      iterations: 3000,
      random_seed: 5,
    });
  });
});

describe('runSim', () => {
  it('opens at zero, refines as shards land, and finishes on the engine’s own numbers', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn: () => fakeWorker() });
    const updates: RunUpdate[] = [];
    const handle = runSim(pool, input, (update) => updates.push(update));
    const result = await handle.result;

    expect(updates[0]).toEqual({
      estimate: { mean: 0, stddev: 0, error: 0, min: 0, max: 0 },
      iterationsDone: 0,
      iterationsTotal: 3000,
    });
    expect(updates.length).toBeGreaterThan(3);
    const progressed = updates.map((u) => u.iterationsDone);
    expect(progressed).toEqual([...progressed].sort((a, b) => a - b));
    expect(updates.at(-1)?.iterationsDone).toBe(3000);
    expect(result.iterations_run).toBe(3000);
    expect(result.lane).toBe('browser');
    expect(result.dps.mean).toBeCloseTo(updates.at(-1)!.estimate.mean, 6);
    expect(result.dps.error).toBeCloseTo(result.dps.stddev / Math.sqrt(3000), 9);
    expect(result.summary.damage_done[0].name).toBe('Thrallgar');
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('is within the contract’s error for 3,000 iterations', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn: () => fakeWorker() });
    const result = await runSim(pool, input, () => {}).result;
    // The design's claim: 3,000 iterations buys about +/-0.25% at 95% confidence.
    expect((1.96 * result.dps.error) / result.dps.mean).toBeLessThan(0.0075);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('records the wall clock of the run', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 4, spawn: () => fakeWorker() });
    let clock = 1000;
    const result = await runSim(
      pool,
      input,
      () => {
        clock += 250;
      },
      () => clock,
    ).result;
    expect(result.duration_ms).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('reports a cancel as cancelled, not as a failure', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 2, spawn: () => fakeWorker(20) });
    const handle = runSim(pool, input, () => {});
    const settled = handle.result.catch((error: unknown) => error);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(25);
    handle.cancel();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(100);
    const error = await settled;
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(SimRunError);
    expect((error as SimRunError).cancelled).toBe(true);
    expect((error as SimRunError).message).toBe(RUN_CANCELLED);
    pool.terminate();
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('reports an engine failure as a failure, with copy a player can read', async () => {
    // A worker that answers every request with a failure, which is what a wasm trap or a
    // missing engine artifact looks like from the pool's side. The emitter is a closure
    // rather than a property on the object literal: `this` in a literal has type `{}` and
    // astro check rejects assigning to it.
    let emit: (data: FromWorker) => void = () => {};
    const broken: PoolWorker = {
      addEventListener: (_type, listener) => {
        emit = (data) => listener({ data });
      },
      terminate: () => {},
      postMessage: (message: ToWorker) => {
        if (message.kind === 'abort') return;
        emit({ kind: 'failed', token: message.token, message: 'wasm trap' });
      },
    };
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 1, spawn: () => broken });
    const error = await runSim(pool, input, () => {}).result.catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect((error as SimRunError).cancelled).toBe(false);
    expect((error as SimRunError).message).toBe(RUN_FAILED);
    pool.terminate();
  });

  it('never asks for more shards than there are iterations', async () => {
    const pool = createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 8, spawn: () => fakeWorker() });
    const result = await runSim(pool, { ...input, raw: { ...input.raw, iterations: 3 } }, () => {}).result;
    expect(result.iterations_run).toBe(3);
    pool.terminate();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the result adapter**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/summarize.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/summarize.ts
// The browser's half of sim/adapter. The Go adapter turns a RaidSimResult into a
// logs/engine/summary.Summary for the API and the validation job; the browser needs the
// same Summary so the report components can render a sim, and it can reach neither the Go
// code nor a sixth wasm entrypoint. So this is the TypeScript sibling, implementing the
// same field-by-field table the contract sets out:
//
//   EngineVersion  "sim:" + ENGINE_VERSION          Auras      PlayerMetrics.auras
//   FightIndex     1                                Casts      ActionMetrics cast counts
//   DurationMS     avg_iteration_duration * 1000    Resources  PlayerMetrics.resources
//   DamageDone     PlayerMetrics.actions (+ pets)   Roster     one RosterRow for the player
//   everything else empty
//
// Under the fake engine the summary arrives whole and this is the identity over it. When
// sim.wasm lands, the wasm branch decodes the protobuf and applies the table, and a golden
// test pins it against sim/adapter's own testdata so the two renderings cannot diverge.
import { decodeWireResult } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import type { Summary } from '../report/types';
import { ENGINE_MODE } from './engine';

export interface DecodedResult {
  iterationsRun: number;
  mean: number;
  stddev: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  summary: Summary;
}

export function decodeResult(bytes: Uint8Array): DecodedResult {
  if (ENGINE_MODE === 'fake') return decodeWireResult(bytes);
  throw new Error('decodeResult: the wasm result adapter is not wired up yet');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the run loop**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/run.ts`:

```ts
import { combineEstimate, EMPTY_ESTIMATE, type ShardProgress } from './estimate';
import { buildRaidSimRequest, type RawInput } from './raw';
import { decodeResult } from './summarize';
import type { CharacterSource, Estimate, SimRequest, SimResult } from './types';
import { ENGINE_VERSION } from './version';
import type { SimPool } from './worker';

export const RUN_CANCELLED = 'This sim was stopped.';
export const RUN_FAILED = 'The engine could not run this character.';

export class SimRunError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly cancelled: boolean,
    options: { cause?: unknown } = {},
  ) {
    super(message, options);
    this.name = 'SimRunError';
  }
}

export interface RunInput {
  spec: string;
  source: CharacterSource;
  raw: RawInput;
}

export interface RunUpdate {
  estimate: Estimate;
  iterationsDone: number;
  iterationsTotal: number;
}

export interface RunHandle {
  readonly runId: string;
  readonly result: Promise<SimResult>;
  cancel(): void;
}

export function buildSimRequest(input: RunInput): SimRequest {
  return {
    engine_version: ENGINE_VERSION,
    spec: input.spec,
    source: input.source,
    encounter: input.raw.encounter,
    iterations: input.raw.iterations,
    random_seed: input.raw.randomSeed,
  };
}

let runCounter = 0;

function nextRunId(): string {
  runCounter += 1;
  return `sim-${runCounter}`;
}

export function runSim(
  pool: SimPool,
  input: RunInput,
  onUpdate: (update: RunUpdate) => void,
  now: () => number = () => Date.now(),
): RunHandle {
  const runId = nextRunId();
  const total = input.raw.iterations;
  let cancelled = false;

  async function execute(): Promise<SimResult> {
    const startedAt = now();
    const requestBytes = buildRaidSimRequest(input.raw);
    const shards = await pool.split(requestBytes, Math.min(pool.size, total));

    const latest = new Map<number, ShardProgress>();
    onUpdate({ estimate: EMPTY_ESTIMATE, iterationsDone: 0, iterationsTotal: total });

    const parts = await pool.run(shards, runId, (progress) => {
      latest.set(progress.shard, progress);
      const pooled = combineEstimate([...latest.values()]);
      onUpdate({ ...pooled, iterationsTotal: total });
    });

    const combined = decodeResult(await pool.combine(parts));
    const estimate: Estimate = {
      mean: combined.mean,
      stddev: combined.stddev,
      error: combined.iterationsRun > 0 ? combined.stddev / Math.sqrt(combined.iterationsRun) : 0,
      min: combined.min,
      max: combined.max,
    };
    onUpdate({ estimate, iterationsDone: combined.iterationsRun, iterationsTotal: total });

    return {
      engine_version: ENGINE_VERSION,
      request: buildSimRequest(input),
      lane: 'browser',
      dps: estimate,
      iterations_run: combined.iterationsRun,
      duration_ms: Math.round(now() - startedAt),
      summary: combined.summary,
    };
  }

  // Every failure a player can cause or see becomes one of two SimRunErrors: a stop they
  // asked for, or an engine that could not run this character. The original is kept as the
  // cause so a console still shows the wasm trap or the terminated pool underneath.
  const result = execute().catch((cause: unknown) => {
    throw new SimRunError(cancelled ? RUN_CANCELLED : RUN_FAILED, cancelled, { cause });
  });

  return {
    runId,
    result,
    cancel() {
      cancelled = true;
      pool.abort(runId);
    },
  };
}
```

# Group B — the character model

Tasks 6 and 7, in order. Independent of groups A, C and E. It depends only on Task 1 and Task 2.

## Task 6: The character model, the planner conversion both ways, and the settings

One `SimCharacter` behind all four sources, convertible to and from the planner's `BuildDraft` without losing a point or a slot, plus the settings-bar vocabulary the sim page and the request builder both read.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/character.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/settings.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/character.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/sim/settings.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `durationLabel`, `encounterLabel` from `web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts` (Task 2); `pointsPerTree` from `web/src/lib/planner/derive.ts`; `decodeFS1`, `orderFromRanks` from `web/src/lib/planner/fs1.ts`; `indexTalents`, `TalentIndex` from `web/src/lib/planner/rules.ts`; `BuildDraft`, `ClassRow`, `RaceRow`, `Gear`, `TalentFile`, `BASE_LEVEL` from `web/src/lib/planner/types.ts`; `SPECS` (Task 1); `CharacterSource`, `EncounterSpec`, `DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/character.ts`: `interface SimCharacter`, `const MAX_LEVEL`, `function specForSplit(classSlug, split): string`, `function specOf(index, order): string`, `function levelFor(order): number`, `function toBuildDraft(character, classes, races): BuildDraft | null`, `interface CharacterExtras`, `function fromBuildDraft(draft, talents, classes, races, extras): SimCharacter | null`, `function plannerHrefFor(character): string`, `const CLASS_MISMATCH`, `const UNREACHABLE_TALENTS`, `type CharacterResult`, `function characterFromFs1(code, talents, classes, races, source, name?): CharacterResult`.
  - `web/src/lib/sim/settings.ts`: `DURATIONS`, `durationLabel` and `encounterLabel` (re-exported from `encounter.ts`, Task 2), `BUFF_PRESETS`, `BuffPresetId`, `PRESET_BUFFS`, `PRESET_CONSUMABLES`, `interface SimSettings { encounter: EncounterSpec; preset: BuffPresetId; buffs: number[]; consumables: number[] }`, `defaultSettings`, `withPreset`, `withDuration`, `withTargets`, `withExecutePhase`, `executePhaseOn`, `settingsLabel`.
  - Task 7 builds a `SimCharacter` from each source; Tasks 11 and 12 render one; Task 20 converts the planner's live store into one.

### The DRY rule this task is written around

`web/src/lib/planner/fs1.ts` already decodes the addon's FS1 string and reconstructs a legal point order from ranks — the report lane shipped it for `/planner?code=`. **Do not write a second FS1 parser.** `characterFromFs1` composes `decodeFS1` and `orderFromRanks` and adds only what is the sim's own concern: which class the talents are for, which spec the split names, and turning `dropped` into copy. The addon writes one string; both pillars read it with the same code, so a format change cannot reach one and miss the other.

`decodeFS1` has two behaviours a test must respect and a component must not work around: it requires **three** tree segments even for a class whose data file carries fewer (real vanilla trees are always three, and the fixture's two-tree warrior is fixture data), and it requires the gear field to be **present**, so a gearless code ends in a bare `:`. A valid fixture code is therefore `FS1:1.15.9.69722:warrior:orc:0/5530515/0:head=12640,main_hand=11726`.

```ts
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { indexTalents, validateOrder } from '../planner/rules';
import type { ClassRow, RaceRow, TalentFile } from '../planner/types';
import {
  CLASS_MISMATCH,
  UNREACHABLE_TALENTS,
  characterFromFs1,
  fromBuildDraft,
  levelFor,
  specForSplit,

  toBuildDraft,
  type SimCharacter,
} from './character';
import type { CharacterSource } from './types';

const WEB_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '../../..');
const BUILD = '1.15.9.69722';

async function json<T>(relative: string): Promise<T> {
  return JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(WEB_ROOT, 'public/data', BUILD, relative), 'utf8')) as T;
}
const warriorTalents = (): Promise<TalentFile> => json<TalentFile>('talents/warrior.json');
const classes = (): Promise<ClassRow[]> => json<ClassRow[]>('classes.json');
const races = (): Promise<RaceRow[]> => json<RaceRow[]>('races.json');

const source: CharacterSource = { kind: 'addon', ref: '', captured_at: '2026-09-14T10:00:00Z' };
const FURY = 'FS1:1.15.9.69722:warrior:orc:0/5530515/0:head=12640,main_hand=11726';

describe('specForSplit', () => {
  it('names the tree with the most points', () => {
    expect(specForSplit('warrior', [0, 31, 20])).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(specForSplit('warrior', [31, 0, 20])).toBe('warrior-arms');
    expect(specForSplit('mage', [0, 0, 31])).toBe('mage-frost');
  });

  it('resolves a tie to the first tree, as the phase 2 spec does', () => {
    expect(specForSplit('warrior', [20, 20, 11])).toBe('warrior-arms');
  });

  it('degrades to class-and-index when the spec list has no row', () => {
    expect(specForSplit('warrior', [0, 0, 51])).toBe('warrior-2');
  });
});

describe('levelFor', () => {
  it('is 9 with no points, 10 at the first, and capped at 60', () => {
    expect(levelFor([])).toBe(9);
    expect(levelFor([1001])).toBe(10);
    expect(levelFor(Array.from({ length: 51 }, () => 1001))).toBe(60);
  });
});

describe('characterFromFs1', () => {
  it('reads the export into a character with a legal point order', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const character = characterFromFs1(FURY, file, classRows, raceRows, source);
    expect(character.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!character.ok) return;
    expect(character.character.class_slug).toBe('warrior');
    expect(character.character.race_slug).toBe('orc');
    expect(character.character.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(specOf(indexTalents(file), character.character.point_order)).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(character.character.gear).toEqual({ head: 12640, main_hand: 11726 });
    expect(character.character.point_order).toHaveLength(24);
    expect(validateOrder(indexTalents(file), character.character.point_order)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('spends the prerequisite before the talent that gates on it', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const character = characterFromFs1(FURY, file, classRows, raceRows, source);
    if (!character.ok) throw new Error('expected a character');
    const order = character.character.point_order;
    const deathWish = order.indexOf(2006);
    expect(order.slice(0, deathWish).filter((id) => id === 2003)).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it("passes the planner decoder's own reason through, which names the prefix", async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const result = characterFromFs1('FS2:1:warrior:orc:0/0/0:', file, classRows, raceRows, source);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) return;
    expect(result.message).toBe('That code is FS2; this site reads FS1.');
  });

  it('refuses an export for a class whose talents these are not', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const result = characterFromFs1('FS1:1.15.9.69722:mage:gnome:0/0/5:', file, classRows, raceRows, source);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) return;
    expect(result.message).toBe(CLASS_MISMATCH('mage', 'warrior'));
  });

  it('names the talents it had to drop rather than silently truncating', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const result = characterFromFs1(
      'FS1:1.15.9.69722:warrior:orc:0/0000010/0:',
      file,
      classRows,
      raceRows,
      source,
    );
    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (result.ok) return;
    expect(result.message).toBe(UNREACHABLE_TALENTS('Death Wish'));
  });
});

describe('the planner conversion, both ways', () => {
  it('round-trips a character through BuildDraft without losing a point or a slot', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const loaded = characterFromFs1(FURY, file, classRows, raceRows, source, 'Thrallgar');
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error(loaded.message);
    const order = loaded.character.point_order;
    const character: SimCharacter = {
      name: 'Thrallgar',
      spec: 'warrior-fury',
      class_slug: 'warrior',
      race_slug: 'orc',
      level: 33,
      tree_version: BUILD,
      point_order: order,
      gear: { head: 12640, main_hand: 11726 },
      buffs: [16609],
      consumables: [11405],
      source,
    };

    const draft = toBuildDraft(character, classRows, raceRows);
    expect(draft).not.toBeNull();
    expect(draft).toEqual({
      class_id: 1,
      race_id: 2,
      tree_version: BUILD,
      point_order: order,
      gear: { head: 12640, main_hand: 11726 },
    });

    const back = fromBuildDraft(draft!, file, classRows, raceRows, {
      name: 'Thrallgar',
      buffs: [16609],
      consumables: [11405],
      source,
    });
    expect(back).toEqual({ ...character, level: 33 });
  });

  it('refuses a draft whose talent file is for another class', async () => {
    const [file, classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([warriorTalents(), classes(), races()]);
    const draft = { class_id: 3, race_id: 2, tree_version: BUILD, point_order: [], gear: {} };
    expect(fromBuildDraft(draft, file, classRows, raceRows, { source })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refuses a character whose race the reference data does not know', async () => {
    const [classRows, raceRows] = await Promise.all([classes(), races()]);
    const character: SimCharacter = {
      name: 'Nobody',
      spec: 'warrior-fury',
      class_slug: 'warrior',
      race_slug: 'skyborne-unknown',
      level: 60,
      tree_version: BUILD,
      point_order: [],
      gear: {},
      buffs: [],
      consumables: [],
      source,
    };
    expect(toBuildDraft(character, classRows, raceRows)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the character model**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/character.ts`:

```ts
import { pointsPerTree } from '../planner/derive';
import { decodeFS1, orderFromRanks } from '../planner/fs1';
import { indexTalents, type TalentIndex } from '../planner/rules';
import type { BuildDraft, ClassRow, Gear, RaceRow, TalentFile } from '../planner/types';
import { BASE_LEVEL, FIRST_POINT_LEVEL } from '../planner/types';
import { SPECS } from './specs';
import type { CharacterSource } from './types';

export interface SimCharacter {
  name: string;
  spec: string;
  class_slug: string;
  race_slug: string;
  level: number;
  tree_version: string;
  point_order: number[];
  gear: Gear;
  buffs: number[];
  consumables: number[];
  source: CharacterSource;
}

export const MAX_LEVEL = 60;

export function specForSplit(classSlug: string, split: readonly number[]): string {
  let best = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < split.length; i += 1) {
    if (split[i] > split[best]) best = i;
  }
  const row = SPECS.find((entry) => entry.class_slug === classSlug && entry.tree_index === best);
  return row?.spec ?? `${classSlug}-${best}`;
}

export function specOf(index: TalentIndex, order: number[]): string {
  return specForSplit(index.file.class_slug, pointsPerTree(index, order));
}

export function levelFor(order: number[]): number {
  return order.length === 0 ? BASE_LEVEL : Math.min(MAX_LEVEL, BASE_LEVEL + order.length);
}

export function toBuildDraft(
  character: SimCharacter,
  classes: readonly ClassRow[],
  races: readonly RaceRow[],
): BuildDraft | null {
  const classRow = classes.find((row) => row.slug === character.class_slug);
  const raceRow = races.find((row) => row.slug === character.race_slug);
  if (classRow === undefined || raceRow === undefined) return null;
  return {
    class_id: classRow.id,
    race_id: raceRow.id,
    tree_version: character.tree_version,
    point_order: [...character.point_order],
    gear: { ...character.gear },
  };
}

export interface CharacterExtras {
  name?: string;
  buffs?: number[];
  consumables?: number[];
  source: CharacterSource;
}

export function fromBuildDraft(
  draft: BuildDraft,
  talents: TalentFile,
  classes: readonly ClassRow[],
  races: readonly RaceRow[],
  extras: CharacterExtras,
): SimCharacter | null {
  const classRow = classes.find((row) => row.id === draft.class_id);
  const raceRow = races.find((row) => row.id === draft.race_id);
  if (classRow === undefined || raceRow === undefined) return null;
  if (talents.class_slug !== classRow.slug) return null;
  const index = indexTalents(talents);
  return {
    name: extras.name ?? classRow.name,
    spec: specOf(index, draft.point_order),
    class_slug: classRow.slug,
    race_slug: raceRow.slug,
    level: levelFor(draft.point_order),
    tree_version: draft.tree_version,
    point_order: [...draft.point_order],
    gear: { ...(draft.gear ?? {}) },
    buffs: [...(extras.buffs ?? [])],
    consumables: [...(extras.consumables ?? [])],
    source: extras.source,
  };
}

export function plannerHrefFor(character: SimCharacter): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ class: character.class_slug, race: character.race_slug });
  return `/planner?${params.toString()}`;
}

export const FIRST_TALENT_LEVEL = FIRST_POINT_LEVEL;

export const CLASS_MISMATCH = (exported: string, loaded: string): string =>
  `That export is a ${exported}; these are ${loaded} talents.`;

export const UNREACHABLE_TALENTS = (names: string): string =>
  `That export is not a legal build: ${names} cannot be reached.`;

export type CharacterResult = { ok: true; character: SimCharacter } | { ok: false; message: string };

/**
 * An addon export into a character. Decoding and the rank-to-order reconstruction are the
 * planner's (lib/planner/fs1.ts): the addon writes one string and both pillars read it with
 * the same code, so a format change cannot reach one and miss the other.
 */
export function characterFromFs1(
  code: string,
  talents: TalentFile,
  classes: readonly ClassRow[],
  races: readonly RaceRow[],
  source: CharacterSource,
  name?: string,
): CharacterResult {
  const decoded = decodeFS1(code);
  if (!decoded.ok) return { ok: false, message: decoded.message };
  if (decoded.build.classSlug !== talents.class_slug) {
    return { ok: false, message: CLASS_MISMATCH(decoded.build.classSlug, talents.class_slug) };
  }

  const index = indexTalents(talents);
  const { order, dropped } = orderFromRanks(index, decoded.build.treeRanks);
  if (dropped.length > 0) {
    const names = dropped.map((id) => index.byId.get(id)?.name ?? String(id)).join(', ');
    return { ok: false, message: UNREACHABLE_TALENTS(names) };
  }

  const classRow = classes.find((row) => row.slug === decoded.build.classSlug);
  const raceRow =
    races.find((row) => row.slug === decoded.build.raceSlug) ?? races.find((row) => row.slug !== undefined);
  if (classRow === undefined || raceRow === undefined) {
    return { ok: false, message: CLASS_MISMATCH(decoded.build.classSlug, talents.class_slug) };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    character: {
      name: name ?? classRow.name,
      spec: specOf(index, order),
      class_slug: classRow.slug,
      race_slug: raceRow.slug,
      level: levelFor(order),
      tree_version: decoded.build.dataBuild,
      point_order: order,
      gear: { ...decoded.build.gear },
      buffs: [],
      consumables: [],
      source,
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  BUFF_PRESETS,
  DURATIONS,
  MAX_DURATION_SEC,
  MAX_TARGETS,
  MIN_DURATION_SEC,
  defaultSettings,
  durationLabel,
  executePhaseOn,
  settingsLabel,
  withDuration,
  withExecutePhase,
  withPreset,
  withTargets,
} from './settings';

describe('defaultSettings', () => {
  it('is the contract\'s EncounterSpec defaults, raid-buffed', () => {
    const settings = defaultSettings();
    expect(settings.encounter).toEqual({
      duration_sec: 180,
      variation: 0.2,
      targets: 1,
      execute_ratio: 0.25,
      profile: '',
    });
    expect(settings.preset).toBe('raid-buffed');
    expect(settings.buffs.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

describe('DURATIONS and durationLabel', () => {
  it('runs from one to eight minutes in thirty-second steps', () => {
    expect(DURATIONS[0]).toBe(MIN_DURATION_SEC);
    expect(DURATIONS.at(-1)).toBe(MAX_DURATION_SEC);
    expect(DURATIONS).toContain(180);
    expect(DURATIONS.every((seconds, i) => i === 0 || seconds - DURATIONS[i - 1] === 30)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('reads as a clock, not as seconds', () => {
    expect(durationLabel(60)).toBe('1:00');
    expect(durationLabel(180)).toBe('3:00');
    expect(durationLabel(210)).toBe('3:30');
    expect(durationLabel(480)).toBe('8:00');
  });
});

describe('the setters never mutate and always clamp', () => {
  it('keeps duration inside one to eight minutes', () => {
    const base = defaultSettings();
    expect(withDuration(base, 30).encounter.duration_sec).toBe(MIN_DURATION_SEC);
    expect(withDuration(base, 9999).encounter.duration_sec).toBe(MAX_DURATION_SEC);
    expect(base.encounter.duration_sec).toBe(180);
  });

  it('keeps targets between one and ten', () => {
    const base = defaultSettings();
    expect(withTargets(base, 0).encounter.targets).toBe(1);
    expect(withTargets(base, 99).encounter.targets).toBe(MAX_TARGETS);
  });

  it('turns the execute phase off by zeroing the ratio, and back on to the default', () => {
    const base = defaultSettings();
    const off = withExecutePhase(base, false);
    expect(off.encounter.execute_ratio).toBe(0);
    expect(executePhaseOn(off)).toBe(false);
    expect(executePhaseOn(withExecutePhase(off, true))).toBe(true);
    expect(withExecutePhase(off, true).encounter.execute_ratio).toBe(0.25);
  });

  it('swaps the whole buff and consumable list with the preset, and leaves it alone for custom', () => {
    const base = defaultSettings();
    const solo = withPreset(base, 'solo');
    expect(solo.buffs).toEqual([]);
    expect(solo.consumables).toEqual([]);
    const custom = withPreset(base, 'custom');
    expect(custom.buffs).toEqual(base.buffs);
    expect(custom.preset).toBe('custom');
  });

  it('offers exactly the three presets the design names', () => {
    expect(BUFF_PRESETS.map((row) => row.id)).toEqual(['raid-buffed', 'solo', 'custom']);
  });
});

describe('settingsLabel', () => {
  it('is the one line a saved sim is titled with', () => {
    expect(settingsLabel(defaultSettings())).toBe('Raid-buffed, 3:00, single target');
    expect(settingsLabel(withTargets(withPreset(defaultSettings(), 'solo'), 4))).toBe(
      'Solo, 3:00, 4 targets',
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
// durationLabel and encounterLabel live in encounter.ts, not here: the Worker's unfurl copy
// needs them too and it is built by a group that runs in parallel with this one. They are
// re-exported so every caller has one import for "the settings vocabulary".
import { durationLabel, encounterLabel } from './encounter';
import { DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER, type EncounterSpec } from './types';

export { durationLabel, encounterLabel };

export const MIN_DURATION_SEC = 60;
export const MAX_DURATION_SEC = 480;
export const DURATION_STEP_SEC = 30;
export const MAX_TARGETS = 10;

export const DURATIONS: readonly number[] = Array.from(
  { length: (MAX_DURATION_SEC - MIN_DURATION_SEC) / DURATION_STEP_SEC + 1 },
  (_, i) => MIN_DURATION_SEC + i * DURATION_STEP_SEC,
);

export const BUFF_PRESETS = [
  { id: 'raid-buffed', label: 'Raid-buffed' },
  { id: 'solo', label: 'Solo' },
  { id: 'custom', label: 'Custom' },
] as const;
export type BuffPresetId = (typeof BUFF_PRESETS)[number]['id'];

export const PRESET_BUFFS: Record<Exclude<BuffPresetId, 'custom'>, number[]> = {
  'raid-buffed': [16609, 25289, 10938, 20217, 24425],
  solo: [],
};

export const PRESET_CONSUMABLES: Record<Exclude<BuffPresetId, 'custom'>, number[]> = {
  'raid-buffed': [11405, 17539, 11474],
  solo: [],
};

export interface SimSettings {
  encounter: EncounterSpec;
  preset: BuffPresetId;
  buffs: number[];
  consumables: number[];
}

export function defaultSettings(): SimSettings {
  return {
    encounter: { ...DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER },
    preset: 'raid-buffed',
    buffs: [...PRESET_BUFFS['raid-buffed']],
    consumables: [...PRESET_CONSUMABLES['raid-buffed']],
  };
}

export function withPreset(settings: SimSettings, preset: BuffPresetId): SimSettings {
  if (preset === 'custom') return { ...settings, preset };
  return {
    ...settings,
    preset,
    buffs: [...PRESET_BUFFS[preset]],
    consumables: [...PRESET_CONSUMABLES[preset]],
  };
}

export function withDuration(settings: SimSettings, seconds: number): SimSettings {
  const clamped = Math.min(MAX_DURATION_SEC, Math.max(MIN_DURATION_SEC, Math.round(seconds)));
  return { ...settings, encounter: { ...settings.encounter, duration_sec: clamped } };
}

export function withTargets(settings: SimSettings, targets: number): SimSettings {
  const clamped = Math.min(MAX_TARGETS, Math.max(1, Math.round(targets)));
  return { ...settings, encounter: { ...settings.encounter, targets: clamped } };
}

export function withExecutePhase(settings: SimSettings, on: boolean): SimSettings {
  return {
    ...settings,
    encounter: { ...settings.encounter, execute_ratio: on ? DEFAULT_ENCOUNTER.execute_ratio : 0 },
  };
}

export function executePhaseOn(settings: SimSettings): boolean {
  return settings.encounter.execute_ratio > 0;
}

export function settingsLabel(settings: SimSettings): string {
  const preset = BUFF_PRESETS.find((row) => row.id === settings.preset)?.label ?? 'Custom';
  const targets =
    settings.encounter.targets === 1 ? 'single target' : `${settings.encounter.targets} targets`;
  return `${preset}, ${durationLabel(settings.encounter.duration_sec)}, ${targets}`;
}
```

## Task 7: The character sources

A stored character (whatever the API's newest source for it is), an addon export, a planner build, and a logged fight — one function each, one `SimCharacter` out of all of them, and the pill that says where it came from.

### Armory is a launch limitation, and the page says so

The contract's `sim-input` row is explicit: **Armory is not a source yet.** Nothing in the repository stores an Armory refresh, so the API answers from the newest addon export or the last ranked fight and returns `source` as `"addon"` or `"fight"`; `"armory"` appears when Blizzard's profile API for Forever exists, with no change to the shape.

Two consequences this task is written around, and neither is a workaround to be tidied away later:

1. `fromStoredCharacter` never invents a source. It takes the `source` the API returned and puts that in the character, so the pill says "Addon export, 2 hours ago" for a character the API filled from an addon export — which is the truth — and will say "Armory, 2 hours ago" the day the API starts saying `"armory"`, with no code change here.
2. The source switcher (Task 11) keeps an **Armory** card, and it is a sign-in prompt, not a loader. Its copy says what actually happens: signing in finds the player's characters, and each one's gear comes from their last addon export or last logged fight until the Armory exists. Removing the card would be a worse lie than showing it — a player looking for Armory needs to be told where their gear is coming from instead.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/sources.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/sources.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/test-support/msw.ts`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimCharacter`, `characterFromFs1`, `fromBuildDraft`, `specForSplit`, `levelFor` (Task 6); `fetchSimInput` (Task 2); `CharacterPath`, `parseCharacterPath` from `web/src/lib/characters.ts`; `loadTalents`, `loadReference` from `web/src/lib/planner/load.ts`; `fetchReportMeta`, `fetchSummary` from `web/src/lib/report/load.ts`; `gearFromCombatant`, `treeRanksFromTalents`, `GEAR_SLOT_ORDER` from `web/src/lib/report/planner-link.ts`; `classSlugFromName` from `web/src/lib/report/tree-sizes.ts`; `BuildRecord` from `web/src/lib/planner/types.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/sources.ts`: `interface LoadContext { treeVersion: string; apiBase?: string }`, `type SourceResult = { ok: true; character: SimCharacter } | { ok: false; message: string }`, `function fromStoredCharacter(path: CharacterPath, ctx): Promise<SourceResult>`, `function fromAddonExport(code, ctx): Promise<SourceResult>`, `function fromPlannerBuild(buildId, ctx): Promise<SourceResult>`, `function fromLoggedFight(ref, ctx): Promise<SourceResult>`, `function parseFightRef(ref): { reportId: string; fightIndex: number } | null`, `function sourcePill(source, now?): string`, `function relativeTime(iso, now?): string`.
  - Task 11 calls all of them from the source switcher; Task 16 calls `fromLoggedFight`; Task 18 calls `fromStoredCharacter` once per character in the landing state.

### Why no dependency injection here

Every fetch this module makes is either our API (MSW answers it) or a `/data/<build>/…` file (MSW answers that too). Injecting loaders would add a parameter object to four call sites to buy nothing a handler does not already buy, and it would let a component pass a different loader than the page does. The functions import the real loaders; the test adds two handlers.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the data-file and build handlers to MSW**

In `web/src/test-support/msw.ts`, add these three handlers to `simHandlers`, above the existing ones. `readFile` is used rather than an import so the handler serves the same bytes the browser would.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const WEB_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..');
const FIXTURE_BUILD = '1.15.9.69722';

/** The planner's own data files, served from public/ the way the site serves them. */
function dataFile(relative: string): HttpResponse {
  try {
    return HttpResponse.text(
      readFileSync(path.join(WEB_ROOT, 'public/data', FIXTURE_BUILD, relative), 'utf8'),
      { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } },
    );
  } catch {
    return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 404 });
  }
}

export const FIXTURE_BUILD_ID = 'bld123456789';

export const dataHandlers = [
  http.get('*/data/:build/talents/:file', ({ params }) => dataFile(`talents/${params.file}`)),
  http.get('*/data/:build/:file', ({ params }) => dataFile(String(params.file))),
  http.get(`${API}/v1/builds/:id`, async ({ request, params }) => {
    await record(request);
    if (params.id !== FIXTURE_BUILD_ID) return failure('no such build', 404);
    return envelope({
      id: FIXTURE_BUILD_ID,
      class_id: 1,
      race_id: 2,
      tree_version: FIXTURE_BUILD,
      point_order: [2001, 2001, 2001, 2001, 2001, 2002, 2002, 2002, 2002, 2002, 2003, 2003, 2003],
      gear: { head: 12640 },
      title: 'Fury opener',
      created_at: '2026-09-13T20:00:00Z',
      views: 4,
    });
  }),
];
```

and change the server construction to include them:

```ts
const base = setupServer(...simHandlers, ...dataHandlers);
```

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { HttpResponse, http } from 'msw';
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { FIXTURE_BUILD_ID, server } from '../../test-support/msw';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import type { CharacterPath } from '../characters';
import {
  fromAddonExport,
  fromLoggedFight,
  fromPlannerBuild,
  fromStoredCharacter,
  parseFightRef,
  relativeTime,
  sourcePill,
} from './sources';

const ctx = { treeVersion: '1.15.9.69722', apiBase: 'https://api.test' };
const FURY = 'FS1:1.15.9.69722:warrior:orc:0/5530515/0:head=12640,main_hand=11726';

beforeAll(() => server.listen({ onUnhandledRequest: 'error' }));
afterEach(() => server.resetHandlers());
afterAll(() => server.close());

describe('fromStoredCharacter', () => {
  const path: CharacterPath = { region: 'us', ruleset: 'normal', slug: 'thrallgar' };

  it('builds a character from the newest model the API holds', async () => {
    const result = await fromStoredCharacter(path, ctx);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(result.character.name).toBe('thrallgar');
    expect(result.character.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(result.character.class_slug).toBe('warrior');
    expect(result.character.gear.head).toBe(12640);
  });

  it('records the source the API actually had, never "armory" by assumption', async () => {
    const result = await fromStoredCharacter(path, ctx);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.message);
    expect(result.character.source).toEqual({
      kind: 'addon',
      ref: 'us/normal/thrallgar',
      captured_at: '2026-09-14T09:40:00Z',
    });
  });

  it('carries "armory" through unchanged the day the API starts sending it', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get('https://api.test/v1/characters/:region/:ruleset/:name/sim-input', () =>
        HttpResponse.json({
          ok: true,
          data: {
            spec: 'warrior-fury',
            gear: { head: 12640 },
            talents: [2001],
            buffs: [],
            captured_at: '2026-09-14T09:40:00Z',
            source: 'armory',
          },
          error: null,
          request_id: 'r',
        }),
      ),
    );
    const result = await fromStoredCharacter(path, ctx);
    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.message);
    expect(result.character.source.kind).toBe('armory');
  });
});

describe('fromAddonExport', () => {
  it('builds a character from the addon string, marked as just captured', async () => {
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-09-14T12:00:00Z'));
    const result = await fromAddonExport(FURY, ctx);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(result.character.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(result.character.source.kind).toBe('addon');
    expect(result.character.source.captured_at).toBe('2026-09-14T12:00:00.000Z');
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('passes the decoder’s reason through unchanged', async () => {
    const result = await fromAddonExport('FS2:1:warrior:orc:0/0/0:', ctx);
    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, message: 'That code is FS2; this site reads FS1.' });
  });
});

describe('fromPlannerBuild', () => {
  it('builds a character from a saved build, keeping its point order', async () => {
    const result = await fromPlannerBuild(FIXTURE_BUILD_ID, ctx);
    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    expect(result.character.point_order).toHaveLength(13);
    expect(result.character.source).toEqual({
      kind: 'build',
      ref: FIXTURE_BUILD_ID,
      captured_at: '2026-09-13T20:00:00Z',
    });
  });

  it('says so when the link points at nothing', async () => {
    expect(await fromPlannerBuild('zzzzzzzzzzzz', ctx)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      message: simCopy.buildNotFound,
    });
  });
});

describe('parseFightRef', () => {
  it('reads <report_id>:<fight_index>', () => {
    expect(parseFightRef('fixture2abcd:2')).toEqual({ reportId: 'fixture2abcd', fightIndex: 2 });
  });

  it('refuses anything else', () => {
    expect(parseFightRef('fixture2abcd')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFightRef('FIXTURE2ABCD:2')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFightRef('fixture2abcd:-1')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseFightRef('fixture2abcd:x')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('fromLoggedFight', () => {
  it('refuses a malformed reference before it asks the API for anything', async () => {
    expect(await fromLoggedFight('nonsense', ctx)).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      message: simCopy.fightRefInvalid,
    });
  });
});

describe('relativeTime', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-09-14T12:00:00Z');

  it('reads the way a person would say it', () => {
    expect(relativeTime('2026-09-14T11:59:30Z', now)).toBe('just now');
    expect(relativeTime('2026-09-14T11:40:00Z', now)).toBe('20 minutes ago');
    expect(relativeTime('2026-09-14T11:00:00Z', now)).toBe('1 hour ago');
    expect(relativeTime('2026-09-14T10:00:00Z', now)).toBe('2 hours ago');
    expect(relativeTime('2026-09-12T12:00:00Z', now)).toBe('2 days ago');
  });

  it('does not invent a time it cannot read', () => {
    expect(relativeTime('', now)).toBe('at an unknown time');
    expect(relativeTime('not a date', now)).toBe('at an unknown time');
  });
});

describe('sourcePill', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-09-14T12:00:00Z');

  it('says where the character came from, and when it matters', () => {
    expect(sourcePill({ kind: 'armory', ref: 'us/forever/x', captured_at: '2026-09-14T10:00:00Z' }, now)).toBe(
      'Armory, 2 hours ago',
    );
    expect(sourcePill({ kind: 'addon', ref: '', captured_at: '2026-09-14T11:59:50Z' }, now)).toBe(
      'Addon export, just now',
    );
    expect(sourcePill({ kind: 'build', ref: 'bld1', captured_at: '2026-09-13T20:00:00Z' }, now)).toBe(
      'Build from planner',
    );
    expect(sourcePill({ kind: 'fight', ref: 'abc:2', captured_at: '2026-09-13T20:00:00Z' }, now)).toBe(
      'From this fight',
    );
    expect(sourcePill({ kind: 'manual', ref: '', captured_at: '2026-09-13T20:00:00Z' }, now)).toBe(
      'Entered by hand',
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the module**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/sources.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/sources.ts
// The four ways a character reaches the simulator, and the pill that says which. Every one
// of them ends in a SimCharacter, so nothing downstream -- the strip, the request builder,
// the planner link -- knows or cares where it came from.
//
//   stored   GET /v1/characters/<region>/<ruleset>/<name>/sim-input -- whatever the API's
//            newest source for that character is. Armory is not one of them yet (simulator
//            contract, sim-input row): today the API answers from the newest addon export or
//            the last ranked fight and says which, so this function never names the source
//            itself -- it copies the one the API returned. The day Armory lands, the pill
//            starts saying "Armory" with no change here.
//   addon    an FS1 string, pasted or pushed by the companion
//   build    GET /v1/builds/<id>, the planner's own record
//   fight    <report_id>:<fight_index>, read out of the fight's COMBATANT_INFO row
//
// Each returns a result rather than throwing: every one of these is something a player
// typed or pasted, and the page shows the reason inline beside the field.
import { API_BASE_URL } from '../planner/config';
import { loadReference, loadTalents } from '../planner/load';
import type { BuildRecord } from '../planner/types';
import { fetchReportMeta, fetchSummary } from '../report/load';
import { gearFromCombatant, treeRanksFromTalents } from '../report/planner-link';
import { classSlugFromName } from '../report/tree-sizes';
import { requestEnvelope } from '../account/api';
import type { CharacterPath } from '../characters';
import { characterFromFs1, fromBuildDraft, levelFor, specForSplit, type SimCharacter } from './character';
import { fetchSimInput } from './api';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import type { CharacterSource } from './types';

export interface LoadContext {
  /** The active data build; every /data/<build>/ fetch and every character carries it. */
  treeVersion: string;
  apiBase?: string;
}

export type SourceResult = { ok: true; character: SimCharacter } | { ok: false; message: string };

const FIGHT_REF = /^([a-z2-7]{12}):(\d{1,6})$/;

export function parseFightRef(ref: string): { reportId: string; fightIndex: number } | null {
  const match = FIGHT_REF.exec(ref.trim());
  return match === null ? null : { reportId: match[1], fightIndex: Number.parseInt(match[2], 10) };
}

const MINUTE = 60_000;
const HOUR = 60 * MINUTE;
const DAY = 24 * HOUR;

function plural(count: number, one: string): string {
  return `${count} ${count === 1 ? one : `${one}s`} ago`;
}

export function relativeTime(iso: string, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const at = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(at.getTime())) return 'at an unknown time';
  const elapsed = Math.max(0, now.getTime() - at.getTime());
  if (elapsed < MINUTE) return 'just now';
  if (elapsed < HOUR) return plural(Math.floor(elapsed / MINUTE), 'minute');
  if (elapsed < DAY) return plural(Math.floor(elapsed / HOUR), 'hour');
  return plural(Math.floor(elapsed / DAY), 'day');
}

/**
 * The strip's source pill. Armory and addon carry a time because freshness is the question
 * a player has about them; a build and a fight are fixed things, so naming them is enough.
 */
export function sourcePill(source: CharacterSource, now: Date = new Date()): string {
  switch (source.kind) {
    case 'armory':
      return `Armory, ${relativeTime(source.captured_at, now)}`;
    case 'addon':
      return `Addon export, ${relativeTime(source.captured_at, now)}`;
    case 'build':
      return 'Build from planner';
    case 'fight':
      return 'From this fight';
    default:
      return 'Entered by hand';
  }
}

async function reference(ctx: LoadContext) {
  return loadReference(ctx.treeVersion);
}

export async function fromStoredCharacter(
  path: CharacterPath,
  ctx: LoadContext,
): Promise<SourceResult> {
  let input;
  try {
    input = await fetchSimInput(path, ctx.apiBase ?? API_BASE_URL);
  } catch (error) {
    return { ok: false, message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : simCopy.characterFailed };
  }
  const characterKey = `${path.region}/${path.ruleset}/${path.slug}`;

  const classSlug = input.spec.split('-')[0];
  try {
    const [talents, { classes, races }] = await Promise.all([
      loadTalents(ctx.treeVersion, classSlug),
      reference(ctx),
    ]);
    const classRow = classes.find((row) => row.slug === classSlug);
    if (classRow === undefined) return { ok: false, message: simCopy.characterFailed };
    const raceRow = races[0];
    const name = path.slug;
    return {
      ok: true,
      character: {
        name,
        spec: input.spec,
        class_slug: classSlug,
        race_slug: raceRow.slug,
        level: levelFor(input.talents),
        tree_version: ctx.treeVersion,
        point_order: [...input.talents],
        gear: { ...input.gear },
        buffs: [...input.buffs],
        consumables: [],
        // The API's own word for where this came from -- never a guess, so the pill is
        // true today and true unchanged when Armory lands.
        source: { kind: input.source, ref: characterKey, captured_at: input.captured_at },
      },
    };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: simCopy.characterFailed };
  }
}

export async function fromAddonExport(code: string, ctx: LoadContext): Promise<SourceResult> {
  // The class is in the string, so the talent file is chosen from it rather than guessed.
  const classSlug = code.trim().split(':')[2] ?? '';
  let talents;
  let classes;
  let races;
  try {
    [talents, { classes, races }] = await Promise.all([
      loadTalents(ctx.treeVersion, classSlug),
      reference(ctx),
    ]);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: simCopy.characterFailed };
  }
  return characterFromFs1(code, talents, classes, races, {
    kind: 'addon',
    ref: '',
    captured_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  });
}

export async function fromPlannerBuild(buildId: string, ctx: LoadContext): Promise<SourceResult> {
  let record: BuildRecord | null;
  try {
    ({ data: record } = await requestEnvelope<BuildRecord>(
      `/v1/builds/${buildId}`,
      ctx.apiBase ?? API_BASE_URL,
      { credentials: 'omit', failureMessage: simCopy.buildNotFound },
    ));
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: simCopy.buildNotFound };
  }
  if (record === null) return { ok: false, message: simCopy.buildNotFound };

  try {
    const { classes, races } = await reference(ctx);
    const classRow = classes.find((row) => row.id === record.class_id);
    if (classRow === undefined) return { ok: false, message: simCopy.buildNotFound };
    const talents = await loadTalents(record.tree_version, classRow.slug);
    const character = fromBuildDraft(record, talents, classes, races, {
      name: record.title === undefined || record.title === '' ? classRow.name : record.title,
      source: { kind: 'build', ref: record.id, captured_at: record.created_at },
    });
    return character === null ? { ok: false, message: simCopy.buildNotFound } : { ok: true, character };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: simCopy.buildNotFound };
  }
}

/**
 * A logged fight. The gear comes from the fight's COMBATANT_INFO row through the report
 * lane's own gearFromCombatant, so the sim and the planner read a fight the same way.
 * Talents are only usable when the log recorded one rank per talent in tab order; when it
 * did not, the character still loads with its gear and the page says the tree is empty.
 */
export async function fromLoggedFight(ref: string, ctx: LoadContext): Promise<SourceResult> {
  const parsed = parseFightRef(ref);
  if (parsed === null) return { ok: false, message: simCopy.fightRefInvalid };

  try {
    const meta = await fetchReportMeta(parsed.reportId, ctx.apiBase ?? API_BASE_URL);
    const summary = await fetchSummary(meta.data_base_url, parsed.fightIndex);
    const roster = summary.roster.find((row) => row.class !== undefined && row.role === 'dps');
    const combatant = summary.combatants.find((row) => row.guid === roster?.guid);
    if (roster === undefined || combatant === undefined) {
      return { ok: false, message: simCopy.fightNoCombatant };
    }

    // `find` filters on `class !== undefined` but TypeScript does not narrow through it.
    const classSlug = classSlugFromName(roster.class ?? '');
    const [talents, { classes, races }] = await Promise.all([
      loadTalents(ctx.treeVersion, classSlug),
      reference(ctx),
    ]);
    const treeSizes = talents.trees.map((tree) => tree.talents.length);
    const treeRanks = treeRanksFromTalents(combatant.talents, treeSizes);
    const split = treeRanks === null ? treeSizes.map(() => 0) : treeRanks.map((tree) => tree.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0));
    const order =
      treeRanks === null
        ? []
        : characterFromFs1(
            `FS1:${ctx.treeVersion}:${classSlug}:${races[0].slug}:${treeRanks
              .map((tree) => tree.map((rank) => rank.toString(36)).join('').replace(/0+$/, '') || '0')
              .join('/')}:`,
            talents,
            classes,
            races,
            { kind: 'fight', ref, captured_at: meta.created_at },
          );
    const point_order = Array.isArray(order) ? [] : order.ok ? order.character.point_order : [];

    return {
      ok: true,
      character: {
        name: roster.name,
        spec: specForSplit(classSlug, split),
        class_slug: classSlug,
        race_slug: races[0].slug,
        level: 60,
        tree_version: ctx.treeVersion,
        point_order,
        gear: gearFromCombatant(combatant.gear),
        buffs: combatant.raid_buffs.map((buff) => buff.spell_id),
        consumables: combatant.consumables.map((buff) => buff.spell_id),
        source: { kind: 'fight', ref, captured_at: meta.created_at },
      },
    };
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, message: simCopy.fightNoCombatant };
  }
}
```

# Group C — the routes, the island and the Worker

Tasks 8 and 9, in order. Independent of groups A, B and E. It depends only on Task 1 and Task 2.

## Task 8: `/sim`, `/sim/specs`, `/sim/<sim_id>` and the `sim-island` entry

Three static Astro shells and one standalone Svelte island, built and size-budgeted like `planner-island` and `report-island`. The island mounted here is a placeholder that renders the page frame and its empty state; Group D fills it in. That is deliberate: the routes, the build wiring, the budget and the Worker have to be provably in place before a component depends on them.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/pages/sim.astro`, `web/src/pages/sim/specs.astro`, `web/src/pages/sim/[id].astro`
- Create: `web/src/sim-island.ts`, `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`
- Create: `web/vite.sim-island.config.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/url.ts`
- Test: `web/src/pages/_sim.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/sim/url.test.ts`, `web/src/sim-island.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/vite.island.config.ts`, `web/package.json`, `web/scripts/check-island-size.mjs`, `web/src/components/Header.astro`, `web/wrangler.jsonc`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Base.astro`; `islandConfig` from `web/vite.island.config.ts`; `simCopy` (Task 2); `ENGINE_VERSION` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/url.ts`: `interface SimState { source: SourceKind | ''; ref: string; mode: 'sim' | 'compare'; fight: string }`, `function defaultSimState(): SimState`, `function parseSimState(search: string): SimState`, `function simSearch(state: SimState): string`, `function withSimState(state, patch): SimState`, `const SIM_ID_PATTERN: RegExp`, `function simIdFrom(pathname: string): string`.
  - `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`: `let { simId = '', inlineResult = null }: { simId?: string; inlineResult?: SimResult | null } = $props();`
  - `web/src/sim-island.ts`: the entry; mounts `SimView` on `#sim`.
  - Task 9 rewrites the shell's head; Tasks 11–19 replace `SimView`'s body.

### The page, decided

`/sim` is one column inside the site's `max-w-[1344px]` main, sections stacked with `gap-[22px] md:gap-8`, in this order and no other:

1. **Heading row** — `<h1 class="section-title">Simulator</h1>` on the left, and on the right a muted mono `Engine 7779ebb` that links to `/sim/specs`. On phone the engine label wraps under the heading.
2. **Character strip** (Task 11).
3. **Settings bar** (Task 12).
4. **Run control** (Task 13).
5. **The sentence** — one `<p>` at `text-[15px] text-strong`, `role="status"`, appearing only once a result exists.
6. **Results** (Task 14).
7. **Actions** — `Save this sim` and `Compare with a fight`, a row of secondary buttons.

Before a character is loaded, sections 2 to 7 are replaced by the **empty state**: the heading row, one line of muted copy, and the four source cards (Task 11). Nothing else. There is no hero, no marketing line, and no "get started".

`/sim/specs` is the heading row, one paragraph of standing copy, and a responsive grid of spec cards (Task 15). `/sim/<sim_id>` is the same frame with the run control replaced by a read-only result header (Task 17).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { defaultSimState, parseSimState, simIdFrom, simSearch, withSimState } from './url';

describe('parseSimState', () => {
  it('is the default for an empty query', () => {
    expect(parseSimState('')).toEqual(defaultSimState());
    expect(defaultSimState()).toEqual({ source: '', ref: '', mode: 'sim', fight: '' });
  });

  it('reads every parameter the page writes', () => {
    expect(parseSimState('?source=fight&ref=fixture2abcd%3A2&mode=compare&fight=fixture2abcd%3A3')).toEqual({
      source: 'fight',
      ref: 'fixture2abcd:2',
      mode: 'compare',
      fight: 'fixture2abcd:3',
    });
  });

  it('drops a source or mode it does not recognise rather than trusting the URL', () => {
    expect(parseSimState('?source=rumour&mode=hack').source).toBe('');
    expect(parseSimState('?source=rumour&mode=hack').mode).toBe('sim');
  });

  it('caps a ref at a length no real id reaches, since the query is attacker-controlled', () => {
    expect(parseSimState(`?ref=${'x'.repeat(5000)}`).ref).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe('simSearch', () => {
  it('writes only what differs from the default, so a plain /sim stays clean', () => {
    expect(simSearch(defaultSimState())).toBe('');
    expect(simSearch({ ...defaultSimState(), source: 'build', ref: 'bld1' })).toBe('?source=build&ref=bld1');
  });

  it('round-trips every state it writes', () => {
    const state = { source: 'fight' as const, ref: 'fixture2abcd:2', mode: 'compare' as const, fight: 'fixture2abcd:3' };
    expect(parseSimState(simSearch(state))).toEqual(state);
  });
});

describe('withSimState', () => {
  it('returns a new state and never mutates the old one', () => {
    const base = defaultSimState();
    const next = withSimState(base, { mode: 'compare' });
    expect(next.mode).toBe('compare');
    expect(base.mode).toBe('sim');
  });
});

describe('simIdFrom', () => {
  it('reads the twelve base32 characters out of /sim/<id>', () => {
    expect(simIdFrom('/sim/simfixture01')).toBe('simfixture01');
    expect(simIdFrom('/sim/simfixture01/')).toBe('simfixture01');
  });

  it('is empty for /sim, /sim/specs and anything else', () => {
    expect(simIdFrom('/sim')).toBe('');
    expect(simIdFrom('/sim/specs')).toBe('');
    expect(simIdFrom('/sim/TOOSHORT')).toBe('');
    expect(simIdFrom('/reports/fixture2abcd')).toBe('');
  });
});
```

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/url.ts
// /sim's URL state. The page is static and the island reads the query, exactly as the
// planner reads ?class= and the report reads ?fight=, so a link to "sim this fight" or
// "sim this build" is a plain URL a player can paste.
//
// Everything here is validated against a fixed vocabulary: the query is attacker-controlled
// and the values reach fetch() paths.
import type { SourceKind } from './types';

const SOURCES: readonly SourceKind[] = ['armory', 'addon', 'build', 'fight', 'manual'];
const MODES = ['sim', 'compare'] as const;
export type SimMode = (typeof MODES)[number];

/** A build id is 12, a character key about 40, a fight ref 14. 128 is generous and finite. */
const MAX_REF = 128;

export interface SimState {
  source: SourceKind | '';
  ref: string;
  mode: SimMode;
  /** The fight compare mode is comparing against: "<report_id>:<fight_index>". */
  fight: string;
}

export function defaultSimState(): SimState {
  return { source: '', ref: '', mode: 'sim', fight: '' };
}

function bounded(value: string | null): string {
  if (value === null || value.length > MAX_REF) return '';
  return value;
}

export function parseSimState(search: string): SimState {
  const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
  const source = params.get('source');
  const mode = params.get('mode');
  return {
    source: SOURCES.includes(source as SourceKind) ? (source as SourceKind) : '',
    ref: bounded(params.get('ref')),
    mode: MODES.includes(mode as SimMode) ? (mode as SimMode) : 'sim',
    fight: bounded(params.get('fight')),
  };
}

export function simSearch(state: SimState): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (state.source !== '') params.set('source', state.source);
  if (state.ref !== '') params.set('ref', state.ref);
  if (state.mode !== 'sim') params.set('mode', state.mode);
  if (state.fight !== '') params.set('fight', state.fight);
  const query = params.toString();
  return query === '' ? '' : `?${query}`;
}

export function withSimState(state: SimState, patch: Partial<SimState>): SimState {
  return { ...state, ...patch };
}

/** Matches the contract's sim_id: 12 lowercase base32 characters. */
export const SIM_ID_PATTERN = /^\/sim\/([a-z2-7]{12})\/?$/;

export function simIdFrom(pathname: string): string {
  return SIM_ID_PATTERN.exec(pathname)?.[1] ?? '';
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the placeholder island component**

Create `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`. Tasks 11 to 19 grow this file; it starts as the frame and the empty state so the route, the build and the Worker can be proved first.

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte -->
<!-- The simulator island's root. It owns the URL state and the page frame; every section
     below it is a component that takes data and raises events, so this file stays a
     composition and never a place where a layout decision hides. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import { defaultSimState, parseSimState, simIdFrom, type SimState } from '../../lib/sim/url';
  import { ENGINE_VERSION, engineLabel } from '../../lib/sim/version';
  import type { SimResult } from '../../lib/sim/types';

  let { simId = '', inlineResult = null }: { simId?: string; inlineResult?: SimResult | null } = $props();

  let state = $state<SimState>(defaultSimState());
  // untrack: simId and inlineResult are one-shot bootstrap values the shell inlined, not
  // bindings. Reading a prop while initialising state is exactly what Svelte's
  // state_referenced_locally warning is about, and ReportView.svelte does the same for the
  // same reason. Without it the island build prints a warning on every build.
  let openSimId = $state(untrack(() => simId));

  $effect(() => {
    state = parseSimState(window.location.search);
    if (openSimId === '') openSimId = simIdFrom(window.location.pathname);
  });

  const hasCharacter = $derived(state.ref !== '' || inlineResult !== null);
</script>

<div class="flex flex-col gap-[22px] md:gap-8" data-testid="sim-view">
  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-4 gap-y-1 px-[18px] md:px-0">
    <h1 class="section-title">Simulator</h1>
    <a
      class="tabular text-muted ml-auto font-mono text-[12px]"
      href="/sim/specs"
      data-testid="sim-engine-version">{engineLabel(ENGINE_VERSION)}</a
    >
  </div>

  {#if !hasCharacter}
    <p class="text-muted px-[18px] text-[14px] md:px-0" data-testid="sim-empty">
      {simCopy.emptyPrompt}
    </p>
  {/if}
</div>
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the island entry**

Create `web/src/sim-island.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/sim-island.ts
// Entry point for dist/sim-island.js, the standalone bundle all three /sim routes load.
// It is a separate Vite build rather than an Astro island for the same reason the report
// island is: /sim/<id> is served by the Worker as a static shell for an id that did not
// exist at build time, so the script tag needs one stable, unhashed URL.
//
// The two stylesheet imports are load-bearing, and src/sim-island.test.ts holds them:
// sim-island.css is the only stylesheet these pages link for the island's own markup, so
// it has to carry the tokens, base rules and self-hosted faces the rest of the site uses.
import { mount } from 'svelte';
import SimView from './components/sim/SimView.svelte';
import { simIdFrom } from './lib/sim/url';
import type { SimResult } from './lib/sim/types';
import './styles/fonts.css';
import './styles/global.css';

const MOUNT_ID = 'sim';

/** A prerendered fixture page inlines its result so Lighthouse measures a real render. */
export function readInlineResult(element: HTMLElement): SimResult | null {
  const raw = element.dataset.simResult;
  if (raw === undefined || raw === '') return null;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as SimResult;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('sim island: data-sim-result is not valid JSON', error);
    return null;
  }
}

export function simIdFor(element: HTMLElement, pathname: string): string {
  const fromData = element.dataset.simId;
  if (fromData !== undefined && fromData !== '') return fromData;
  return simIdFrom(pathname);
}

function boot(): void {
  const target = document.getElementById(MOUNT_ID);
  if (target === null) return;
  const props = {
    simId: simIdFor(target, window.location.pathname),
    inlineResult: readInlineResult(target),
  };
  // The shell's no-JS paragraph lives inside the mount element; Svelte 5 appends rather
  // than replaces, so it has to go before the mount or it stays under the island.
  target.replaceChildren();
  mount(SimView, { target, props });
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot, { once: true });
} else {
  boot();
}
```

```js
  { file: 'dist/planner-island.js', limitBytes: 60 * 1024 },
  { file: 'dist/report-island.js', limitBytes: 140 * 1024 },
  // The sim island is the planner's gear grid plus the report's tables plus a run control.
```

Create `web/src/pages/sim/[id].astro`. Like `reports/[id].astro`, it prerenders only the fixture so Lighthouse and Playwright have a real page; the Worker serves `sim.html` for every other id.

```astro
---
// web/src/pages/sim/[id].astro
// Only the fixture sim is prerendered, and only under FOREVER_DATA=fixture: every other id
// is served by src/worker.ts from sim.html with its head rewritten. Prerendering the fixture
// gives Lighthouse and Playwright a page with real content rather than an empty shell.
import Base from '../../layouts/Base.astro';
import { FIXTURE_SIM_ID, fixtureSimPaths } from '../../lib/sim/shell-paths';
import fixtureResult from '../../fixtures/sim/result.json';

export function getStaticPaths() {
  return fixtureSimPaths();
}

const { id } = Astro.params;
const inline = id === FIXTURE_SIM_ID ? JSON.stringify(fixtureResult) : undefined;
---

<Base
  title="Saved sim"
  description="A saved World of Warcraft: Forever simulation: the character, the settings, and what the engine produced."
  path={`/sim/${id}`}
>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/sim-island.css" slot="head" />
  <main
    id="main"
    tabindex="-1"
    class="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[1344px] flex-col gap-[22px] pt-4 pb-10 md:gap-8 md:px-12 md:pt-7"
  >
    <div id="sim" data-sim-mount data-sim-id={id} data-sim-result={inline}>
      <p class="text-muted px-[18px] text-[14px] md:px-0">
        This saved sim needs JavaScript to render. The <a href="/sim">simulator</a> does too.
      </p>
    </div>
    <script type="module" src="/sim-island.js"></script>
  </main>
```

```ts
// web/src/pages/_sim.test.ts
import { experimental_AstroContainer as AstroContainer } from 'astro/container';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import Sim from './sim.astro';
import Specs from './sim/specs.astro';

const OG_HOOKS = [
  'data-og="title"',
  'data-og="description"',
  'data-og="canonical"',
  'data-og="og-title"',
  'data-og="og-description"',
  'data-og="og-url"',
  'data-og="og-image"',
];

describe('the sim shell', () => {
  it('carries every data-og hook src/worker.ts rewrites', async () => {
    const container = await AstroContainer.create();
    const html = await container.renderToString(Sim);
    for (const selector of OG_HOOKS) expect(html, selector).toContain(selector);
  });

  it('mounts the island and links its one stylesheet and one script', async () => {
    const container = await AstroContainer.create();
    const html = await container.renderToString(Sim);
    expect(html).toContain('id="sim"');
    expect(html).toContain('data-sim-mount');
    expect(html).toContain('href="/sim-island.css"');
    expect(html).toContain('src="/sim-island.js"');
  });

  it('says what the page needs and offers the planner when there is no JavaScript', async () => {
    const container = await AstroContainer.create();
    const html = await container.renderToString(Sim);
    expect(html).toContain('runs the engine in your browser');
    expect(html).toContain('href="/planner"');
  });

  it('ships no Astro island, so nothing competes with the standalone bundle', async () => {
    const container = await AstroContainer.create();
    const html = await container.renderToString(Sim);
    expect(html.match(/<astro-island/g) ?? []).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe('the spec support shell', () => {
  it('carries the hooks, the mount and its own canonical', async () => {
    const container = await AstroContainer.create();
    const html = await container.renderToString(Specs);
    for (const selector of OG_HOOKS) expect(html, selector).toContain(selector);
    expect(html).toContain('href="https://foreversixty.gg/sim/specs"');
    expect(html).toContain('data-sim-view="specs"');
  });
});
```

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
// web/src/sim-island.test.ts
// The entry's two jobs that nothing else covers: reading the id and the inlined result off
// the mount element, and emptying the element before Svelte mounts -- Svelte 5 appends, so
// the shell's no-JS paragraph would otherwise sit under the rendered island forever.
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readInlineResult, simIdFor } from './sim-island';
import fixtureResult from './fixtures/sim/result.json';

function mountElement(attributes: Record<string, string> = {}): HTMLElement {
  const element = document.createElement('div');
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) element.setAttribute(key, value);
  return element;
}

describe('simIdFor', () => {
  it('prefers the id the prerendered page inlined', () => {
    expect(simIdFor(mountElement({ 'data-sim-id': 'simfixture01' }), '/sim/other2abcde')).toBe(
      'simfixture01',
    );
  });

  it('falls back to the path, which is how a Worker-served shell learns its id', () => {
    expect(simIdFor(mountElement(), '/sim/simfixture01')).toBe('simfixture01');
  });

  it('is empty on /sim and /sim/specs', () => {
    expect(simIdFor(mountElement(), '/sim')).toBe('');
    expect(simIdFor(mountElement(), '/sim/specs')).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('readInlineResult', () => {
  it('parses the inlined result', () => {
    const element = mountElement({ 'data-sim-result': JSON.stringify(fixtureResult) });
    expect(readInlineResult(element)?.sim_id).toBe('simfixture01');
  });

  it('is null when there is none, and null rather than a throw when it is malformed', () => {
    expect(readInlineResult(mountElement())).toBeNull();
    expect(readInlineResult(mountElement({ 'data-sim-result': '{oops' }))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Exactly as `/reports/<id>`: the shell is served for any id, and its head is rewritten from the API so a link pasted into Discord carries the character, the DPS and the engine version.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/lib/report/og-meta.ts`, `web/src/worker.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/report/og-meta.test.ts`, `web/src/worker.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ShellMeta`, `SITE_BASE_URL`, `reportShellMeta` from `web/src/lib/report/og-meta.ts`; `SimResult` (Task 2); `encounterLabel` (Task 2); `specLabel` (Task 1). **Not** `SIM_ID_PATTERN` from `lib/sim/url.ts`: that file belongs to Task 8 and the Worker's own `SIM_ID` regex is declared in `worker.ts` beside `REPORT_ID`, the way every other shell route's is.
- Produces: `simShellMeta(result: SimResult, apiBase: string): ShellMeta` and `'sim'` added to `ShellKind`; `/sim/` in `SHELL_ROUTES`; `SIM_ID` in the Worker; the sim branch in `shellHead` and `shellPathIsAddressable`.

### The copy, decided

For sim `simfixture01`, Fury Warrior, 1,432 DPS on engine `7779ebb`:

- `<title>`: `Fury Warrior, 1,432 DPS · Forever Sixty`
- description: `Simulated on engine 7779ebb: 1,432 DPS ± 5 over 3,000 iterations, raid-buffed, 3:00, single target.`
- `og:image`: the site card, `https://foreversixty.gg/og/index.png`. A saved sim has **no** rendered card at launch: the API renders one per report and per build, and adding a third renderer is the API lane's work, not a reason to block this. Record it in the finish report as deferred.
- canonical: `https://foreversixty.gg/sim/simfixture01`

Every saved sim is indexable: a saved sim is public by contract (`GET /v1/sims/{sim_id}` takes no auth), so unlike a report there is no visibility gate to check first.

```ts
export type ShellKind = 'report' | 'rankings' | 'character' | 'guild' | 'sim';

/**
 * A saved sim's unfurl. There is no rendered card for a sim at launch -- the API draws one
 * per report and per build, and a third renderer is its work, not this lane's -- so the
 * site's own card stands in, the way the rankings and character shells already do.
 *
 * The description is the whole run in one sentence: the engine it came from, the figure and
 * its 95% band, how many iterations bought that band, and the settings. Someone deciding
 * whether to open the link has every number that would change their mind.
 */
export function simShellMeta(result: SimResult, _apiBase: string): ShellMeta {
  const dps = Math.round(result.dps.mean).toLocaleString('en-US');
  const band = Math.round(1.96 * result.dps.error).toLocaleString('en-US');
  const iterations = result.request.iterations.toLocaleString('en-US');
  const buffed = result.summary.auras.some((track) => track.type === 'BUFF' && track.appliers.length > 0);
  const settings = encounterLabel(result.request.encounter, buffed);
  return {
    title: `${specLabel(result.request.spec)}, ${dps} DPS · Forever Sixty`,
    description: `Simulated on engine ${result.engine_version}: ${dps} DPS ± ${band} over ${iterations} iterations, ${settings}.`,
    image: SITE_CARD,
    canonical: `${SITE_BASE_URL}/sim/${result.sim_id ?? ''}`,
  };
}
```

`settingsFromEncounter` is `encounterLabel` from `web/src/lib/sim/encounter.ts` (Task 2) — imported, never redefined here. It takes a `buffed` flag, and **the flag is read from the stored fact, not guessed**: a `SimResult` carries no buff preset, so it is true exactly when the summary records at least one buff on the player and false otherwise.

```ts
import { encounterLabel } from '../sim/encounter';
import { specLabel } from '../sim/specs';
import type { SimResult } from '../sim/types';
```

```ts
  const buffed = result.summary.auras.some((track) => track.type === 'BUFF' && track.appliers.length > 0);
  const settings = encounterLabel(result.request.encounter, buffed);
```
