```ts
function shellPathIsAddressable(url: URL): boolean {
  return (
    REPORT_ID.test(url.pathname) ||
    RANKINGS_SLUG.test(url.pathname) ||
    SIM_ID.test(url.pathname) ||
    parseCharacterPath(url.pathname) !== null ||
    parseGuildPath(url.pathname) !== null
  );
}
```

# Group E — the execution column

Task 10 alone. It depends on Task 1 and Task 2 and on nothing else in this plan; it touches only the rankings, character and guild surfaces. Run it in parallel with A, B and C.

## Task 10: The execution score column and the `execution` sort

Design 4.6 and 4.7: "92% of what your gear can do" beside the parse percentile on every ranked fight, and a second sort order so a player in dungeon gear competes with a full raider on how well they played.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/lib/rankings/api.ts`, `web/src/lib/rankings/url.ts`
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/execution.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/components/Rankings.svelte`, `web/src/components/Character.svelte`, `web/src/components/Guild.svelte`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/execution.test.ts`, `web/src/lib/rankings/url.test.ts`, `web/tests/e2e/sim-execution.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RankingRow`, `RankingMetric`, `CharacterFight`, `GuildRosterBest` from `web/src/lib/rankings/api.ts`; `formatPercent`, `percentileToken` from `web/src/lib/report/format.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/execution.ts`: `const EXECUTION_UNSCORED`, `const EXECUTION_HEADING`, `function executionLabel(score: number | null): string`, `function executionTitle(score: number | null): string`, `function executionHref(reportId: string, fightIndex: number): string`.
  - `RankingMetric` widened to `'dps' | 'hps' | 'damage_taken' | 'execution'`; `execution_score: number | null` on `RankingRow`, `CharacterFight` and `GuildRosterBest`.

### The column, decided

- Heading: `Exec`, in the same `label text-muted` the other headings use. Desktop only; on phone it joins the existing phone label row as `92% executed`.
- Value: the score as a whole percentage, mono and tabular — `92%`. Not coloured: the design forbids colour-only signalling, and a percentile token beside a second coloured number would read as two rankings.
- Null: an em dash `—` with `title` and `aria-label` reading *"Not scored yet: this spec is not validated, or the fight predates scoring."* Null is the common case at launch — two validated specs out of fourteen — so it must read as a normal state, not a failure.
- The value is a link to `/sim?source=fight&ref=<report_id>:<fight_index>&mode=compare`, which is compare mode one click away, exactly as the design asks.
- Sort: `Execution` joins the metric select. When it is chosen, the `value` column's heading becomes `DPS` still (the row keeps showing DPS) and the rows arrive ordered by execution from the API — the web adds the option and the column and sorts nothing itself.

```ts
    expect(executionLabel(1)).toBe('100%');
```

Expected: FAIL. Then create `web/src/lib/sim/execution.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/execution.ts
// "92% of what your gear can do", beside the parse percentile on every ranked fight.
//
// The href is composed here rather than through lib/sim/url.ts's simSearch on purpose: this
// module ships with the rankings pages, which are a different group of this plan from the
// sim page and must be buildable without it. Two query parameters are not worth a
// dependency that would order two independent groups.
//
// The column is deliberately not coloured. A percentile already carries a colour token, and
// a second coloured number in the same row would read as a competing ranking rather than a
// different question. The design system also forbids colour-only signalling, so the state
// this column most often shows -- no score at all, which is every unvalidated spec -- is a
// dash with a sentence, not a grey cell.
export const EXECUTION_HEADING = 'Exec';
export const EXECUTION_UNSCORED =
  'Not scored yet: this spec is not validated, or the fight predates scoring.';

export function executionLabel(score: number | null): string {
  return score === null ? '—' : `${Math.round(score * 100)}%`;
}

export function executionTitle(score: number | null): string {
  return score === null ? EXECUTION_UNSCORED : `${executionLabel(score)} of what this gear can do, simulated`;
}

/** Compare mode on that fight: the design's "compare mode one click away". */
export function executionHref(reportId: string, fightIndex: number): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    source: 'fight',
    ref: `${reportId}:${fightIndex}`,
    mode: 'compare',
  });
  return `/sim?${params.toString()}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Widen the ranking types and the metric vocabulary**

In `web/src/lib/rankings/api.ts`:

```ts
/** The contract's Amendments metric enum, plus `execution` from the simulator contract. */
```

# Group D — the sim page

Tasks 11 to 19, strictly in order. Each one composes on the one before it, and Task 14 onward mount the report lane's components. The whole group depends on A, B and C being finished.

Every component in this group follows the same three rules, so they are stated once here rather than repeated nine times:

- **Props in, callbacks out.** No component in `web/src/components/sim/` fetches, runs a sim, or reads the URL. `SimView.svelte` owns all of that; everything below it takes data and raises `on…` callbacks. That is what makes each one renderable in a test without a pool, an API or a router.
- **Copy from `simCopy`.** No string literal a player can read appears in a component.
- **Phone in the same edit.** Each component's phone layout is written with its desktop layout, per the Global Constraints. Task 19 audits; it does not implement.

## Task 11: The island store, the character strip, and the source switcher

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/store.svelte.ts`
- Create: `web/src/components/sim/CharacterStrip.svelte`, `web/src/components/sim/SourceSwitcher.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/store.test.ts`, `web/tests/e2e/sim-sources.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimCharacter`, `toBuildDraft`, `plannerHrefFor` (Task 6); `fromStoredCharacter`, `fromAddonExport`, `fromPlannerBuild`, `fromLoggedFight`, `sourcePill` (Task 7); `SimSettings`, `defaultSettings` (Task 6); `createPool`, `SimPool` (Task 4); `runSim`, `RunHandle`, `RunUpdate`, `SimRunError` (Task 5); `SimState`, `parseSimState`, `simSearch` (Task 8); `loadItems`, `dataUrl` from `web/src/lib/planner/load.ts`; `SLOTS`, `SLOT_LABELS` from `web/src/lib/planner/types.ts`; `rarityClassFor` from `web/src/lib/planner/items.ts`; `classColorVar` from `web/src/lib/report/format.ts`; `specLabel` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/store.svelte.ts`: `type SimPhase = 'idle' | 'loading-character' | 'loading-engine' | 'running' | 'done' | 'error'`, `interface SimStore`, `function createSimStore(init: { treeVersion: string; apiBase?: string; pool?: SimPool }): SimStore` with getters `phase`, `character`, `settings`, `estimate`, `iterationsDone`, `iterationsTotal`, `result`, `message`, `items`, `state`, `precision`, `premium`, and methods `loadFrom(state)`, `loadAddon(code)`, `loadBuild(id)`, `loadFight(ref)`, `loadStored(path)`, `setSettings(next)`, `setPrecision(n)`, `run()`, `stop()`, `save()`.
  - `CharacterStrip.svelte`: `let { character, items, onchange, onplanner }: { character: SimCharacter; items: Map<number, Item>; onchange: () => void; onplanner: () => void } = $props();`
  - `SourceSwitcher.svelte`: `let { busy, message, signedIn, onaddon, onbuild, onfight, onsignin }: { busy: boolean; message: string | null; signedIn: boolean; onaddon: (code: string) => void; onbuild: (id: string) => void; onfight: (ref: string) => void; onsignin: () => void } = $props();`

### The character strip, decided

One `bg-raised border border-line rounded-panel` panel, `p-4`, `flex flex-col gap-4`.

Row 1, `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3`:
- A 36px square in the class colour (`style={`background: ${classColorVar(className)}`}`, `rounded-control`), decorative (`aria-hidden`).
- The name at `text-[17px] font-semibold`, in the class colour.
- A muted `·`-separated line: `Orc Fury Warrior · 60`, at `text-[13px]`.
- The source pill, `class="pill pill-site"`, reading `sourcePill(character.source)`. Right-aligned with `ml-auto` on desktop; wraps under on phone.
- A `Change source` secondary button (36px desktop, `min-h-11` phone), which flips the panel back to the source switcher.

Row 2, the gear grid: the planner's own layout, `grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-4`, one button-less `div` per slot (the sim does not edit gear; the planner does) carrying the 28px icon, the `label text-muted` slot name and the item name in its rarity class. An empty slot reads `Empty` in `text-muted`. Reusing `GearPanel.svelte` outright is rejected: it is interactive, bound to `PlannerStore`, and opens an item picker. The markup is duplicated deliberately and the comment in the file says so.

Row 3, `flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-3 text-[13px]`: `Talents 0/31/20`, mono; and a link `Open in planner` to `plannerHrefFor(character)`.

On phone, row 1 wraps to three lines and the gear grid is two columns; nothing is hidden.

### The source switcher, decided

A `grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2` of four cards, each a `bg-card-top border border-line rounded-panel p-4 flex flex-col gap-3`. Order, titles, and copy:

1. **From the addon** — "Paste the export string from the Forever Sixty addon, or let the companion push it." A `<textarea rows="2">` with `placeholder="FS1:…"`, and a `Load` button. This is first because it is the only source that needs no account and gives the most accurate character.
2. **From a build** — "Paste a planner link or its id." An `<input>` accepting either `https://foreversixty.gg/b/<id>` or a bare id; the component strips everything up to the last `/` before calling `onbuild`.
3. **From a logged fight** — "Paste a report link, or open a fight from a report and choose Sim this fight." An `<input>` accepting `https://foreversixty.gg/reports/<id>?fight=2` or `<id>:<fight>`; the same strip-and-parse.
4. **Your characters** — signed out: a `Sign in with Battle.net` button and `simCopy.armorySignIn`, then `simCopy.armoryNotYet` in `text-muted text-[12px]`. Signed in: Task 18 replaces the card's body with the landing state's character list.

Below the grid, one `role="alert"` paragraph in `text-strong text-[13px]` carrying `message`, and every control is `disabled` while `busy`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing store test**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/store.test.ts`:

```ts
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createFakeEngine } from '../../fixtures/sim/engine-fake';
import { decodeWireProgress } from '../../fixtures/sim/wire';
import { FIXTURE_BUILD_ID, server } from '../../test-support/msw';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import { createSimStore } from './store.svelte';
import { createPool, type FromWorker, type PoolWorker, type ToWorker } from './worker';

const FURY = 'FS1:1.15.9.69722:warrior:orc:0/5530515/0:head=12640,main_hand=11726';

function fakeWorker(): PoolWorker {
  const engine = createFakeEngine({ tickMs: 0, ticks: 2 });
  const listeners: ((event: { data: FromWorker }) => void)[] = [];
  const emit = (data: FromWorker): void => listeners.forEach((listen) => listen({ data }));
  return {
    addEventListener: (_type, listener) => listeners.push(listener),
    terminate: () => {},
    postMessage(message: ToWorker) {
      void (async () => {
        if (message.kind === 'abort') return engine.abortById(message.runId);
        const token = message.token;
        try {
          if (message.kind === 'split') {
            emit({ kind: 'many', token, results: engine.raidSimRequestSplit(message.request, message.shards) });
          } else if (message.kind === 'combine') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.raidSimResultCombination(message.results) });
          } else if (message.kind === 'stats') {
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result: engine.computeStats(message.request) });
          } else {
            const result = await engine.raidSimAsync(
              message.request,
              (bytes) => emit({ kind: 'progress', token, ...decodeWireProgress(bytes) }),
              message.runId,
            );
            emit({ kind: 'one', token, result });
          }
        } catch (error) {
          emit({ kind: 'failed', token, message: (error as Error).message });
        }
      })();
    },
  };
}

function store() {
  return createSimStore({
    treeVersion: '1.15.9.69722',
    apiBase: 'https://api.test',
    pool: createPool({ hardwareConcurrency: 2, spawn: () => fakeWorker() }),
  });
}

beforeAll(() => server.listen({ onUnhandledRequest: 'error' }));
afterEach(() => server.resetHandlers());
afterAll(() => server.close());

describe('createSimStore', () => {
  it('opens with no character and nothing running', () => {
    const sim = store();
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('idle');
    expect(sim.character).toBeNull();
    expect(sim.result).toBeNull();
    expect(sim.message).toBeNull();
    expect(sim.settings.encounter.duration_sec).toBe(180);
    expect(sim.precision).toBe(3000);
  });

  it('loads an addon export and lands on the character', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('idle');
    expect(sim.character?.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(sim.message).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the old character and shows the reason when a load fails', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    await sim.loadBuild('zzzzzzzzzzzz');
    expect(sim.character?.spec).toBe('warrior-fury');
    expect(sim.message).toBe(simCopy.buildNotFound);
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('idle');
  });

  it('loads a planner build', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadBuild(FIXTURE_BUILD_ID);
    expect(sim.character?.source.kind).toBe('build');
  });

  it('runs, publishes a rising estimate, and finishes with a result', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    const finished = sim.run();
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('running');
    await finished;
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('done');
    expect(sim.iterationsDone).toBe(3000);
    expect(sim.iterationsTotal).toBe(3000);
    expect(sim.estimate.mean).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(sim.result?.summary.damage_done[0].name).toBe('Thrallgar');
  });

  it('refuses to run with no character rather than building an empty request', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.run();
    expect(sim.phase).toBe('idle');
    expect(sim.message).toBe(simCopy.noCharacter);
  });

  it('carries the precision toggle into the request', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    sim.setPrecision(10_000);
    await sim.run();
    expect(sim.result?.request.iterations).toBe(10_000);
    expect(sim.result?.iterations_run).toBe(10_000);
  });

  it('carries the settings into the request', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    sim.setSettings({ ...sim.settings, encounter: { ...sim.settings.encounter, targets: 4 } });
    await sim.run();
    expect(sim.result?.request.encounter.targets).toBe(4);
  });

  it('reports a stop as a stop, keeping any result already on screen', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    await sim.run();
    const first = sim.result;
    const running = sim.run();
    sim.stop();
    await running;
    expect(sim.message).toBe(simCopy.stopped);
    expect(sim.result).toBe(first);
  });

  it('saves a finished result and hands back its id', async () => {
    const sim = store();
    await sim.loadAddon(FURY);
    await sim.run();
    expect(await sim.save()).toMatch(/^[a-z2-7]{12}$/);
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL. Then create `web/src/lib/sim/store.svelte.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/store.svelte.ts
// The simulator island's single source of truth, in the same shape as the planner's
// store.svelte.ts: runes compile here, no DOM dependency, so it is unit-tested like any
// other module and every component below it is a pure render of what it exposes.
//
// Two decisions this file makes and the components must not second-guess:
//
//   * A failed load keeps the character already on screen. Pasting a bad build id after
//     loading a good addon export must not empty the page; the message says what went wrong
//     and the strip stays.
//   * The pool is created on the first run, never on mount. The wasm is 4 MB and the sim
//     page has the site's 1.6 s mobile LCP budget, so nothing touches the engine until the
//     player asks for a number. `init.pool` exists for tests and for the planner, which
//     brings its own.
import { loadItems } from '../planner/load';
import type { Item } from '../planner/types';
import { saveSim } from './api';
import type { SimCharacter } from './character';
import { simCopy } from './copy';
import { EMPTY_ESTIMATE } from './estimate';
import { buildRaidSimRequest } from './raw';
import { runSim, SimRunError, type RunHandle } from './run';
import { defaultSettings, type SimSettings } from './settings';
import {
  fromAddonExport,
  fromLoggedFight,
  fromPlannerBuild,
  fromStoredCharacter,
  type SourceResult,
} from './sources';
import type { CharacterPath } from '../characters';
import type { Estimate, IterationCount, SimResult } from './types';
import { createPool, type SimPool } from './worker';

export type SimPhase = 'idle' | 'loading-character' | 'loading-engine' | 'running' | 'done' | 'error';

export interface SimStoreInit {
  treeVersion: string;
  apiBase?: string;
  /** Injected by tests and by the planner, which keeps one pool for its live estimates. */
  pool?: SimPool;
}

export function createSimStore(init: SimStoreInit) {
  const ctx = { treeVersion: init.treeVersion, apiBase: init.apiBase };

  let phase = $state<SimPhase>('idle');
  let character = $state<SimCharacter | null>(null);
  let settings = $state<SimSettings>(defaultSettings());
  let precision = $state<IterationCount>(3000);
  let estimate = $state<Estimate>(EMPTY_ESTIMATE);
  let iterationsDone = $state(0);
  let iterationsTotal = $state(0);
  let result = $state<SimResult | null>(null);
  let message = $state<string | null>(null);
  let premium = $state(false);
  // A plain Map: it is replaced wholesale when the class changes and only ever read by key,
  // so SvelteMap's per-key tracking would be machinery for mutations that never happen.
  // eslint-disable-next-line svelte/prefer-svelte-reactivity
  let items = $state<Map<number, Item>>(new Map());

  let pool: SimPool | null = init.pool ?? null;
  let handle: RunHandle | null = null;

  function poolOnce(): SimPool {
    pool ??= createPool({});
    return pool;
  }

  /** Every source funnels through here, so the failure rule lives in one place. */
  async function adopt(load: Promise<SourceResult>): Promise<void> {
    phase = 'loading-character';
    message = null;
    const outcome = await load;
    if (!outcome.ok) {
      // The character already on screen stays: a bad paste is not a reason to empty a page.
      message = outcome.message;
      phase = character === null ? 'idle' : 'idle';
      return;
    }
    character = outcome.character;
    result = null;
    estimate = EMPTY_ESTIMATE;
    iterationsDone = 0;
    iterationsTotal = 0;
    phase = 'idle';
    try {
      const file = await loadItems(outcome.character.tree_version, outcome.character.class_slug);
      items = new Map(file.items.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
    } catch {
      // The strip renders slot names and "Empty" without the item file; it is a nicety, and
      // a failed fetch here must not stop a player from running a sim.
      items = new Map();
    }
  }

  return {
    get phase() {
      return phase;
    },
    get character() {
      return character;
    },
    get settings() {
      return settings;
    },
    get precision() {
      return precision;
    },
    get estimate() {
      return estimate;
    },
    get iterationsDone() {
      return iterationsDone;
    },
    get iterationsTotal() {
      return iterationsTotal;
    },
    get result() {
      return result;
    },
    get message() {
      return message;
    },
    get items() {
      return items;
    },
    get premium() {
      return premium;
    },

    setPremium(value: boolean): void {
      premium = value;
    },
    setSettings(next: SimSettings): void {
      settings = next;
    },
    setPrecision(value: IterationCount): void {
      precision = value;
    },

    loadAddon: (code: string) => adopt(fromAddonExport(code, ctx)),
    loadBuild: (id: string) => adopt(fromPlannerBuild(id, ctx)),
    loadFight: (ref: string) => adopt(fromLoggedFight(ref, ctx)),
    loadStored: (path: CharacterPath) => adopt(fromStoredCharacter(path, ctx)),

    /** Adopts a result the page was handed rather than ran: a saved sim, or a server run. */
    adoptResult(next: SimResult): void {
      result = next;
      estimate = next.dps;
      iterationsDone = next.iterations_run;
      iterationsTotal = next.request.iterations;
      phase = 'done';
    },

    async run(): Promise<void> {
      if (character === null) {
        message = simCopy.noCharacter;
        return;
      }
      message = null;
      phase = 'loading-engine';
      iterationsTotal = precision;
      iterationsDone = 0;

      const input = {
        spec: character.spec,
        source: character.source,
        raw: {
          spec: character.spec,
          race: character.race_slug,
          level: character.level,
          gear: character.gear as Record<string, number>,
          talents: character.point_order,
          buffs: settings.buffs,
          consumables: settings.consumables,
          encounter: settings.encounter,
          iterations: precision,
          randomSeed: 0,
        },
      };
      // Built here rather than inside runSim so a malformed character fails before a worker
      // is ever spawned, which is the difference between a message and a hung button.
      buildRaidSimRequest(input.raw);

      phase = 'running';
      handle = runSim(poolOnce(), input, (update) => {
        estimate = update.estimate;
        iterationsDone = update.iterationsDone;
        iterationsTotal = update.iterationsTotal;
      });

      try {
        result = await handle.result;
        phase = 'done';
      } catch (error) {
        const failure = error instanceof SimRunError ? error : null;
        message = failure?.cancelled === true ? simCopy.stopped : (failure?.message ?? simCopy.runFailed);
        phase = failure?.cancelled === true && result !== null ? 'done' : 'error';
      } finally {
        handle = null;
      }
    },

    stop(): void {
      handle?.cancel();
    },

    async save(): Promise<string | null> {
      if (result === null) return null;
      try {
        return await saveSim(result, init.apiBase);
      } catch (error) {
        message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : simCopy.saveFailed;
        return null;
      }
    },

    dispose(): void {
      handle?.cancel();
      pool?.terminate();
      pool = null;
    },
  };
}

export type SimStore = ReturnType<typeof createSimStore>;
```

```ts
  stopped: 'Stopped.',
  runFailed: 'The engine could not run this character.',
  changeSource: 'Change source',
  openInPlanner: 'Open in planner',
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `SourceSwitcher.svelte`**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/SourceSwitcher.svelte -->
<!-- The four ways in. Order is deliberate: the addon export is first because it needs no
     account and produces the most accurate character, and "your characters" is last because
     it is the one that needs a sign-in and, until Blizzard ships a Forever profile API, is
     really the addon export under a different name -- which its own copy says out loud
     rather than letting a player assume their Armory gear is being read. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';

  let {
    busy,
    message,
    signedIn,
    onaddon,
    onbuild,
    onfight,
    onsignin,
  }: {
    busy: boolean;
    message: string | null;
    signedIn: boolean;
    onaddon: (code: string) => void;
    onbuild: (id: string) => void;
    onfight: (ref: string) => void;
    onsignin: () => void;
  } = $props();

  let addonCode = $state('');
  let buildRef = $state('');
  let fightRef = $state('');

  const card = 'border-line bg-card-top rounded-panel flex flex-col gap-3 border p-4';
  const field =
    'border-line-warm rounded-control bg-raised text-text min-h-11 w-full border px-3 py-2 text-[14px]';
  const action = 'border-line-warm-strong rounded-control text-strong label min-h-11 self-start border px-4';

  /** Accepts a full link or a bare id: people paste whichever is in their clipboard. */
  export function lastSegment(value: string): string {
    const withoutQuery = value.trim().split('?')[0].replace(/\/+$/, '');
    return withoutQuery.split('/').at(-1) ?? '';
  }

  /** A report link carries the fight in ?fight=; a pasted ref already has it after a colon. */
  export function fightRefOf(value: string): string {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    if (trimmed.includes(':')) return trimmed;
    const [path, query = ''] = trimmed.split('?');
    const id = path.replace(/\/+$/, '').split('/').at(-1) ?? '';
    const fight = new URLSearchParams(query).get('fight') ?? '1';
    return `${id}:${fight}`;
  }
</script>

<section class="mx-[18px] flex flex-col gap-3 md:mx-0" data-testid="sim-sources">
  <div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
    <div class={card}>
      <h2 class="section-title text-[15px]">{simCopy.sourceAddonTitle}</h2>
      <p class="text-muted text-[13px]">{simCopy.sourceAddonBody}</p>
      <label class="sr-only" for="sim-addon">{simCopy.sourceAddonTitle}</label>
      <textarea
        id="sim-addon"
        rows="2"
        class={field}
        placeholder="FS1:…"
        bind:value={addonCode}
        disabled={busy}
        data-testid="sim-addon-input"
      ></textarea>
      <button type="button" class={action} disabled={busy} onclick={() => onaddon(addonCode)} data-testid="sim-addon-load">
        Load
      </button>
    </div>

    <div class={card}>
      <h2 class="section-title text-[15px]">{simCopy.sourceBuildTitle}</h2>
      <p class="text-muted text-[13px]">{simCopy.sourceBuildBody}</p>
      <label class="sr-only" for="sim-build">{simCopy.sourceBuildTitle}</label>
      <input
        id="sim-build"
        class={field}
        placeholder="foreversixty.gg/b/…"
        bind:value={buildRef}
        disabled={busy}
        data-testid="sim-build-input"
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        class={action}
        disabled={busy}
        onclick={() => onbuild(lastSegment(buildRef))}
        data-testid="sim-build-load">Load</button
      >
    </div>

    <div class={card}>
      <h2 class="section-title text-[15px]">{simCopy.sourceFightTitle}</h2>
      <p class="text-muted text-[13px]">{simCopy.sourceFightBody}</p>
      <label class="sr-only" for="sim-fight">{simCopy.sourceFightTitle}</label>
      <input
        id="sim-fight"
        class={field}
        placeholder="foreversixty.gg/reports/…?fight=2"
        bind:value={fightRef}
        disabled={busy}
        data-testid="sim-fight-input"
      />
      <button
        type="button"
        class={action}
        disabled={busy}
        onclick={() => onfight(fightRefOf(fightRef))}
        data-testid="sim-fight-load">Load</button
      >
    </div>

    <div class={card} data-testid="sim-account-card">
      <h2 class="section-title text-[15px]">{simCopy.sourceAccountTitle}</h2>
      {#if signedIn}
        <slot name="characters" />
      {:else}
        <p class="text-muted text-[13px]">{simCopy.armorySignIn}</p>
        <button type="button" class={action} onclick={onsignin} data-testid="sim-signin">
          Sign in with Battle.net
        </button>
      {/if}
      <p class="text-muted text-[12px]" data-testid="sim-armory-note">{simCopy.armoryNotYet}</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  {#if message}
    <p role="alert" class="text-strong text-[13px]" data-testid="sim-source-message">{message}</p>
  {/if}
</section>
```

## Task 12: The settings bar

Design 4.1: fight length, targets, execute phase, a named buff preset, and the rotation shown by name with a link to what it does.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/components/sim/SettingsBar.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/tests/e2e/sim-settings.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimSettings`, `DURATIONS`, `durationLabel`, `BUFF_PRESETS`, `MAX_TARGETS`, `withDuration`, `withTargets`, `withExecutePhase`, `withPreset`, `executePhaseOn` (Task 6); `specLabel` (Task 1).
- Produces: `SettingsBar.svelte`: `let { settings, spec, disabled, onchange }: { settings: SimSettings; spec: string; disabled: boolean; onchange: (next: SimSettings) => void } = $props();`

### Decided

One `bg-raised border border-line rounded-panel p-4` panel, `flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4 md:gap-5`. Each control is a `<label class="flex flex-col gap-1">` with a `label text-muted` caption above it, matching `SummaryBar.svelte` exactly, and every control is `min-h-11` on phone.

| Caption | Control | Values |
|---|---|---|
| `Fight length` | `<select>` | `DURATIONS`, labelled `1:00` … `8:00`, default `3:00` |
| `Targets` | `<select>` | 1 to `MAX_TARGETS`, labelled `1` … `10`, default `1` |
| `Execute phase` | checkbox | on by default; off sets `execute_ratio` to 0 |
| `Buffs` | `<select>` | `Raid-buffed`, `Solo`, `Custom` |
| `Rotation` | static text | `Default for Fury`, followed by a link `what it does` to `/sim/specs#<spec>` |

The rotation is **not** editable: the design defers the APL builder, and a disabled select would imply otherwise. It is rendered as text so the page states which rotation produced the number, which is the honest minimum.

`variation` (0.2) and `profile` are not exposed. The panel carries one muted footnote — `simCopy.settingsFootnote`, "Fight length varies by 20% between iterations, the way real pulls do." — so the number is explained rather than hidden.

The "Custom" preset opens nothing at launch: choosing it keeps the current buff list and stops the preset from overwriting it. A full buff picker is S3 work. The option's own `title` says so: `simCopy.customPresetNote`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the component**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/SettingsBar.svelte -->
<!-- The five things that change the number, and nothing else. Variation and the encounter
     profile are fixed at the contract's defaults and stated in the footnote rather than
     exposed: a control nobody moves is a control that costs a phone row. The rotation is
     text, not a disabled select, because the APL builder is deferred and a greyed-out
     control would promise it. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import {
    BUFF_PRESETS,
    DURATIONS,
    MAX_TARGETS,
    durationLabel,
    executePhaseOn,
    withDuration,
    withExecutePhase,
    withPreset,
    withTargets,
    type BuffPresetId,
    type SimSettings,
  } from '../../lib/sim/settings';
  import { specLabel, specRow } from '../../lib/sim/specs';

  let {
    settings,
    spec,
    disabled,
    onchange,
  }: { settings: SimSettings; spec: string; disabled: boolean; onchange: (next: SimSettings) => void } =
    $props();

  const control =
    'border-line-warm rounded-control bg-raised text-text min-h-11 min-w-[7rem] border px-3 text-[14px] font-semibold md:min-h-9';
  const targets = Array.from({ length: MAX_TARGETS }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  const rotationName = $derived(specRow(spec)?.spec_slug ?? spec);
  const rotationLabel = $derived(
    `${simCopy.rotationPrefix} ${rotationName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${rotationName.slice(1)}`,
  );
</script>

<section
  class="border-line bg-raised rounded-panel mx-[18px] flex flex-col gap-3 border p-4 md:mx-0"
  data-testid="sim-settings"
>
  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4 md:gap-5">
    <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.fightLength}</span>
      <select
        class={control}
        {disabled}
        value={String(settings.encounter.duration_sec)}
        onchange={(event) => onchange(withDuration(settings, Number(event.currentTarget.value)))}
        data-testid="sim-duration"
      >
        {#each DURATIONS as seconds (seconds)}
          <option value={String(seconds)}>{durationLabel(seconds)}</option>
        {/each}
      </select>
    </label>

    <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.targets}</span>
      <select
        class={control}
        {disabled}
        value={String(settings.encounter.targets)}
        onchange={(event) => onchange(withTargets(settings, Number(event.currentTarget.value)))}
        data-testid="sim-targets"
      >
        {#each targets as count (count)}
          <option value={String(count)}>{count}</option>
        {/each}
      </select>
    </label>

    <label class="flex min-h-11 flex-col gap-1 md:min-h-0">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.executePhase}</span>
      <span class="flex min-h-11 items-center md:min-h-9">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          class="accent-gold h-5 w-5"
          {disabled}
          checked={executePhaseOn(settings)}
          onchange={(event) => onchange(withExecutePhase(settings, event.currentTarget.checked))}
          data-testid="sim-execute"
        />
      </span>
    </label>

    <label class="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.buffs}</span>
      <select
        class={control}
        {disabled}
        value={settings.preset}
        onchange={(event) => onchange(withPreset(settings, event.currentTarget.value as BuffPresetId))}
        data-testid="sim-preset"
      >
        {#each BUFF_PRESETS as preset (preset.id)}
          <option value={preset.id} title={preset.id === 'custom' ? simCopy.customPresetNote : undefined}>
            {preset.label}
          </option>
        {/each}
      </select>
    </label>

    <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.rotation}</span>
      <span class="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-[14px] md:min-h-9">
        <span class="text-strong font-semibold" data-testid="sim-rotation">{rotationLabel}</span>
        <a href={`/sim/specs#${spec}`} class="text-[13px]" data-testid="sim-rotation-link">
          {simCopy.rotationLink}
        </a>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <p class="text-muted text-[12px]" data-testid="sim-settings-footnote">{simCopy.settingsFootnote}</p>
</section>
```

## Task 13: The run control and every state it has

Design 4.1: one button, a DPS figure inside a second that refines as iterations land, its error range, a progress line, a precision toggle, and "Run on our servers" for premium accounts.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/components/sim/RunControl.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/tests/e2e/sim-run.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SimPhase`, `SimStore` (Task 11); `Estimate`, `IterationCount`, `ITERATIONS` (Task 2); `confidenceBand` (Task 4); `isStale`, `engineLabel`, `ENGINE_VERSION` (Task 1).
- Produces: `RunControl.svelte`:
  ```ts
  let {
    phase,
    estimate,
    iterationsDone,
    iterationsTotal,
    precision,
    premium,
    message,
    staleVersion,
    onrun,
    onstop,
    onprecision,
    onserver,
    onrerun,
  }: {
    phase: SimPhase;
    estimate: Estimate;
    iterationsDone: number;
    iterationsTotal: number;
    precision: IterationCount;
    premium: boolean;
    message: string | null;
    /** The engine version a loaded result came from, when it is not the current one. */
    staleVersion: string | null;
    onrun: () => void;
    onstop: () => void;
    onprecision: (value: IterationCount) => void;
    onserver: () => void;
    onrerun: () => void;
  } = $props();
  ```

### Every state, decided

| Phase | Button | Figure | Progress line | Extra |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `idle`, no result | `Run sim`, enabled | `—` in `text-muted` | hidden | — |
| `loading-character` | `Run sim`, disabled | `—` | hidden | — |
| `loading-engine` | `Loading engine…`, disabled | `—` | bar at 0 | `simCopy.engineLoading` under the bar |
| `running` | `Stop`, enabled | the live figure in gold, `± band` beside it | bar, `1,125 of 3,000 iterations` | — |
| `done` | `Run again`, enabled | the final figure in gold, `± band` | bar at 100%, `3,000 iterations` | — |
| `error` | `Run sim`, enabled | last figure if any, else `—` | hidden | the message in `role="alert"` |
| stale engine | as `done` | as `done`, plus a `pill pill-sample` reading `Engine 6a1c2d9` | as `done` | `simCopy.staleEngine` and a `Run again` button |

Layout: one `bg-raised border border-line rounded-panel p-4` panel, `flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:gap-6`.

- The button is first and is `min-w-[9rem]`, so the figure never moves sideways when the label changes length. That is the whole reason for the fixed width.
- The figure is `font-mono tabular text-[32px] leading-none text-gold`, with the band at `text-[14px] text-muted` beside it and a `label text-muted` `DPS` after. `tabular` is what stops the digits jittering while the estimate refines — without it the number visibly wobbles ten times a second.
- The progress bar is the design system's: 6px, `bg-line-soft` track, `bg-gold` fill, `role="progressbar"` with `aria-valuenow`, and a `transition-[width] duration-120`.
- The progress line under it is `font-mono tabular text-[12px] text-muted`.
- Precision is a checkbox labelled `High precision`, whose `title` reads `simCopy.precisionNote` — "10,000 iterations instead of 3,000: about half the error, about three times the wait." It is disabled while running.
- `Run on our servers` appears only when `premium`. It is a secondary button, never the primary one: the free lane is the product.
- On phone the panel stacks; the button is full width, the figure is on its own line, and the precision row is last.

The figure is announced once, not continuously: the panel carries `aria-live="polite"` on the **message** only. A live region on a number that changes ten times a second would make a screen reader unusable.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the component**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/RunControl.svelte -->
<!-- One button and one number. Everything here exists to keep that number honest while it
     moves: the band beside it is the 95% confidence interval, the line under it says how
     many iterations bought that band, and the figure is tabular so the digits do not
     jitter as it refines ten times a second.
     aria-live is on the message and never on the figure: a live region on a number that
     changes that often makes a screen reader unusable. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { confidenceBand } from '../../lib/sim/estimate';
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import type { SimPhase } from '../../lib/sim/store.svelte';
  import { ITERATIONS, type Estimate, type IterationCount } from '../../lib/sim/types';
  import { engineLabel } from '../../lib/sim/version';

  let {
    phase,
    estimate,
    iterationsDone,
    iterationsTotal,
    precision,
    premium,
    message,
    staleVersion,
    onrun,
    onstop,
    onprecision,
    onserver,
    onrerun,
  }: {
    phase: SimPhase;
    estimate: Estimate;
    iterationsDone: number;
    iterationsTotal: number;
    precision: IterationCount;
    premium: boolean;
    message: string | null;
    staleVersion: string | null;
    onrun: () => void;
    onstop: () => void;
    onprecision: (value: IterationCount) => void;
    onserver: () => void;
    onrerun: () => void;
  } = $props();

  const running = $derived(phase === 'running');
  const loadingEngine = $derived(phase === 'loading-engine');
  const hasFigure = $derived(estimate.mean > 0);

  const figure = $derived(hasFigure ? Math.round(estimate.mean).toLocaleString('en-US') : '—');
  const band = $derived(
    hasFigure && estimate.error > 0 ? `± ${Math.round(confidenceBand(estimate)).toLocaleString('en-US')}` : '',
  );
  const percent = $derived(
    iterationsTotal > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((iterationsDone / iterationsTotal) * 100)) : 0,
  );
  const showBar = $derived(running || loadingEngine || phase === 'done');
  const label = $derived(
    running
      ? simCopy.stop
      : loadingEngine
        ? simCopy.engineLoadingButton
        : phase === 'done'
          ? simCopy.runAgain
          : simCopy.run,
  );
  const progressLine = $derived(
    phase === 'done'
      ? `${iterationsDone.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${simCopy.iterations}`
      : `${iterationsDone.toLocaleString('en-US')} of ${iterationsTotal.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${simCopy.iterations}`,
  );
</script>

<section
  class="border-line bg-raised rounded-panel mx-[18px] flex flex-col gap-4 border p-4 md:mx-0 md:flex-row md:items-center md:gap-6"
  data-testid="sim-run"
>
  <button
    type="button"
    class="border-line-warm-strong rounded-control bg-card-top text-strong label min-h-11 w-full border px-5 disabled:opacity-50 md:w-auto md:min-w-[9rem]"
    disabled={loadingEngine || phase === 'loading-character'}
    onclick={() => (running ? onstop() : onrun())}
    data-testid="sim-run-button"
  >
    {label}
  </button>

  <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-2">
      <span class="tabular text-gold font-mono text-[32px] leading-none" data-testid="sim-dps">
        {figure}
      </span>
      <span class="tabular text-muted font-mono text-[14px]" data-testid="sim-error">{band}</span>
      <span class="label text-muted">DPS</span>
      {#if staleVersion}
        <span class="pill pill-sample" data-testid="sim-stale-pill">{engineLabel(staleVersion)}</span>
      {/if}
    </div>

    {#if showBar}
      <div
        class="bg-line-soft h-[6px] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full"
        role="progressbar"
        aria-valuemin="0"
        aria-valuemax="100"
        aria-valuenow={percent}
        aria-label={simCopy.progressLabel}
        data-testid="sim-progress-bar"
      >
        <div class="bg-gold h-full transition-[width] duration-120" style={`width:${percent}%`}></div>
      </div>
      <p class="text-muted tabular font-mono text-[12px]" data-testid="sim-progress">{progressLine}</p>
    {/if}

    {#if loadingEngine}
      <p class="text-muted text-[12px]" data-testid="sim-engine-loading">{simCopy.engineLoading}</p>
    {/if}
  </div>

  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
    <label class="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-[13px] md:min-h-0">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        class="accent-gold h-5 w-5"
        checked={precision === ITERATIONS.precise}
        disabled={running}
        title={simCopy.precisionNote}
        onchange={(event) =>
          onprecision(event.currentTarget.checked ? ITERATIONS.precise : ITERATIONS.normal)}
        data-testid="sim-precision"
      />
      <span class="text-muted">{simCopy.highPrecision}</span>
    </label>
    {#if premium}
      <button
        type="button"
        class="border-line-warm rounded-control text-nav label min-h-11 border px-4 md:min-h-9"
        disabled={running}
        onclick={onserver}
        data-testid="sim-server-run">{simCopy.runOnServers}</button
      >
    {/if}
  </div>

  {#if staleVersion}
    <p class="order-last w-full text-[13px]" data-testid="sim-stale">
      <span class="text-strong">{simCopy.staleEngine}</span>
      <button type="button" class="ml-2 underline" onclick={onrerun} data-testid="sim-rerun">
        {simCopy.runAgain}
      </button>
    </p>
  {/if}

  {#if message}
    <p
      role="alert"
      aria-live="polite"
      class="text-strong order-last w-full text-[13px]"
      data-testid="sim-message"
    >
      {message}
    </p>
  {/if}
</section>
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Mount it in `SimView.svelte`**

```svelte
    <RunControl
      phase={store.phase}
      estimate={store.estimate}
      iterationsDone={store.iterationsDone}
      iterationsTotal={store.iterationsTotal}
      precision={store.precision}
      premium={store.premium}
      message={store.message}
      staleVersion={store.result !== null && isStale(store.result.engine_version)
        ? store.result.engine_version
        : null}
      onrun={() => void store.run()}
      onstop={() => store.stop()}
      onprecision={(value) => store.setPrecision(value)}
      onserver={() => void store.runOnServer()}
      onrerun={() => void store.run()}
    />
```

`store.runOnServer()` is added in this task: it calls `dispatchServerSim(buildSimRequest(...))` with `raw` set, then polls `fetchSimProgress` every 2 seconds, updating `estimate.mean` and `iterationsDone` from the response, and calls `fetchSim(simId)` once the state is `done`. A 402 sets `message` to `simCopy.premiumRequired` and leaves the browser result untouched.

## Task 14: Results — the sentence, and the report components

Design 4.1's results: a one-sentence plain-language summary, then the report page's own components. Nothing is re-rendered here; this task is a layout over `web/src/components/report/`.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/sentence.ts`, `web/src/components/sim/SimResults.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/sentence.test.ts`, `web/tests/e2e/sim-results.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Summary`, `Actor` from `web/src/lib/report/types.ts`; `ActorTable`, `AuraTable`, `CastTable`, `ResourceGraphs`, `TimelinesView` from `web/src/components/report/`; `fullWindow` from `web/src/lib/report/window.ts`; `formatAmount`, `formatDuration` from `web/src/lib/report/format.ts`; `Estimate` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/sentence.ts`: `const NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE`, `function playerActor(summary: Summary): Actor | null`, `function summarySentence(summary: Summary): string`.
  - `SimResults.svelte`: `let { summary, estimate, iterationsRun }: { summary: Summary; estimate: Estimate; iterationsRun: number } = $props();`

### The report components this page mounts, and with what

The report lane's components take plain data and never fetch, which is what makes this a layout. The exact props:

| Tab | Component | Props |
|---|---|---|
| `Damage` | `ActorTable` | `actors={summary.damage_done}` `durationMs={summary.duration_ms}` `metricLabel="DPS"` |
| `Buffs` | `AuraTable` | `tracks={buffs}` `durationMs={summary.duration_ms}` `kind="BUFF"` |
| `Debuffs` | `AuraTable` | `tracks={debuffs}` `durationMs={summary.duration_ms}` `kind="DEBUFF"` |
| `Casts` | `CastTable` | `rows={summary.casts}` `durationMs={summary.duration_ms}` |
| `Resources` | `ResourceGraphs` | `tracks={summary.resources}` `durationMs={summary.duration_ms}` |
| `Timeline` | `TimelinesView` | `summary` `window={fullWindow(summary.duration_ms)}` `classOf={new Map()}` |
| `Distribution` | `DpsDistribution` (new, this task) | `estimate` `iterationsRun` |

`percentiles` and `approximate` are left at their defaults: a sim has no parse percentile to compare against and no brushed window, so passing either would be a claim the page cannot support.

`buffs` and `debuffs` are `summary.auras.filter((track) => track.type === 'BUFF' | 'DEBUFF')`, derived in this component and not in the store, because it is a rendering split.

`TimelinesView` is the design's "sample cast timeline from one iteration": the engine's result carries one iteration's cast log, so `CastRow.sequence` on a sim is a real sequence of real casts, not an average, and the lane view draws it exactly as it draws a logged fight's. It is given the full window — `fullWindow(summary.duration_ms)` from `web/src/lib/report/window.ts` — because a sim has no brush.

`TimeChart` is the one report component **not** mounted. Its per-second series is a fight's own damage over time; a sim's `Actor.series` is an average over three thousand iterations, and brushing an average would produce numbers with no meaning. `Distribution` is what stands in its place, and the strip names it so nobody looks for a chart that would lie.

### The tab strip and the distribution

The tab strip is the report's own idiom: `role="tablist"`, a horizontally scrollable `flex flex-nowrap overflow-x-auto md:flex-wrap` row of `min-h-11` pills with `aria-selected`, `Damage` selected first. The selected tab is local component state, not URL state: a sim is not a thing people deep-link a tab of, and `/sim/<id>?tab=` would be a second URL vocabulary to keep in step with the report's.

`DpsDistribution.svelte` is a 6px-bar histogram, not a chart library: 20 buckets between `estimate.min` and `estimate.max`, heights from a normal density at the run's mean and standard deviation, with the mean marked in gold and the 95% band shaded. It is drawn as `<div>`s inside a `flex items-end gap-[2px] h-32`, so it costs nothing and needs no canvas. Under it, four mono rows: `Mean`, `Standard deviation`, `Lowest iteration`, `Highest iteration`. The histogram carries `aria-hidden="true"` and the four rows are the accessible content — the shape is decoration, the numbers are the data.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Summary } from '../report/types';
import { fixtureResult } from '../../test-support/msw';
import { NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE, playerActor, summarySentence } from './sentence';

const summary = fixtureResult.summary;

function withoutAuras(base: Summary): Summary {
  return { ...base, auras: [] };
}

describe('summarySentence', () => {
  it('names the two biggest damage sources, their share, and the biggest buff', () => {
    expect(summarySentence(summary)).toBe(
      'Bloodthirst and white hits are 61% of your damage; Flurry is up 78% of the fight.',
    );
  });

  it('drops the buff clause when there are no buffs to name', () => {
    expect(summarySentence(withoutAuras(summary))).toBe('Bloodthirst and white hits are 61% of your damage.');
  });

  it('reads as one ability when there is only one', () => {
    const actor = summary.damage_done[0];
    const one: Summary = {
      ...withoutAuras(summary),
      damage_done: [{ ...actor, total: 94210, abilities: [actor.abilities[0]] }],
    };
    expect(summarySentence(one)).toBe('Bloodthirst is 100% of your damage.');
  });

  it('says so rather than dividing by zero when nothing landed', () => {
    expect(summarySentence({ ...summary, damage_done: [] })).toBe(NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE);
    expect(summarySentence({ ...summary, damage_done: [{ ...summary.damage_done[0], total: 0 }] })).toBe(
      NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE,
    );
  });
});

describe('playerActor', () => {
  it('is the actor with the most damage, since the sim has one player and its pets', () => {
    const pet = { ...summary.damage_done[0], guid: 'sim-pet', name: 'Pet', total: 10 };
    expect(playerActor({ ...summary, damage_done: [pet, summary.damage_done[0]] })?.guid).toBe('sim-player');
  });

  it('is null for an empty table', () => {
    expect(playerActor({ ...summary, damage_done: [] })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL. Then create `web/src/lib/sim/sentence.ts`:

```ts
import type { Actor, Summary } from '../report/types';

export const NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE = 'This run recorded no damage; the rotation did not fire.';

const PROSE_NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  Melee: 'white hits',
  'Melee (Off Hand)': 'off-hand white hits',
  'Auto Shot': 'auto shots',
};

function prose(name: string): string {
  return PROSE_NAMES[name] ?? name;
}

function percent(part: number, whole: number): number {
  return whole <= 0 ? 0 : Math.round((part / whole) * 100);
}

export function playerActor(summary: Summary): Actor | null {
  if (summary.damage_done.length === 0) return null;
  return summary.damage_done.reduce((best, actor) => (actor.total > best.total ? actor : best));
}

export function summarySentence(summary: Summary): string {
  const actor = playerActor(summary);
  if (actor === null || actor.total <= 0) return NO_DAMAGE_SENTENCE;

  const top = [...actor.abilities].sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total).slice(0, 2);
  const share = percent(
    top.reduce((sum, ability) => sum + ability.total, 0),
    actor.total,
  );
  const damage =
    top.length >= 2
      ? `${prose(top[0].name)} and ${prose(top[1].name)} are ${share}% of your damage`
      : `${prose(top[0].name)} is ${share}% of your damage`;

  const aura = [...summary.auras]
    .filter((track) => track.target_guid === actor.guid && track.type === 'BUFF')
    .sort((a, b) => b.uptime_ms - a.uptime_ms)[0];
  if (aura === undefined || summary.duration_ms <= 0) return `${damage}.`;

  return `${damage}; ${aura.name} is up ${percent(aura.uptime_ms, summary.duration_ms)}% of the fight.`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `DpsDistribution.svelte`**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/DpsDistribution.svelte -->
<!-- The run's distribution, as twenty bars and four numbers. Deliberately not a chart
     library and not a canvas: it is a shape, the four rows below it are the data, and a
     dependency for twenty divs would cost more than the whole island's budget allows.
     The bars are aria-hidden; a screen reader reads the numbers. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import { confidenceBand } from '../../lib/sim/estimate';
  import type { Estimate } from '../../lib/sim/types';

  let { estimate, iterationsRun }: { estimate: Estimate; iterationsRun: number } = $props();

  const BUCKETS = 20;

  /** Normal density at the run's own mean and standard deviation, scaled to the tallest bar. */
  const bars = $derived.by(() => {
    const spread = estimate.max - estimate.min;
    if (spread <= 0 || estimate.stddev <= 0) return [] as { height: number; inBand: boolean }[];
    const band = confidenceBand(estimate);
    const raw = Array.from({ length: BUCKETS }, (_, i) => {
      const at = estimate.min + (spread * (i + 0.5)) / BUCKETS;
      const z = (at - estimate.mean) / estimate.stddev;
      return { density: Math.exp(-0.5 * z * z), inBand: Math.abs(at - estimate.mean) <= band };
    });
    const peak = Math.max(...raw.map((bucket) => bucket.density));
    return raw.map((bucket) => ({ height: Math.round((bucket.density / peak) * 100), inBand: bucket.inBand }));
  });

  const rows = $derived([
    [simCopy.distMean, Math.round(estimate.mean).toLocaleString('en-US')],
    [simCopy.distStdDev, Math.round(estimate.stddev).toLocaleString('en-US')],
    [simCopy.distLowest, Math.round(estimate.min).toLocaleString('en-US')],
    [simCopy.distHighest, Math.round(estimate.max).toLocaleString('en-US')],
    [simCopy.distIterations, iterationsRun.toLocaleString('en-US')],
  ]);
</script>

<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4" data-testid="sim-distribution">
  {#if bars.length > 0}
    <div class="flex h-32 items-end gap-[2px]" aria-hidden="true">
      {#each bars as bar, i (i)}
        <span
          class={`flex-1 rounded-t-[2px] ${bar.inBand ? 'bg-gold' : 'bg-line'}`}
          style={`height:${Math.max(2, bar.height)}%`}
        ></span>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}
  <dl class="flex flex-col">
    {#each rows as [key, value] (key)}
      <div class="border-line-soft flex justify-between gap-3 border-b py-1 last:border-b-0">
        <dt class="text-muted text-[13px]">{key}</dt>
        <dd class="tabular text-strong m-0 font-mono text-[13px]">{value}</dd>
      </div>
    {/each}
  </dl>
</div>
```

with the copy:

```ts

  distStdDev: 'Standard deviation',
  distLowest: 'Lowest iteration',
  distHighest: 'Highest iteration',
  distIterations: 'Iterations',
  tabDamage: 'Damage',
  tabBuffs: 'Buffs',
  tabDebuffs: 'Debuffs',
  tabCasts: 'Casts',
  tabResources: 'Resources',
  tabTimeline: 'Timeline',
  tabDistribution: 'Distribution',
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `SimResults.svelte`**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/SimResults.svelte -->
<!-- The sim's results are the report's results. Every table here is the component the logs
     report already mounts, taking the same Summary the adapter produced, which is what the
     design means by "no second renderer": an improvement to the damage table reaches the
     simulator for free and cannot drift away from it.
     TimeChart is the one report component deliberately left out. Its series is a real
     fight's second-by-second damage; a sim's series is an average over three thousand
     iterations, and brushing an average would produce numbers that mean nothing. The
     Distribution tab is what stands in its place, and the strip says so. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import AuraTable from '../report/AuraTable.svelte';
  import ActorTable from '../report/ActorTable.svelte';
  import CastTable from '../report/CastTable.svelte';
  import ResourceGraphs from '../report/ResourceGraphs.svelte';
  import TimelinesView from '../report/TimelinesView.svelte';
  import { fullWindow } from '../../lib/report/window';
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import { summarySentence } from '../../lib/sim/sentence';
  import type { Estimate } from '../../lib/sim/types';
  import type { Summary } from '../../lib/report/types';
  import DpsDistribution from './DpsDistribution.svelte';

  let {
    summary,
    estimate,
    iterationsRun,
  }: { summary: Summary; estimate: Estimate; iterationsRun: number } = $props();

  const TABS = [
    { id: 'damage', label: simCopy.tabDamage },
    { id: 'buffs', label: simCopy.tabBuffs },
    { id: 'debuffs', label: simCopy.tabDebuffs },
    { id: 'casts', label: simCopy.tabCasts },
    { id: 'resources', label: simCopy.tabResources },
    { id: 'timeline', label: simCopy.tabTimeline },
    { id: 'distribution', label: simCopy.tabDistribution },
  ] as const;

  let tab = $state<(typeof TABS)[number]['id']>('damage');

  // A sim's roster carries its own class name, so TimelinesView never falls back to this
  // lookup. It is created once rather than per render so the prop is referentially stable.
  const emptyClassMap = new Map<string, string>();

  const sentence = $derived(summarySentence(summary));
  const buffs = $derived(summary.auras.filter((track) => track.type === 'BUFF'));
  const debuffs = $derived(summary.auras.filter((track) => track.type === 'DEBUFF'));

  const pill =
    'inline-flex min-h-11 shrink-0 items-center px-3 text-[12px] font-bold tracking-[0.06em] uppercase md:min-h-9';
</script>

<p
  class="text-strong px-[18px] text-[15px] md:px-0"
  role="status"
  aria-live="polite"
  data-testid="sim-sentence"
>
  {sentence}
</p>

<section class="flex flex-col gap-3" data-testid="sim-results">
  <div
    class="-mx-[18px] flex flex-nowrap gap-x-1 overflow-x-auto px-[18px] md:mx-0 md:flex-wrap md:px-0"
    role="tablist"
    aria-label={simCopy.resultsTablist}
  >
    {#each TABS as entry (entry.id)}
      <button
        type="button"
        role="tab"
        class={`${pill} ${tab === entry.id ? 'text-gold' : 'text-nav'}`}
        aria-selected={tab === entry.id}
        onclick={() => (tab = entry.id)}
        data-testid={`sim-tab-${entry.id}`}
      >
        {entry.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <div class="border-line bg-raised rounded-panel mx-[18px] border md:mx-0">
    {#if tab === 'damage'}
      <ActorTable actors={summary.damage_done} durationMs={summary.duration_ms} metricLabel="DPS" />
    {:else if tab === 'buffs'}
      <AuraTable tracks={buffs} durationMs={summary.duration_ms} kind="BUFF" />
    {:else if tab === 'debuffs'}
      <AuraTable tracks={debuffs} durationMs={summary.duration_ms} kind="DEBUFF" />
    {:else if tab === 'casts'}
      <CastTable rows={summary.casts} durationMs={summary.duration_ms} />
    {:else if tab === 'resources'}
      <ResourceGraphs tracks={summary.resources} durationMs={summary.duration_ms} />
    {:else if tab === 'timeline'}
      <!-- One iteration's real cast sequence, not an average: the engine's result carries a
           sample cast log, so the lane view means the same thing here as on a logged fight. -->
      <TimelinesView {summary} window={fullWindow(summary.duration_ms)} classOf={emptyClassMap} />
    {:else}
      <DpsDistribution {estimate} {iterationsRun} />
    {/if}
  </div>
</section>
```

with `resultsTablist: 'Results'` added to the copy.

## Task 15: The unsupported-spec state and `/sim/specs`

Design 4.4, and the 4.1 state that reads "instead of a number, the spec's card from the support page". One card component serves both, which is why they are one task.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/components/sim/SpecCard.svelte`, `web/src/components/sim/SpecGrid.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/specs.test.ts` (extend), `web/tests/e2e/sim-specs.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SpecFidelity`, `SpecState` (Task 2); `fetchSpecs` (Task 2); `specLabel`, `classOfSpec`, `SPECS` (Task 1); `classColorVar`, `formatPercent` from `web/src/lib/report/format.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `SpecCard.svelte`: `let { row, compact = false }: { row: SpecFidelity; compact?: boolean } = $props();`
  - `SpecGrid.svelte`: `let { rows, error }: { rows: SpecFidelity[] | null; error: string | null } = $props();`
  - `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`: `specStateLabel`, `specStateNote`, `specsIntro`, `specsLoading`.

### The card, decided

`bg-card-top border border-line rounded-panel p-4 flex flex-col gap-3`, with `id={row.spec}` so `/sim/specs#warrior-fury` from the settings bar's rotation link scrolls to it.

- **Title**: the spec name at `text-[15px] font-semibold`, in the class colour.
- **State pill**, immediately after the title:

| `state` | Pill | Class |
|---|---|---|
| `validated` | `Validated` | `pill pill-site` |
| `in_progress` | `In progress` | `pill pill-sample` |
| `unsupported` | `Not yet` | `pill pill-sample` |

- **The figure**, one mono line: `Median gap 3.1% over 50 parses`. When `median_gap` is null: `No parses yet`. When `parses` is under 50 and the state is `validated`, the row still says the real count — the contract's threshold is the API's business, and the page reports what it is told.
- **A sentence** in `text-muted text-[13px]`, from `specStateNote(row)`:
  - validated: *"Within 5% of the top 50 parses. Numbers from this spec are trustworthy."*
  - in_progress: *"Being corrected against real parses. Treat the number as a direction, not a figure."*
  - unsupported: *"Not modelled yet. Specs arrive in the order people are actually playing them."*
- **The largest gaps**, up to three rows, mono, `Heroic Strike 44 cast, 57 simmed` — the actual first, because the player's own number is the one they recognise. Omitted entirely when `worst_actions` is empty, rather than rendering an empty heading.
- **Footer**: `Engine 7779ebb · 2026-09-14`, mono, muted. When `engine_version` is null: just the date.

`compact` drops the gap rows and the footer, for the in-page unsupported state where the card is one item in a column rather than one of fourteen in a grid.

### The page, decided

`/sim/specs` renders: the heading row (from `SimView`), one paragraph of standing copy, then `SpecGrid` — `grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`, cards in `SPECS` order, which is rankings-population order and therefore the order they are being built in. A spec in `SPECS` with no row from the API renders as `unsupported` with zero parses, so the grid is always fourteen cards and nobody has to wonder whether a spec is missing or unlisted.

`specsIntro`: *"The simulator is only worth as much as its numbers. A nightly job sims the top 50 parses for each spec and publishes the gap here, whatever it is."*

While loading: the grid renders fourteen skeleton cards at the same height, so the page does not reflow when the answer lands — the LCP budget is measured on this page too. On failure: one `role="alert"` line with `simCopy.specsFailed` and a `Try again` button.

### The in-page unsupported state

When `store.character !== null` and that spec's row is `unsupported`, `SimView` renders — **instead of** the run control, the sentence and the results, not above them — the settings bar (still useful: it says what would be simulated) and one `SpecCard` with `compact`, preceded by one line: `simCopy.specUnsupportedLead`, *"The simulator does not model this spec yet."*

- [ ] **Step 2: Add `mergeSpecRows` and the state vocabulary**

These belong beside the spec list rather than in the generated file, so create `web/src/lib/sim/spec-state.ts`:

```ts
// web/src/lib/sim/spec-state.ts
// How a spec's fidelity reads, and the rule that the support page always shows every spec.
//
// The grid is always the canonical list, never the API's answer: fourteen cards, in
// rankings-population order, so a spec with no row reads "Not yet" rather than being absent.
// An absent card is indistinguishable from a page that failed to load half its content, and
// "can I trust this" is the question this page exists to answer.
import { SPECS } from './specs';
import type { SpecFidelity, SpecState } from './types';

const LABELS: Record<SpecState, string> = {
  validated: 'Validated',
  in_progress: 'In progress',
  unsupported: 'Not yet',
};

const NOTES: Record<SpecState, string> = {
  validated: 'Within 5% of the top 50 parses. Numbers from this spec are trustworthy.',
  in_progress: 'Being corrected against real parses. Treat the number as a direction, not a figure.',
  unsupported: 'Not modelled yet. Specs arrive in the order people are actually playing them.',
};

export function specStateLabel(state: SpecState): string {
  return LABELS[state];
}

export function specStateNote(state: SpecState): string {
  return NOTES[state];
}

export function specPillClass(state: SpecState): string {
  return state === 'validated' ? 'pill pill-site' : 'pill pill-sample';
}

export function mergeSpecRows(rows: readonly SpecFidelity[]): SpecFidelity[] {
  const bySpec = new Map(rows.map((row) => [row.spec, row]));
  return SPECS.map(
    (entry) =>
      bySpec.get(entry.spec) ?? {
        spec: entry.spec,
        state: 'unsupported' as const,
        median_gap: null,
        parses: 0,
        worst_actions: [],
        engine_version: null,
        updated_at: '',
      },
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `SpecCard.svelte`**

```svelte
<!-- web/src/components/sim/SpecCard.svelte -->
<!-- "Can I trust this" in one card. The figure is whatever the validation job measured,
     published either way -- the design's whole point is that the answer lives on the site
     rather than in a footnote, so a bad gap is shown as plainly as a good one.
     The cast-gap rows read actual first: the player's own number is the one they recognise
     from their own log, and the simmed one is the claim being made about it. -->
<script lang="ts">
  import { classColorVar } from '../../lib/report/format';
  import { simCopy } from '../../lib/sim/copy';
  import { specPillClass, specStateLabel, specStateNote } from '../../lib/sim/spec-state';
  import { classOfSpec, specLabel } from '../../lib/sim/specs';
  import type { SpecFidelity } from '../../lib/sim/types';

  let { row, compact = false }: { row: SpecFidelity; compact?: boolean } = $props();

  const colour = $derived(classColorVar(classOfSpec(row.spec)));
  const figure = $derived(
    row.median_gap === null
      ? simCopy.specNoParses
      : `${simCopy.specMedianGap} ${(row.median_gap * 100).toFixed(1)}% ${simCopy.specOver} ${row.parses} ${row.parses === 1 ? simCopy.specParse : simCopy.specParses}`,
  );
  const footer = $derived(
    [row.engine_version === null ? '' : `Engine ${row.engine_version}`, row.updated_at.slice(0, 10)]
      .filter((part) => part !== '')
      .join(' · '),
  );
</script>

<article
  id={row.spec}
  class="border-line bg-card-top rounded-panel flex flex-col gap-3 border p-4"
  data-testid={`spec-${row.spec}`}
>
  <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
    <h3 class="text-[15px] font-semibold" style={`color: ${colour}`}>{specLabel(row.spec)}</h3>
    <span class={specPillClass(row.state)} data-testid="spec-state">{specStateLabel(row.state)}</span>
  </div>

  <p class="tabular text-strong font-mono text-[13px]" data-testid="spec-figure">{figure}</p>
  <p class="text-muted text-[13px]">{specStateNote(row.state)}</p>

  {#if !compact && row.worst_actions.length > 0}
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span class="label text-muted">{simCopy.specWorstActions}</span>
      {#each row.worst_actions.slice(0, 3) as action (action.name)}
        <span class="tabular text-muted font-mono text-[12px]">
          {action.name} {action.actual_casts} {simCopy.specCast}, {action.sim_casts} {simCopy.specSimmed}
        </span>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}

  {#if !compact && footer !== ''}
    <p class="tabular text-muted mt-auto font-mono text-[12px]" data-testid="spec-footer">{footer}</p>
  {/if}
</article>
```

Three states in one component: `rows === null && error === null` renders fourteen skeleton articles at `min-h-[168px]` with `bg-card-top border border-line rounded-panel` and nothing inside, so the grid's height is the same before and after the fetch and the page does not reflow. `error !== null` renders one `role="alert"` paragraph and a `Try again` button that raises `onretry`. - [ ] **Step 5: Wire both into `SimView.svelte`**

`SimView` gains a `view` prop read from `data-sim-view` (`'sim' | 'specs'`). When it is `'specs'`, the whole body is `specsIntro` plus `SpecGrid`, fetched once in a `$effect` through `fetchSpecs`. When it is `'sim'` and the loaded character's spec merges to `unsupported`, the run control, sentence and results are replaced by `specUnsupportedLead` and a compact `SpecCard`. `SimView` fetches the spec list on mount in both cases; it is one small, cacheable, credential-free GET and the page needs it either way.

## Task 16: Compare mode

Design 4.2: the character loaded from a fight's recorded gear, the sim run, and two columns — simulated and actual — per ability and per buff, with the differences explained in words.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/lib/sim/compare.ts`, `web/src/components/sim/CompareView.svelte`
- Modify: `web/src/components/sim/SimView.svelte`, `web/src/components/report/ReportView.svelte`, `web/src/lib/sim/copy.ts`
- Test: `web/src/lib/sim/compare.test.ts`, `web/tests/e2e/sim-compare.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Summary` from `web/src/lib/report/types.ts`; `fetchReportMeta`, `fetchSummary` from `web/src/lib/report/load.ts`; `playerActor` (Task 14); `parseFightRef`, `fromLoggedFight` (Task 7); `formatPercent`, `formatDuration` from `web/src/lib/report/format.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `web/src/lib/sim/compare.ts`: `interface AbilityDiff`, `interface AuraDiff`, `interface Comparison`, `const COMPARE_NO_PLAYER`, `const MAX_ABILITY_LINES`, `const MAX_AURA_LINES`, `function compareSummaries(sim: Summary, actual: Summary, actualPlayerName: string): Comparison`.
  - `CompareView.svelte`: `let { comparison, simDuration, actualDuration }: { comparison: Comparison; simDuration: number; actualDuration: number } = $props();`

### No second parse, and no second API

The contract is explicit: "Compare mode reads the fight summary from the report's existing `data_base_url`; no new API." So compare mode fetches exactly two things — `GET /v1/reports/{id}` for the base url, and `${base}/fights/{n}/summary.json` — through the report lane's own `fetchReportMeta` and `fetchSummary`. It never parses, never re-summarises, and never asks the API for a comparison.

### The layout, decided

Compare mode **replaces** the sentence and the results, and keeps the strip, the settings bar and the run control. `?mode=compare&ref=<report>:<fight>` is what opens it.

1. **The headline**, one `text-[15px] text-strong` line: *"This fight did 1,175 DPS; the sim expects 1,432. That is 82% of what this gear can do."* It is the answer; everything below it is the working.
2. **The lines**, a `<ul>` of at most eight `text-[14px]` sentences, five cast gaps then three uptime gaps, largest first, each exactly as the design writes them: *"Bloodthirst cast 33 times, the sim expects 41."* and *"Flurry uptime 61% against 78%."* This is what the design means by "explained in words", and it comes before the tables because most players will read it and stop.
3. **Two column headings** — `This fight` then `Simulated`, in that order, because the fight is the thing the player recognises.
4. **The ability table**: one row per ability either run had. `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_auto]` on phone, `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.6fr)_72px_72px_88px_88px]` from md: name, actual casts, sim casts, actual damage, sim damage. Rows ordered by the larger of the two damage figures. The two casts columns sit side by side so the eye reads the pair, not the rows.
5. **The buff table**: name, actual uptime, sim uptime, each as a whole percentage of that run's own duration — never of the other's, which is why `compareSummaries` divides each by its own `duration_ms`.
6. **A footnote**: `simCopy.compareFootnote`, *"The two fights are not the same length; every share is of its own fight."*

Differences are not coloured. A red-and-green diff would be the fourth colour vocabulary on the page and the design system forbids colour-only signalling; the words above the table already say which way each gap runs.

Reached from three places, all writing the same URL: the report's fight header gets a `Sim this fight` link (this task adds it to `ReportView.svelte`), a ranking row's execution cell already links there (Task 10), and the sim page's own fight source card.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `web/src/lib/sim/compare.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { fixtureResult } from '../../test-support/msw';
import type { Summary } from '../report/types';
import { COMPARE_NO_PLAYER, compareSummaries } from './compare';

const sim = fixtureResult.summary;

/** The same character, played worse: fewer casts, less uptime, less damage. */
function actualFight(): Summary {
  const actor = sim.damage_done[0];
  return {
    ...sim,
    damage_done: [
      {
        ...actor,
        total: 211423,
        abilities: actor.abilities.map((ability) => ({
          ...ability,
          total: Math.round(ability.total * 0.82),
        })),
      },
    ],
    casts: [
      { ...sim.casts[0], succeeded: 33, started: 33 },
      { ...sim.casts[1], succeeded: 19, started: 19 },
    ],
    auras: [
      { ...sim.auras[0], uptime_ms: 109800 },
      { ...sim.auras[1], uptime_ms: 72000 },
    ],
  };
}

describe('compareSummaries', () => {
  it('leads with the two DPS figures and what the fraction means', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Thrallgar');
    expect(comparison.headline).toBe(
      'This fight did 1,175 DPS; the sim expects 1,432. That is 82% of what this gear can do.',
    );
    expect(comparison.executionScore).toBeCloseTo(0.82, 2);
  });

  it('explains each cast-count gap in words, biggest first', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Thrallgar');
    expect(comparison.lines[0]).toBe('Bloodthirst cast 33 times, the sim expects 41.');
    expect(comparison.lines).toContain('Whirlwind cast 19 times, the sim expects 22.');
  });

  it('explains each uptime gap the way the design writes it', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Thrallgar');
    expect(comparison.lines).toContain('Flurry uptime 61% against 78%.');
    expect(comparison.lines).toContain('Death Wish uptime 40% against 60%.');
  });

  it('pairs every ability the two runs have between them, biggest first', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Thrallgar');
    expect(comparison.abilities.map((row) => row.name)).toEqual([
      'Bloodthirst',
      'Melee',
      'Whirlwind',
      'Heroic Strike',
    ]);
    expect(comparison.abilities[0]).toEqual({
      name: 'Bloodthirst',
      simCasts: 41,
      actualCasts: 33,
      simDamage: 94210,
      actualDamage: 77252,
    });
  });

  it('clamps the score to the range the contract stores', () => {
    const doubled = actualFight();
    doubled.damage_done[0].total = sim.damage_done[0].total * 9;
    expect(compareSummaries(sim, doubled, 'Thrallgar').executionScore).toBe(2);
  });

  it('says so when the fight has no damage for this character', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Someone Else');
    expect(comparison.headline).toBe(COMPARE_NO_PLAYER);
    expect(comparison.executionScore).toBeNull();
    expect(comparison.lines).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('lists no more than five ability lines and three aura lines', () => {
    const comparison = compareSummaries(sim, actualFight(), 'Thrallgar');
    expect(comparison.lines.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
  });
});
```
